+import { SSEServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/sse.js";
+import express from "express";
+import { AsyncLocalStorage } from "node:async_hooks";
+import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
+import type http from "node:http";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../config/timeouts.js";
+import { createMCPServer } from "../core/mcp-server.js";
+import { createLogger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import { RateLimiter } from "../infrastructure/rate-limiter.js";
+import {
+  extractRequestId,
+  REQUEST_ID_HEADER,
+  RequestContext,
+} from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { maskSensitiveText } from "../shared/log-redaction.js";
+import { ApiConfig, Cache } from "../types/index.js";
+
+const cliHttpLogger = createLogger("cli-http");
+const requestContextStore = new AsyncLocalStorage<RequestContext>();
+
+export const DEFAULT_CLI_HTTP_HOST = "127.0.0.1";
+export const DEFAULT_MAX_SSE_SESSIONS = 100;
+
+const LOOPBACK_HOSTS = new Set(["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1"]);
+const SESSION_ID_REGEX = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/;
+
+export interface TransportSession {
+  transport: SSEServerTransport;
+  createdAt: Date;
+  lastActivity: Date;
+}
+
+export interface CliHttpOptions {
+  apiConfig: ApiConfig;
+  cache: Cache;
+  host?: string;
+  apiKey?: string;
+  allowedOrigins?: string[];
+  allowedHosts?: string[];
+  maxSessions?: number;
+  rateLimitWindowMs?: number;
+  rateLimitMaxRequests?: number;
+  debug?: (...args: unknown[]) => void;
+}
+
+export interface CliHttpContext {
+  app: express.Express;
+  transports: Map<string, TransportSession>;
+  rateLimiter: RateLimiter;
+  closeAll: () => void;
+}
+
+export function parseCommaSeparatedList(raw?: string): string[] {
+  if (!raw) return [];
+  return raw
+    .split(",")
+    .map((entry) => entry.trim())
+    .filter((entry) => entry.length > 0);
+}
+
+export function normalizeHostHeader(hostHeader?: string): string | null {
+  if (!hostHeader || typeof hostHeader !== "string") {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  const trimmed = hostHeader.trim().toLowerCase();
+  if (!trimmed || /[\s/@\\?#]/.test(trimmed)) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  if (trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
+    const match = /^\[([a-f0-9:.]+)\](?::(\d{1,5}))?$/.exec(trimmed);
+    if (!match) {
+      return null;
+    }
+    if (match[2] !== undefined) {
+      const port = Number(match[2]);
+      if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
+        return null;
+      }
+    }
+    return match[1];
+  }
+
+  const firstColon = trimmed.indexOf(":");
+  if (firstColon === -1) {
+    return trimmed;
+  }
+
+  const lastColon = trimmed.lastIndexOf(":");
+  if (firstColon !== lastColon) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  const hostname = trimmed.slice(0, firstColon);
+  const portStr = trimmed.slice(firstColon + 1);
+  if (!hostname || !/^\d{1,5}$/.test(portStr)) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  const port = Number(portStr);
+  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return hostname;
+}
+
+export function isAllowedCliHost(
+  hostHeader: string | undefined,
+  bindHost?: string,
+  extraAllowedHosts?: string[],
+): boolean {
+  const host = normalizeHostHeader(hostHeader);
+  if (!host) {
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  if (LOOPBACK_HOSTS.has(host)) {
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  if (bindHost) {
+    const normalizedBind = normalizeHostHeader(bindHost);
+    if (
+      normalizedBind &&
+      normalizedBind !== "0.0.0.0" &&
+      normalizedBind !== "::" &&
+      host === normalizedBind
+    ) {
+      return true;
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (extraAllowedHosts && extraAllowedHosts.length > 0) {
+    for (const rawAllowed of extraAllowedHosts) {
+      const normalized = normalizeHostHeader(rawAllowed);
+      if (normalized && normalized !== "*" && normalized !== "0.0.0.0" && normalized !== "::") {
+        if (host === normalized) {
+          return true;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  return false;
+}
+
+export function isAllowedCliOrigin(
+  originHeader: string | undefined,
+  allowedOrigins?: string[],
+): { allowed: boolean; allowOriginHeader?: string } {
+  if (originHeader === undefined) {
+    return { allowed: true };
+  }
+
+  if (typeof originHeader !== "string") {
+    return { allowed: false };
+  }
+
+  const trimmed = originHeader.trim();
+  if (!trimmed || trimmed.toLowerCase() === "null" || trimmed === "*") {
+    return { allowed: false };
+  }
+
+  let parsed: URL;
+  try {
+    parsed = new URL(trimmed);
+  } catch {
+    return { allowed: false };
+  }
+
+  if (parsed.protocol !== "http:" && parsed.protocol !== "https:") {
+    return { allowed: false };
+  }
+
+  if (
+    parsed.username ||
+    parsed.password ||
+    parsed.pathname !== "/" ||
+    parsed.search !== "" ||
+    parsed.hash !== "" ||
+    trimmed.endsWith("/")
+  ) {
+    return { allowed: false };
+  }
+
+  const canonicalOrigin = parsed.origin;
+  const nonEmptyAllowed = (allowedOrigins ?? [])
+    .map((entry) => entry.trim())
+    .filter((entry) => entry.length > 0);
+
+  if (nonEmptyAllowed.length > 0) {
+    const exactMatches = nonEmptyAllowed.filter((entry) => entry !== "*");
+    const matched = exactMatches.some(
+      (entry) => entry.toLowerCase() === canonicalOrigin.toLowerCase(),
+    );
+    return matched ? { allowed: true, allowOriginHeader: canonicalOrigin } : { allowed: false };
+  }
+
+  const hostname = parsed.hostname.toLowerCase();
+  if (hostname === "localhost" || hostname === "127.0.0.1" || hostname === "[::1]") {
+    return { allowed: true, allowOriginHeader: canonicalOrigin };
+  }
+
+  return { allowed: false };
+}
+
+export function isValidSessionId(value: unknown): value is string {
+  return typeof value === "string" && SESSION_ID_REGEX.test(value);
+}
+
+export function isLoopbackHost(host: string): boolean {
+  if (typeof host !== "string") {
+    return false;
+  }
+  const normalized = host.trim().toLowerCase();
+  return (
+    normalized === "127.0.0.1" ||
+    normalized === "localhost" ||
+    normalized === "::1" ||
+    normalized === "[::1]"
+  );
+}
+
+export function isValidApiKey(provided: string, expected: string): boolean {
+  if (typeof provided !== "string" || typeof expected !== "string" || !provided || !expected) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  const providedDigest = createHash("sha256").update(provided, "utf8").digest();
+  const expectedDigest = createHash("sha256").update(expected, "utf8").digest();
+  return timingSafeEqual(providedDigest, expectedDigest);
+}
+
+export function createCliHttpApp(options: CliHttpOptions): CliHttpContext {
+  const bindHost = options.host ?? DEFAULT_CLI_HTTP_HOST;
+  const apiKey =
+    typeof options.apiKey === "string" && options.apiKey.trim().length > 0
+      ? options.apiKey.trim()
+      : undefined;
+
+  if (!isLoopbackHost(bindHost) && !apiKey) {
+    throw new Error(
+      `Refusing to start HTTP/SSE server on non-loopback host "${bindHost}" without MCP_API_KEY`,
+    );
+  }
+
+  const debug = options.debug ?? (() => {});
+  const maxSessions =
+    options.maxSessions !== undefined && Number.isFinite(options.maxSessions) && options.maxSessions > 0
+      ? Math.floor(options.maxSessions)
+      : DEFAULT_MAX_SSE_SESSIONS;
+
+  const app = express();
+  app.disable("x-powered-by");
+
+  app.use((req, res, next) => {
+    const requestContext = new RequestContext(extractRequestId(req.headers));
+    res.locals.requestContext = requestContext;
+    res.setHeader(REQUEST_ID_HEADER, requestContext.requestId);
+    next();
+  });
+
+  app.use((_req, res, next) => {
+    res.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
+    res.setHeader("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
+    res.setHeader("Content-Security-Policy", "default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'");
+    res.setHeader("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
+    next();
+  });
+
+  app.use((req, res, next) => {
+    if (!isAllowedCliHost(req.headers.host, options.host, options.allowedHosts)) {
+      res.status(403).json({
+        error: "Forbidden",
+        message: "Invalid or disallowed Host header",
+      });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const rawOrigin = req.headers.origin;
+    if (Array.isArray(rawOrigin)) {
+      res.status(403).json({
+        error: "Forbidden",
+        message: "Invalid Origin header",
+      });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const originCheck = isAllowedCliOrigin(rawOrigin, options.allowedOrigins);
+    if (!originCheck.allowed) {
+      res.status(403).json({
+        error: "Forbidden",
+        message: "Origin not allowed",
+      });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (originCheck.allowOriginHeader) {
+      res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", originCheck.allowOriginHeader);
+      res.setHeader("Vary", "Origin");
+      res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS");
+      res.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type, Authorization, X-API-Key");
+    }
+
+    if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
+      res.sendStatus(204);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    next();
+  });
+
+  if (apiKey) {
+    app.use((req, res, next) => {
+      const rawAuth = req.headers.authorization;
+      const rawApiKey = req.headers["x-api-key"];
+
+      let token: string | undefined;
+      if (!Array.isArray(rawAuth) && !Array.isArray(rawApiKey)) {
+        const trimmedAuth = typeof rawAuth === "string" ? rawAuth.trim() : "";
+        const bearerMatch = trimmedAuth ? /^bearer\s+(.+)$/i.exec(trimmedAuth) : null;
+        if (bearerMatch) {
+          const candidate = bearerMatch[1].trim();
+          if (candidate.length > 0) {
+            token = candidate;
+          }
+        } else if (typeof rawApiKey === "string" && rawApiKey.trim().length > 0) {
+          token = rawApiKey.trim();
+        }
+      }
+
+      if (!token || !isValidApiKey(token, apiKey)) {
+        const requestContext = res.locals.requestContext as RequestContext | undefined;
+        cliHttpLogger.warn(
+          "auth.unauthorized",
+          { path: req.path },
+          requestContext?.requestId,
+        );
+        res.setHeader("WWW-Authenticate", "Bearer");
+        res.status(401).json({
+          error: "Unauthorized",
+          message: "Valid API key required",
+        });
+        return;
+      }
+
+      next();
+    });
+  }
+
+  app.use(express.json({ limit: "1mb" }));
+  app.use(
+    (
+      err: unknown,
+      _req: express.Request,
+      res: express.Response,
+      next: express.NextFunction,
+    ) => {
+      const errorObj = err as { type?: string } | undefined;
+      if (errorObj?.type === "entity.too.large") {
+        res.status(413).json({
+          error: "Payload Too Large",
+          message: "Request body exceeds 1MB limit",
+        });
+        return;
+      }
+      if (err instanceof SyntaxError || errorObj?.type === "entity.parse.failed") {
+        res.status(400).json({
+          error: "Bad Request",
+          message: "Invalid JSON payload",
+        });
+        return;
+      }
+      next(err);
+    },
+  );
+
+  const rateLimiter = new RateLimiter({
+    windowMs: options.rateLimitWindowMs ?? 60 * 1000,
+    maxRequests: options.rateLimitMaxRequests ?? 30,
+  });
+
+  const rateLimitMiddleware = (
+    req: express.Request,
+    res: express.Response,
+    next: express.NextFunction,
+  ) => {
+    const clientId = req.ip || req.socket.remoteAddress || "unknown";
+
+    if (!rateLimiter.checkLimit(clientId)) {
+      const requestContext = res.locals.requestContext as RequestContext | undefined;
+      const resetTime = rateLimiter.getResetTime(clientId);
+      const resetSeconds = Math.ceil(resetTime / 1000);
+      const logClientId = process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production" ? clientId : "[masked]";
+      cliHttpLogger.warn(
+        "http.rate_limited",
+        { clientId: logClientId, path: req.path, retryAfter: resetSeconds },
+        requestContext?.requestId,
+      );
+
+      res.status(429).json({
+        error: "Rate limit exceeded",
+        message: `Too many requests. Please try again in ${resetSeconds} seconds.`,
+        retryAfter: resetSeconds,
+      });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const remaining = rateLimiter.getRemaining(clientId);
+    res.setHeader("X-RateLimit-Limit", String(options.rateLimitMaxRequests ?? 30));
+    res.setHeader("X-RateLimit-Remaining", remaining.toString());
+
+    next();
+  };
+
+  const transports = new Map<string, TransportSession>();
+
+  const connectionMonitor = setInterval(() => {
+    const now = Date.now();
+    const timeout = TIMEOUTS.SSE_CONNECTION_MS;
+
+    for (const [sessionId, session] of transports.entries()) {
+      const age = now - session.lastActivity.getTime();
+      if (age > timeout) {
+        debug(`Cleaning up stale SSE session ${sessionId} (age: ${age}ms)`);
+        session.transport.close?.();
+        transports.delete(sessionId);
+      }
+    }
+
+    if (transports.size > 0) {
+      debug(`Active SSE connections: ${transports.size}`);
+    }
+  }, TIMEOUTS.PING_INTERVAL_MS);
+  connectionMonitor.unref?.();
+
+  let closed = false;
+  const closeAll = () => {
+    if (closed) {
+      return;
+    }
+    closed = true;
+    clearInterval(connectionMonitor);
+    rateLimiter.dispose();
+    for (const session of transports.values()) {
+      try {
+        session.transport.close?.();
+      } catch {
+        // Ignore individual session close errors during drain
+      }
+    }
+    transports.clear();
+  };
+
+  app.get("/mcp", rateLimitMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
+    const requestContext =
+      (res.locals.requestContext as RequestContext | undefined) ??
+      new RequestContext(extractRequestId(req.headers));
+    if (!res.headersSent) {
+      res.setHeader(REQUEST_ID_HEADER, requestContext.requestId);
+    }
+    debug("GET /mcp - Establishing SSE stream");
+    if (transports.size >= maxSessions) {
+      cliHttpLogger.warn(
+        "sse.max_sessions_reached",
+        { activeSessions: transports.size, maxSessions },
+        requestContext.requestId,
+      );
+      res.status(503).json({
+        error: "Service Unavailable",
+        message: "Maximum active SSE sessions reached",
+      });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      const transport = new SSEServerTransport("/messages", res);
+      const sessionId = transport.sessionId;
+      const now = new Date();
+      transports.set(sessionId, {
+        transport,
+        createdAt: now,
+        lastActivity: now,
+      });
+      debug(`Created SSE transport with session ID: ${sessionId} (total sessions: ${transports.size})`);
+      cliHttpLogger.info(
+        "sse.session_connected",
+        { activeSessions: transports.size },
+        requestContext.requestId,
+      );
+
+      transport.onclose = () => {
+        debug(`SSE transport closed for session ${sessionId}`);
+        transports.delete(sessionId);
+        cliHttpLogger.info(
+          "sse.session_closed",
+          { activeSessions: transports.size },
+          requestContext.requestId,
+        );
+      };
+
+      const server = createMCPServer({
+        apiConfig: options.apiConfig,
+        cache: options.cache,
+        getRequestContext: () => requestContextStore.getStore(),
+      });
+      await server.connect(transport);
+      debug(`MCP server connected to SSE transport ${sessionId}`);
+    } catch (error) {
+      debug("Error establishing SSE stream:", error);
+      cliHttpLogger.error(
+        "sse.connection_error",
+        { error: maskSensitiveText(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)) },
+        requestContext.requestId,
+      );
+      if (!res.headersSent) {
+        res.status(500).send("Error establishing SSE stream");
+      }
+    }
+  });
+
+  app.post("/messages", rateLimitMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
+    const requestContext =
+      (res.locals.requestContext as RequestContext | undefined) ??
+      new RequestContext(extractRequestId(req.headers));
+    if (!res.headersSent) {
+      res.setHeader(REQUEST_ID_HEADER, requestContext.requestId);
+    }
+    debug("POST /messages");
+    try {
+      const sessionId = req.query.sessionId;
+      if (!isValidSessionId(sessionId)) {
+        debug("Invalid or missing sessionId parameter in request");
+        cliHttpLogger.warn(
+          "http.invalid_session_id",
+          { path: "/messages" },
+          requestContext.requestId,
+        );
+        res.status(400).json({
+          error: "Bad Request",
+          message: "Invalid or missing sessionId parameter",
+        });
+        return;
+      }
+
+      const session = transports.get(sessionId);
+      if (!session) {
+        debug(`No transport found for session: ${sessionId}`);
+        cliHttpLogger.warn(
+          "http.session_not_found",
+          { path: "/messages" },
+          requestContext.requestId,
+        );
+        res.status(404).json({
+          error: "Not Found",
+          message: "Session not found",
+        });
+        return;
+      }
+
+      session.lastActivity = new Date();
+      await requestContextStore.run(requestContext, () =>
+        session.transport.handlePostMessage(req, res, req.body),
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      debug("Error handling message:", error);
+      cliHttpLogger.error(
+        "http.message_error",
+        { error: maskSensitiveText(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)) },
+        requestContext.requestId,
+      );
+      if (!res.headersSent) {
+        res.status(500).send("Error handling request");
+      }
+    }
+  });
+
+  return {
+    app,
+    transports,
+    rateLimiter,
+    closeAll,
+  };
+}
+
+export interface GracefulShutdownOptions {
+  server?: Pick<http.Server, "listening" | "close"> & {
+    closeIdleConnections?: () => void;
+  };
+  httpContext?: Pick<CliHttpContext, "closeAll">;
+  mcpServer?: { close?: () => Promise<void> | void };
+  cache?: { clear?: () => Promise<void> | void };
+  timeoutMs?: number;
+  logger?: (...args: unknown[]) => void;
+  onExit?: (code: number) => void;
+}
+
+export function createGracefulShutdown(
+  options: GracefulShutdownOptions,
+): (signal?: string) => Promise<void> {
+  const timeoutMs =
+    options.timeoutMs !== undefined && Number.isFinite(options.timeoutMs) && options.timeoutMs > 0
+      ? options.timeoutMs
+      : 5000;
+  const logger = options.logger ?? (() => {});
+
+  let shutdownPromise: Promise<void> | undefined;
+  let exited = false;
+
+  const finishExit = (code: number) => {
+    if (exited) {
+      return;
+    }
+    exited = true;
+    options.onExit?.(code);
+  };
+
+  return (signal?: string) => {
+    if (shutdownPromise) {
+      return shutdownPromise;
+    }
+
+    shutdownPromise = (async () => {
+      logger(`Shutting down gracefully${signal ? ` (${signal})` : ""}...`);
+
+      let forceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+      let timedOut = false;
+
+      const drainPromise = (async () => {
+        try {
+          options.httpContext?.closeAll();
+        } catch {
+          // Best-effort SSE session cleanup
+        }
+
+        if (options.server?.listening) {
+          await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
+            try {
+              options.server!.close(() => resolve());
+              options.server!.closeIdleConnections?.();
+            } catch {
+              resolve();
+            }
+          });
+        }
+
+        try {
+          await options.mcpServer?.close?.();
+        } catch {
+          // Best-effort MCP server close
+        }
+
+        try {
+          await options.cache?.clear?.();
+        } catch {
+          // Best-effort cache clear
+        }
+      })();
+
+      const timeoutPromise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
+        forceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+          timedOut = true;
+          resolve();
+        }, timeoutMs);
+        forceTimer.unref?.();
+      });
+
+      try {
+        await Promise.race([drainPromise, timeoutPromise]);
+      } finally {
+        if (forceTimer !== undefined) {
+          clearTimeout(forceTimer);
+          forceTimer = undefined;
+        }
+      }
+
+      finishExit(timedOut ? 1 : 0);
+    })();
+
+    return shutdownPromise;
+  };
+}

+#!/usr/bin/env node
+import { StdioServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js";
+import type http from "node:http";
+import { createMCPServer } from "../core/mcp-server.js";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../core/cache.js";
+import {
+  loadServerConfiguration,
+  resolveApiConfig,
+  SERVER_NAME,
+  SERVER_VERSION,
+} from "../config/index.js";
+import {
+  createCliHttpApp,
+  createGracefulShutdown,
+  type CliHttpContext,
+  DEFAULT_CLI_HTTP_HOST,
+  DEFAULT_MAX_SSE_SESSIONS,
+  isLoopbackHost,
+  parseCommaSeparatedList,
+} from "./cli-http.js";
+
+const DEBUG = process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production";
+function debug(...args: unknown[]) {
+  if (DEBUG) {
+    console.error("[DEBUG]", ...args);
+  }
+}
+
+const runtimeState: {
+  httpServer?: http.Server;
+  httpContext?: CliHttpContext;
+  mcpServer?: ReturnType<typeof createMCPServer>;
+  cache?: MemoryCache;
+} = {};
+
+let shutdownRunner: ((signal?: string) => Promise<void>) | undefined;
+const handleShutdownSignal = (signal: string) => {
+  if (!shutdownRunner) {
+    shutdownRunner = createGracefulShutdown({
+      server: runtimeState.httpServer,
+      httpContext: runtimeState.httpContext,
+      mcpServer: runtimeState.mcpServer,
+      cache: runtimeState.cache,
+      logger: debug,
+      onExit: (code) => process.exit(code),
+    });
+  }
+  void shutdownRunner(signal);
+};
+
+process.on("SIGINT", () => handleShutdownSignal("SIGINT"));
+process.on("SIGTERM", () => handleShutdownSignal("SIGTERM"));
+
+async function main() {
+  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
+  if (args.includes("--version") || args.includes("-v")) {
+    console.log(`${SERVER_NAME} v${SERVER_VERSION}`);
+    process.exit(0);
+  }
+  if (args.includes("--help") || args.includes("-h")) {
+    console.log(`${SERVER_NAME} v${SERVER_VERSION} - Model Context Protocol server for Brazilian TSE electoral data
+
+Usage:
+  mcp-tse [options]
+  npx @aredes.me/mcp-tse [options]
+
+Options:
+  -h, --help     Show this help message
+  -v, --version  Show version number
+
+Environment Variables:
+  MCP_TRANSPORT            Transport mode: stdio (default) or http
+  MCP_HTTP_PORT            HTTP server port (default: 3000)
+  MCP_HTTP_HOST            HTTP bind host (default: 127.0.0.1)
+  MCP_API_KEY              API key for HTTP/SSE auth (required outside loopback)
+  MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS      Comma-separated allowed Origin URLs for CORS
+  MCP_ALLOWED_HOSTS        Comma-separated allowed Host headers
+  MCP_MAX_SSE_SESSIONS     Maximum concurrent SSE sessions (default: 100)
+  TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL  Custom TSE Resultados EA20 base URL (allowlisted)
+  TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL        Custom TSE Dados Abertos CKAN base URL (allowlisted)
+  API_KEY_HEADER           Optional upstream authentication header name
+  API_KEY_VALUE            Optional upstream authentication header value
+
+Examples:
+  mcp-tse                                    # Start with stdio transport
+  MCP_TRANSPORT=http mcp-tse                 # Start with HTTP transport (127.0.0.1:3000)
+
+For more information, visit: https://github.com/cristianoaredes/mcp-tse`);
+    process.exit(0);
+  }
+
+  const serverConfig = loadServerConfiguration();
+  const apiConfig = resolveApiConfig();
+
+  console.error(`${SERVER_NAME} v${SERVER_VERSION} configuration:`);
+  console.error(`  TSE Resultados API: ${apiConfig.resultadosBaseUrl}`);
+  console.error(`  TSE CKAN API: ${apiConfig.ckanBaseUrl}`);
+  if (apiConfig.authHeaders) {
+    console.error(`  Auth headers: ${Object.keys(apiConfig.authHeaders).join(", ")}`);
+  }
+
+  const cache = new MemoryCache(serverConfig.cacheSize, serverConfig.cacheTTL);
+  runtimeState.cache = cache;
+
+  const mcpServer = createMCPServer({ apiConfig, cache });
+  runtimeState.mcpServer = mcpServer;
+
+  const transportMode = serverConfig.transport;
+  if (transportMode === "http") {
+    const host = serverConfig.httpHost || DEFAULT_CLI_HTTP_HOST;
+    const apiKey = process.env.MCP_API_KEY?.trim() || undefined;
+    if (!isLoopbackHost(host) && !apiKey) {
+      console.error(
+        `[Security] Refusing to bind HTTP/SSE transport on non-loopback host "${host}" without MCP_API_KEY configured.`,
+      );
+      process.exit(1);
+    }
+    if (host === "0.0.0.0" || host === "::") {
+      console.error(
+        `[Security] WARNING: HTTP+SSE transport bound to wildcard interface (${host}). ` +
+          "Prefer 127.0.0.1 and configure MCP_ALLOWED_HOSTS / MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS when exposing externally.",
+      );
+    }
+
+    const parsedMaxSessions = Number.parseInt(process.env.MCP_MAX_SSE_SESSIONS || "", 10);
+    const maxSessions =
+      Number.isFinite(parsedMaxSessions) && parsedMaxSessions > 0
+        ? parsedMaxSessions
+        : DEFAULT_MAX_SSE_SESSIONS;
+
+    const httpContext = createCliHttpApp({
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+      host,
+      apiKey,
+      allowedOrigins: parseCommaSeparatedList(process.env.MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS),
+      allowedHosts: parseCommaSeparatedList(process.env.MCP_ALLOWED_HOSTS),
+      maxSessions,
+      debug,
+    });
+    runtimeState.httpContext = httpContext;
+
+    const port = serverConfig.httpPort;
+    runtimeState.httpServer = httpContext.app.listen(port, host, () => {
+      console.error(
+        `${SERVER_NAME} v${SERVER_VERSION} started on HTTP+SSE transport (port ${port}) [${host}]`,
+      );
+      console.error(
+        `  HTTP+SSE auth: ${apiKey ? "enabled (MCP_API_KEY)" : "disabled (loopback-only)"}`,
+      );
+    });
+  } else {
+    const transport = new StdioServerTransport();
+    await mcpServer.connect(transport);
+    console.error(`${SERVER_NAME} v${SERVER_VERSION} started on stdio transport`);
+  }
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => {
+  console.error("Server failed to start:", error);
+  process.exit(1);
+});

+import { KVCache } from "../core/cache.js";
+import { resolveApiConfig, SERVER_NAME, SERVER_VERSION } from "../config/index.js";
+import { TOOL_DEFINITIONS, executeTool } from "../core/tools.js";
+import { ServerConfig, MCPRequest, MCPResponse } from "../types/index.js";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../config/timeouts.js";
+import { CACHE } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+import { createLogger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import { RequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { maskSensitiveText } from "../shared/log-redaction.js";
+import {
+  resolveMaxToolOutputChars,
+  serializeBoundedToolOutput,
+} from "../shared/agentic-guard.js";
+
+const cloudflareLogger = createLogger("cloudflare");
+const DEFAULT_HEALTH_KV_TIMEOUT_MS = 1000;
+
+declare global {
+  interface KVNamespace {
+    get(key: string): Promise<string | null>;
+    put(
+      key: string,
+      value: string,
+      options?: { expiration?: number; expirationTtl?: number; metadata?: unknown },
+    ): Promise<void>;
+    delete(key: string): Promise<void>;
+  }
+}
+
+export interface Env {
+  MCP_TRANSPORT?: string;
+  MCP_HTTP_PORT?: string;
+  MCP_CACHE_SIZE?: string;
+  MCP_CACHE_TTL?: string;
+  MCP_CACHE?: KVNamespace;
+  MCP_API_KEY?: string;
+  MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS?: string;
+  MCP_DISABLE_RATE_LIMIT?: string;
+  MCP_TRUST_X_FORWARDED_FOR?: string;
+  MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS?: string;
+  MCP_DPO_CONTACT?: string;
+  MCP_KV?: KVNamespace;
+  TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL?: string;
+  TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL?: string;
+}
+
+const WORKER_ENV_KEYS = [
+  "TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL",
+  "TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL",
+] as const;
+
+/**
+ * Propagate allowlisted Worker URL overrides into process.env for tool execution.
+ */
+export function syncWorkerEnv(env?: Env): void {
+  if (!env || typeof process === "undefined" || !process.env) return;
+  for (const key of WORKER_ENV_KEYS) {
+    const value = env[key];
+    if (typeof value === "string" && value.length > 0) {
+      process.env[key] = value;
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+const BASE_CORS_HEADERS: Record<string, string> = {
+  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, OPTIONS",
+  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, Accept, Authorization, X-API-Key",
+  "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400",
+  "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
+  "X-Frame-Options": "DENY",
+  "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer",
+  "Cache-Control": "no-store",
+};
+
+const corsHeaders: Record<string, string> = { ...BASE_CORS_HEADERS };
+
+function parseAllowedOrigins(raw?: string): string[] {
+  if (typeof raw !== "string" || !raw.trim()) return [];
+  const origins: string[] = [];
+  for (const part of raw.split(",")) {
+    const trimmed = part.trim();
+    if (!trimmed || trimmed === "*") continue;
+    try {
+      const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
+      if (parsed.protocol === "https:" || parsed.protocol === "http:") {
+        origins.push(parsed.origin);
+      }
+    } catch {
+      // Ignore malformed origin entries
+    }
+  }
+  return origins;
+}
+
+function getCorsHeaders(
+  request: Request,
+  env?: Env,
+  options?: { isPublic?: boolean },
+): Record<string, string> {
+  const hasApiKey = typeof env?.MCP_API_KEY === "string" && env.MCP_API_KEY.length > 0;
+  const allowedOrigins = parseAllowedOrigins(env?.MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS);
+  const origin = request.headers.get("Origin");
+
+  if (allowedOrigins.length > 0) {
+    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
+      ...BASE_CORS_HEADERS,
+      Vary: "Origin",
+    };
+    if (origin && allowedOrigins.includes(origin)) {
+      headers["Access-Control-Allow-Origin"] = origin;
+    }
+    return headers;
+  }
+
+  if (hasApiKey && !options?.isPublic) {
+    return {
+      ...BASE_CORS_HEADERS,
+      Vary: "Origin",
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ...BASE_CORS_HEADERS,
+    "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
+  };
+}
+
+function handleCORS(
+  request: Request,
+  env?: Env,
+  options?: { isPublic?: boolean },
+): Response | null {
+  if (request.method !== "OPTIONS") return null;
+  const headers = getCorsHeaders(request, env, options);
+  if (request.headers.get("Origin") && !("Access-Control-Allow-Origin" in headers)) {
+    return new Response("Origin not allowed", {
+      status: 403,
+      headers,
+    });
+  }
+  return new Response(null, {
+    status: 204,
+    headers,
+  });
+}
+
+function withCorsHeaders(
+  response: Response,
+  request: Request,
+  env?: Env,
+  options?: { isPublic?: boolean },
+): Response {
+  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
+  headers.delete("Access-Control-Allow-Origin");
+  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(getCorsHeaders(request, env, options))) {
+    headers.set(key, value);
+  }
+  return new Response(response.body, {
+    status: response.status,
+    statusText: response.statusText,
+    headers,
+  });
+}
+
+async function handleHealthCheck(
+  request?: Request,
+  env?: Env,
+  options?: { kvTimeoutMs?: number; requestContext?: RequestContext },
+): Promise<Response> {
+  const kv = env?.MCP_CACHE ?? env?.MCP_KV;
+  let overallStatus: "healthy" | "degraded" = "healthy";
+  let httpStatus = 200;
+  let kvCheck:
+    | { status: "unconfigured" }
+    | { status: "ok"; latencyMs: number }
+    | { status: "degraded"; latencyMs: number; error: string } = {
+    status: "unconfigured",
+  };
+
+  if (kv) {
+    const kvTimeoutMs =
+      options?.kvTimeoutMs !== undefined &&
+      Number.isFinite(options.kvTimeoutMs) &&
+      options.kvTimeoutMs > 0
+        ? options.kvTimeoutMs
+        : DEFAULT_HEALTH_KV_TIMEOUT_MS;
+    const probeStart = Date.now();
+    let timeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+    try {
+      const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_resolve, reject) => {
+        timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
+          reject(new Error(`KV health probe timed out after ${kvTimeoutMs}ms`));
+        }, kvTimeoutMs);
+        (timeoutId as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
+      });
+      await Promise.race([kv.get("__health_check__"), timeoutPromise]);
+      kvCheck = {
+        status: "ok",
+        latencyMs: Date.now() - probeStart,
+      };
+    } catch (error) {
+      const latencyMs = Date.now() - probeStart;
+      overallStatus = "degraded";
+      httpStatus = 503;
+      kvCheck = {
+        status: "degraded",
+        latencyMs,
+        error: "kv_unavailable",
+      };
+      cloudflareLogger.warn(
+        "health.kv_degraded",
+        {
+          latencyMs,
+          reason: maskSensitiveText(
+            error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+          ),
+        },
+        options?.requestContext?.requestId,
+      );
+    } finally {
+      if (timeoutId !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  const healthData = {
+    status: overallStatus,
+    service: SERVER_NAME,
+    version: SERVER_VERSION,
+    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
+    runtime: "cloudflare-workers",
+    checks: {
+      kv: kvCheck,
+    },
+  };
+  const headers = request
+    ? getCorsHeaders(request, env, { isPublic: true })
+    : corsHeaders;
+
+  return new Response(JSON.stringify(healthData), {
+    status: httpStatus,
+    headers: {
+      "Content-Type": "application/json",
+      ...headers,
+    },
+  });
+}
+
+export async function handleMCPRequest(
+  request: MCPRequest,
+  env: Env,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext,
+): Promise<MCPResponse> {
+  syncWorkerEnv(env);
+  const activeContext = requestContext ?? new RequestContext();
+
+  const serverConfig: ServerConfig = {
+    transport: env.MCP_TRANSPORT || "http",
+    httpPort: parseInt(env.MCP_HTTP_PORT || "8787", 10),
+    cacheSize: parseInt(env.MCP_CACHE_SIZE || String(CACHE.DEFAULT_SIZE), 10),
+    cacheTTL: parseInt(env.MCP_CACHE_TTL || String(CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS), 10),
+    apiTimeout: TIMEOUTS.HTTP_REQUEST_MS,
+  };
+
+  const apiConfig = resolveApiConfig({
+    ...(env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL ? { resultadosBaseUrl: env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL } : {}),
+    ...(env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL ? { ckanBaseUrl: env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL } : {}),
+  });
+  const cache = env.MCP_CACHE ? new KVCache(env.MCP_CACHE, serverConfig.cacheTTL) : undefined;
+
+  try {
+    switch (request.method) {
+      case "tools/list":
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id: request.id,
+          result: {
+            tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
+          },
+        };
+
+      case "tools/call": {
+        const params = request.params as { name: string; arguments: unknown };
+        const { name, arguments: args } = params;
+
+        try {
+          const result = await executeTool(name, args, apiConfig, cache, {
+            requestId: activeContext.requestId,
+            requestContext: activeContext,
+          });
+
+          if (result.ok) {
+            const maxOutputChars = env?.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS
+              ? resolveMaxToolOutputChars(env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS)
+              : undefined;
+            return {
+              jsonrpc: "2.0",
+              id: request.id,
+              result: {
+                content: [
+                  {
+                    type: "text",
+                    text: serializeBoundedToolOutput(result.data, maxOutputChars),
+                  },
+                ],
+              },
+            };
+          }
+          return {
+            jsonrpc: "2.0",
+            id: request.id,
+            error: {
+              code: -32603,
+              message: "Tool execution failed",
+              data: result.error,
+            },
+          };
+        } catch (error: unknown) {
+          const err = error as Error;
+          return {
+            jsonrpc: "2.0",
+            id: request.id,
+            error: {
+              code: -32602,
+              message: "Invalid parameters",
+              data: err.message,
+            },
+          };
+        }
+      }
+
+      default:
+        return {
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id: request.id,
+          error: {
+            code: -32601,
+            message: "Method not found",
+            data: `Unknown method: ${request.method}`,
+          },
+        };
+    }
+  } catch (error) {
+    return {
+      jsonrpc: "2.0",
+      id: request.id,
+      error: {
+        code: -32603,
+        message: "Internal error",
+        data: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error",
+      },
+    };
+  }
+}
+
+export async function handleMCPEndpoint(
+  request: Request,
+  env: Env,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext,
+): Promise<Response> {
+  syncWorkerEnv(env);
+  const activeContext = requestContext ?? new RequestContext();
+
+  try {
+    const body = await request.text();
+    let mcpRequest: MCPRequest;
+
+    try {
+      mcpRequest = JSON.parse(body) as MCPRequest;
+    } catch {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          jsonrpc: "2.0",
+          id: null,
+          error: {
+            code: -32700,
+            message: "Parse error",
+            data: "Invalid JSON in request body",
+          },
+        }),
+        {
+          status: 400,
+          headers: {
+            "Content-Type": "application/json",
+            ...corsHeaders,
+          },
+        },
+      );
+    }
+
+    const response = await handleMCPRequest(mcpRequest, env, activeContext);
+
+    return new Response(JSON.stringify(response), {
+      status: 200,
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        ...corsHeaders,
+      },
+    });
+  } catch (error) {
+    cloudflareLogger.error(
+      "mcp.endpoint_error",
+      { error: maskSensitiveText(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)) },
+      activeContext.requestId,
+    );
+
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: null,
+        error: {
+          code: -32603,
+          message: "Internal error",
+          data: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error",
+        },
+      }),
+      {
+        status: 500,
+        headers: {
+          "Content-Type": "application/json",
+          ...corsHeaders,
+        },
+      },
+    );
+  }
+}
+
+export {
+  corsHeaders,
+  getCorsHeaders,
+  parseAllowedOrigins,
+  withCorsHeaders,
+  handleCORS,
+  handleHealthCheck,
+};

+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { resolve } from "node:path";
+import { ApiConfig, ServerConfig, ConfigFile } from "../types/index.js";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "./timeouts.js";
+import { API_URLS, CACHE, SERVER } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+
+// Default API URLs
+export const DEFAULT_TSE_RESULTADOS_URL = API_URLS.DEFAULT_TSE_RESULTADOS;
+export const DEFAULT_TSE_CKAN_URL = API_URLS.DEFAULT_TSE_CKAN;
+
+// Constants
+export const SERVER_NAME = SERVER.NAME;
+export const SERVER_VERSION = SERVER.VERSION;
+
+// Allowed API hosts (SSRF protection)
+export const ALLOWED_TSE_RESULTADOS_HOSTS: readonly string[] = [
+  "resultados.tse.jus.br",
+  "cdn.tse.jus.br",
+];
+
+export const ALLOWED_TSE_CKAN_HOSTS: readonly string[] = [
+  "dadosabertos.tse.jus.br",
+  "cdn.tse.jus.br",
+];
+
+const DEV_LOCALHOST_HOSTS = new Set(["localhost", "127.0.0.1"]);
+
+function isLocalhostAllowed(): boolean {
+  const nodeEnv = typeof process !== "undefined" ? process.env?.NODE_ENV : undefined;
+  return nodeEnv === "development" || nodeEnv === "test";
+}
+
+function sanitizeUrlForLog(url: string): string {
+  try {
+    const parsed = new URL(url);
+    return `${parsed.protocol}//${parsed.host}${parsed.pathname}`;
+  } catch {
+    return "[invalid-url]";
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Validates URL for SSRF protection.
+ * @param url - URL to validate
+ * @param allowedHosts - List of allowed hostnames
+ * @returns true if URL is valid and host is allowed
+ */
+export function validateUrl(url: string, allowedHosts: readonly string[]): boolean {
+  if (typeof url !== "string" || !url.trim()) {
+    console.error("[Security] Rejected empty or non-string URL");
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const parsed = new URL(url);
+
+    // Reject URLs with embedded credentials to avoid credential confusion and secret leakage
+    if (parsed.username || parsed.password) {
+      console.error(
+        `[Security] Rejected URL with embedded credentials: ${parsed.protocol}//${parsed.host}`,
+      );
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    // Loopback is only permitted in explicit development/test environments over HTTP(S)
+    if (DEV_LOCALHOST_HOSTS.has(parsed.hostname)) {
+      if (!isLocalhostAllowed()) {
+        console.error(
+          `[Security] Rejected loopback URL outside development/test: ${parsed.hostname}`,
+        );
+        return false;
+      }
+      if (parsed.protocol !== "http:" && parsed.protocol !== "https:") {
+        console.error(
+          `[Security] Rejected non-HTTP(S) loopback URL: ${parsed.protocol}//${parsed.host}`,
+        );
+        return false;
+      }
+      return true;
+    }
+
+    // Only HTTPS allowed for non-loopback hosts
+    if (parsed.protocol !== "https:") {
+      console.error(`[Security] Rejected non-HTTPS URL: ${sanitizeUrlForLog(url)}`);
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    // Check if host is in allowlist (exact match or subdomain of allowlisted host)
+    const isAllowed = allowedHosts.some((allowed) => {
+      return parsed.hostname === allowed || parsed.hostname.endsWith(`.${allowed}`);
+    });
+
+    if (!isAllowed) {
+      console.error(`[Security] Rejected URL with non-allowed host: ${parsed.hostname}`);
+      console.error(`[Security] Allowed hosts: ${allowedHosts.join(", ")}`);
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+  } catch {
+    console.error(`[Security] Invalid URL format: ${sanitizeUrlForLog(url)}`);
+    return false;
+  }
+}
+
+export function isAllowedTseResultadosUrl(url: string): boolean {
+  return validateUrl(url, ALLOWED_TSE_RESULTADOS_HOSTS);
+}
+
+export function isAllowedTseCkanUrl(url: string): boolean {
+  return validateUrl(url, ALLOWED_TSE_CKAN_HOSTS);
+}
+
+function normalizeBaseUrl(url: string): string {
+  return url.endsWith("/") ? url : url + "/";
+}
+
+function validateAndNormalizeApiConfig(config: ApiConfig): ApiConfig {
+  if (!validateUrl(config.resultadosBaseUrl, ALLOWED_TSE_RESULTADOS_HOSTS)) {
+    throw new Error(
+      `Invalid or disallowed TSE Resultados API URL: ${sanitizeUrlForLog(config.resultadosBaseUrl)}. ` +
+        `Allowed hosts: ${ALLOWED_TSE_RESULTADOS_HOSTS.join(", ")}`,
+    );
+  }
+  if (!validateUrl(config.ckanBaseUrl, ALLOWED_TSE_CKAN_HOSTS)) {
+    throw new Error(
+      `Invalid or disallowed TSE CKAN API URL: ${sanitizeUrlForLog(config.ckanBaseUrl)}. ` +
+        `Allowed hosts: ${ALLOWED_TSE_CKAN_HOSTS.join(", ")}`,
+    );
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ...config,
+    resultadosBaseUrl: normalizeBaseUrl(config.resultadosBaseUrl),
+    ckanBaseUrl: normalizeBaseUrl(config.ckanBaseUrl),
+  };
+}
+
+// Configuration loading with hierarchy: env vars > config file > defaults
+function loadApiConfiguration(): ApiConfig {
+  const config: ApiConfig = {
+    resultadosBaseUrl: DEFAULT_TSE_RESULTADOS_URL,
+    ckanBaseUrl: DEFAULT_TSE_CKAN_URL,
+  };
+
+  // Try to load from config file first (available in Node.js CLI)
+  try {
+    const cwd = typeof process !== "undefined" && typeof process.cwd === "function"
+      ? process.cwd()
+      : ".";
+    const configPath = resolve(cwd, ".mcprc.json");
+    const configFile = JSON.parse(readFileSync(configPath, "utf-8")) as ConfigFile;
+
+    if (typeof configFile?.apiUrls?.resultados === "string") {
+      config.resultadosBaseUrl = configFile.apiUrls.resultados;
+    }
+    if (typeof configFile?.apiUrls?.ckan === "string") {
+      config.ckanBaseUrl = configFile.apiUrls.ckan;
+    }
+    if (configFile?.auth?.headers && typeof configFile.auth.headers === "object") {
+      config.authHeaders = configFile.auth.headers;
+    }
+  } catch {
+    // Config file doesn't exist or is invalid, continue with defaults
+  }
+
+  // Environment variables override config file
+  const env = typeof process !== "undefined" ? process.env : undefined;
+  if (env?.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL) {
+    config.resultadosBaseUrl = env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL;
+  }
+  if (env?.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL) {
+    config.ckanBaseUrl = env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL;
+  }
+
+  // Handle authentication headers from environment variables if explicitly configured
+  if (env?.API_KEY_HEADER && env?.API_KEY_VALUE) {
+    config.authHeaders = {
+      ...config.authHeaders,
+      [env.API_KEY_HEADER]: env.API_KEY_VALUE,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return validateAndNormalizeApiConfig(config);
+}
+
+function parsePositiveIntConfig(raw: string | undefined, fallback: number): number {
+  if (!raw) return fallback;
+  const parsed = parseInt(raw, 10);
+  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed <= 0) {
+    return fallback;
+  }
+  return parsed;
+}
+
+export function loadServerConfiguration(): ServerConfig {
+  const env = typeof process !== "undefined" ? process.env : undefined;
+  return {
+    transport: env?.MCP_TRANSPORT || SERVER.DEFAULT_TRANSPORT,
+    httpPort: parsePositiveIntConfig(env?.MCP_HTTP_PORT, SERVER.DEFAULT_HTTP_PORT),
+    httpHost: env?.MCP_HTTP_HOST?.trim() || "127.0.0.1",
+    cacheSize: parsePositiveIntConfig(env?.MCP_CACHE_SIZE, CACHE.DEFAULT_SIZE),
+    cacheTTL: parsePositiveIntConfig(env?.MCP_CACHE_TTL, CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS),
+    apiTimeout: TIMEOUTS.HTTP_REQUEST_MS,
+  };
+}
+
+export function resolveApiConfig(overrides?: Partial<ApiConfig>): ApiConfig {
+  const base = loadApiConfiguration();
+  if (!overrides) return base;
+  const merged: ApiConfig = {
+    ...base,
+    ...(overrides.resultadosBaseUrl !== undefined
+      ? { resultadosBaseUrl: overrides.resultadosBaseUrl }
+      : {}),
+    ...(overrides.ckanBaseUrl !== undefined ? { ckanBaseUrl: overrides.ckanBaseUrl } : {}),
+  };
+  if (base.authHeaders || overrides.authHeaders) {
+    merged.authHeaders = {
+      ...(base.authHeaders ?? {}),
+      ...(overrides.authHeaders ?? {}),
+    };
+  }
+  return validateAndNormalizeApiConfig(merged);
+}

+/**
+ * Centralized timeout configuration.
+ * All timeouts can be configured via environment variables.
+ */
+
+function getTimeoutMs(envVar: string, defaultValue: number): number {
+  const value = typeof process !== "undefined" ? process.env?.[envVar] : undefined;
+  if (!value) return defaultValue;
+
+  const parsed = parseInt(value, 10);
+  if (isNaN(parsed) || parsed <= 0) {
+    console.warn(
+      `[Config] Invalid timeout value for ${envVar}: ${value}, using default: ${defaultValue}ms`,
+    );
+    return defaultValue;
+  }
+
+  return parsed;
+}
+
+export const TIMEOUTS = {
+  /**
+   * HTTP request timeout for external TSE API calls
+   * Default: 8000ms (8 seconds)
+   * Env: MCP_API_TIMEOUT
+   */
+  get HTTP_REQUEST_MS(): number {
+    return getTimeoutMs("MCP_API_TIMEOUT", 8000);
+  },
+
+  /**
+   * Circuit breaker reset timeout
+   * Default: 30000ms (30 seconds)
+   * Env: MCP_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_TIMEOUT
+   */
+  get CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS(): number {
+    return getTimeoutMs("MCP_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_TIMEOUT", 30000);
+  },
+
+  /**
+   * SSE connection timeout (Cloudflare Workers)
+   * Default: 50000ms (50 seconds) - respects Workers CPU time limit
+   * Env: MCP_SSE_TIMEOUT
+   */
+  get SSE_CONNECTION_MS(): number {
+    return getTimeoutMs("MCP_SSE_TIMEOUT", 50000);
+  },
+
+  /**
+   * Ping interval for worker keep-alive
+   * Default: 30000ms (30 seconds)
+   * Env: MCP_PING_INTERVAL
+   */
+  get PING_INTERVAL_MS(): number {
+    return getTimeoutMs("MCP_PING_INTERVAL", 30000);
+  },
+
+  /**
+   * Rate limiter cleanup interval
+   * Default: 60000ms (1 minute)
+   * Env: MCP_RATE_LIMIT_CLEANUP_INTERVAL
+   */
+  get RATE_LIMIT_CLEANUP_MS(): number {
+    return getTimeoutMs("MCP_RATE_LIMIT_CLEANUP_INTERVAL", 60000);
+  },
+
+  /**
+   * Rate limit window duration
+   * Default: 60000ms (1 minute)
+   * Env: MCP_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW
+   */
+  get RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS(): number {
+    return getTimeoutMs("MCP_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW", 60000);
+  },
+};

+import { CacheEntry, Cache } from "../types/index.js";
+import { CACHE } from "../shared/utils/constants.js";
+
+export const MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS = 60;
+
+export interface KVPutOptions {
+  expiration?: number;
+  expirationTtl?: number;
+  metadata?: unknown;
+}
+
+interface KVNamespace {
+  get(key: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  put(key: string, value: string, options?: KVPutOptions): Promise<void>;
+  delete(key: string): Promise<void>;
+}
+
+export class MemoryCache implements Cache {
+  private cache = new Map<string, CacheEntry>();
+  private accessOrder = new Map<string, number>();
+  private accessCounter = 0;
+  private maxSize: number;
+  private ttl: number;
+  private cleanupInterval?: ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;
+
+  constructor(
+    maxSize: number = CACHE.DEFAULT_SIZE,
+    ttl: number = CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS,
+  ) {
+    this.maxSize = maxSize;
+    this.ttl = ttl;
+
+    if (
+      typeof process !== "undefined" &&
+      process.env?.MCP_CACHE_BACKGROUND_CLEANUP === "true"
+    ) {
+      this.startBackgroundCleanup();
+    }
+  }
+
+  get<T = unknown>(key: string): T | null {
+    const entry = this.cache.get(key);
+    if (!entry) return null;
+
+    if (Date.now() > entry.expires) {
+      this.cache.delete(key);
+      this.accessOrder.delete(key);
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    this.accessOrder.set(key, ++this.accessCounter);
+    return entry.data as T;
+  }
+
+  set<T = unknown>(key: string, data: T): void {
+    if (this.cache.size >= this.maxSize && !this.cache.has(key)) {
+      this.evictLRU();
+    }
+
+    this.cache.set(key, {
+      data,
+      expires: Date.now() + this.ttl,
+    });
+
+    this.accessOrder.set(key, ++this.accessCounter);
+  }
+
+  private startBackgroundCleanup(): void {
+    this.cleanupInterval = setInterval(() => {
+      this.cleanup();
+    }, CACHE.CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS);
+
+    (this.cleanupInterval as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
+  }
+
+  private cleanup(): void {
+    const now = Date.now();
+    for (const [key, entry] of this.cache.entries()) {
+      if (now > entry.expires) {
+        this.cache.delete(key);
+        this.accessOrder.delete(key);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  private evictLRU(): void {
+    let oldestKey = "";
+    let oldestAccess = Infinity;
+    for (const [key, accessTime] of this.accessOrder.entries()) {
+      if (accessTime < oldestAccess) {
+        oldestAccess = accessTime;
+        oldestKey = key;
+      }
+    }
+    if (oldestKey) {
+      this.cache.delete(oldestKey);
+      this.accessOrder.delete(oldestKey);
+    }
+  }
+
+  clear(): void {
+    if (this.cleanupInterval) {
+      clearInterval(this.cleanupInterval);
+      this.cleanupInterval = undefined;
+    }
+
+    this.cache.clear();
+    this.accessOrder.clear();
+    this.accessCounter = 0;
+  }
+
+  entries(): [string, unknown][] {
+    this.cleanup();
+    return Array.from(this.cache.entries()).map(([key, entry]) => [
+      key,
+      entry.data,
+    ]);
+  }
+}
+
+export class KVCache implements Cache {
+  constructor(private kv: KVNamespace, private ttl: number = CACHE.DEFAULT_TTL_MS) {}
+
+  async get<T = unknown>(key: string): Promise<T | null> {
+    const cached = await this.kv.get(key);
+    if (!cached) {
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      const parsed = JSON.parse(cached) as { expires?: unknown; data?: T } | null;
+      if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object" || typeof parsed.expires !== "number") {
+        return null;
+      }
+      if (Date.now() < parsed.expires) {
+        return parsed.data ?? null;
+      }
+    } catch {
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  async set<T = unknown>(key: string, data: T): Promise<void> {
+    const effectiveTtlMs =
+      Number.isFinite(this.ttl) && this.ttl > 0
+        ? this.ttl
+        : MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS * 1000;
+    const expirationTtl = Math.max(
+      MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS,
+      Math.ceil(effectiveTtlMs / 1000),
+    );
+
+    await this.kv.put(
+      key,
+      JSON.stringify({
+        data,
+        expires: Date.now() + effectiveTtlMs,
+      }),
+      { expirationTtl },
+    );
+  }
+
+  async clear(): Promise<void> {
+    // KV namespaces do not expose a global clear operation.
+  }
+}

+import { SERVER_NAME, SERVER_VERSION } from "../config/index.js";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../config/timeouts.js";
+import { CircuitBreaker, CircuitBreakerOpenError } from "../infrastructure/http/circuit-breaker.js";
+import { HttpResponse } from "../types/index.js";
+
+const MAX_CIRCUIT_BREAKERS = 100;
+const circuitBreakers = new Map<string, CircuitBreaker>();
+
+const MAX_RETRIES = 3;
+const INITIAL_RETRY_DELAY_MS = 1000;
+export const RETRY_STATUS_CODES = new Set([429, 500, 502, 503, 504]);
+
+export interface HttpJsonOptions {
+  noContent?: unknown;
+  maxRetries?: number;
+  initialRetryDelayMs?: number;
+  random?: () => number;
+}
+
+class TransientHttpError extends Error {
+  constructor(
+    public readonly response: HttpResponse,
+    public readonly retryable: boolean,
+  ) {
+    super(response.error ?? "transient http error");
+    this.name = "TransientHttpError";
+  }
+}
+
+interface AttemptOutcome {
+  response: HttpResponse;
+  retryable: boolean;
+}
+
+function createUpstreamBreaker(): CircuitBreaker {
+  return new CircuitBreaker({
+    failureThreshold: 5,
+    resetTimeoutMs: TIMEOUTS.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS,
+    halfOpenMaxAttempts: 3,
+  });
+}
+
+export function getUpstreamKey(url: string): string {
+  const parsed = new URL(url);
+  if ((parsed.protocol !== "http:" && parsed.protocol !== "https:") || parsed.origin === "null") {
+    throw new TypeError(`Unsupported URL protocol: ${parsed.protocol}`);
+  }
+  return parsed.origin.toLowerCase();
+}
+
+function getOrCreateCircuitBreaker(origin: string): CircuitBreaker {
+  const existing = circuitBreakers.get(origin);
+  if (existing) {
+    circuitBreakers.delete(origin);
+    circuitBreakers.set(origin, existing);
+    return existing;
+  }
+
+  if (circuitBreakers.size >= MAX_CIRCUIT_BREAKERS) {
+    let victimKey: string | undefined;
+    for (const [key, breaker] of circuitBreakers.entries()) {
+      if (breaker.getState() === "CLOSED") {
+        victimKey = key;
+        break;
+      }
+    }
+    if (victimKey === undefined) {
+      victimKey = circuitBreakers.keys().next().value;
+    }
+    if (victimKey !== undefined) {
+      circuitBreakers.delete(victimKey);
+    }
+  }
+
+  const breaker = createUpstreamBreaker();
+  circuitBreakers.set(origin, breaker);
+  return breaker;
+}
+
+export function getCircuitBreaker(urlOrOrigin: string): CircuitBreaker {
+  return getOrCreateCircuitBreaker(getUpstreamKey(urlOrOrigin));
+}
+
+export function getCircuitBreakerMetrics(): Record<string, ReturnType<CircuitBreaker["getMetrics"]>> {
+  const metrics: Record<string, ReturnType<CircuitBreaker["getMetrics"]>> = {};
+  for (const [origin, breaker] of circuitBreakers.entries()) {
+    metrics[origin] = breaker.getMetrics();
+  }
+  return metrics;
+}
+
+export function resetCircuitBreakers(): void {
+  circuitBreakers.clear();
+}
+
+export function formatTimeoutMessage(timeoutMs: number): string {
+  const seconds = Number((timeoutMs / 1000).toFixed(3));
+  return `request timeout after ${seconds} ${seconds === 1 ? "second" : "seconds"}`;
+}
+
+export function calculateBackoffDelayMs(
+  attempt: number,
+  initialDelayMs: number = INITIAL_RETRY_DELAY_MS,
+  random: () => number = Math.random,
+): number {
+  const baseDelay = initialDelayMs * Math.pow(2, attempt);
+  const half = baseDelay / 2;
+  const raw = random();
+  const r = Number.isFinite(raw) ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, raw)) : 0.5;
+  return Math.round(half + r * half);
+}
+
+function isAbortOrTimeoutError(error: unknown): boolean {
+  if (typeof error !== "object" || error === null) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  const name = (error as { name?: unknown }).name;
+  return name === "AbortError" || name === "TimeoutError";
+}
+
+function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
+}
+
+async function executeWithRetry(
+  fn: () => Promise<AttemptOutcome>,
+  shouldStopRetrying: () => boolean,
+  maxRetries: number,
+  initialRetryDelayMs: number,
+  random: () => number,
+): Promise<HttpResponse> {
+  let lastResponse: HttpResponse | null = null;
+
+  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++) {
+    const outcome = await fn();
+
+    if (outcome.response.ok || !outcome.retryable || shouldStopRetrying() || attempt === maxRetries) {
+      return outcome.response;
+    }
+
+    lastResponse = outcome.response;
+    const delayMs = calculateBackoffDelayMs(attempt, initialRetryDelayMs, random);
+    await sleep(delayMs);
+  }
+
+  return lastResponse!;
+}
+
+export async function httpJson(
+  url: string,
+  authHeaders?: Record<string, string>,
+  timeoutMs = TIMEOUTS.HTTP_REQUEST_MS,
+  options?: HttpJsonOptions,
+): Promise<HttpResponse> {
+  let origin: string;
+  try {
+    origin = getUpstreamKey(url);
+  } catch {
+    return { ok: false, error: "invalid url", source: url };
+  }
+
+  const effectiveTimeoutMs =
+    Number.isFinite(timeoutMs) && timeoutMs > 0 ? timeoutMs : TIMEOUTS.HTTP_REQUEST_MS;
+  const maxRetries =
+    options?.maxRetries !== undefined && Number.isFinite(options.maxRetries) && options.maxRetries >= 0
+      ? Math.floor(options.maxRetries)
+      : MAX_RETRIES;
+  const initialRetryDelayMs =
+    options?.initialRetryDelayMs !== undefined &&
+    Number.isFinite(options.initialRetryDelayMs) &&
+    options.initialRetryDelayMs >= 0
+      ? options.initialRetryDelayMs
+      : INITIAL_RETRY_DELAY_MS;
+  const random = options?.random ?? Math.random;
+
+  const breaker = getOrCreateCircuitBreaker(origin);
+
+  const runAttempt = async (): Promise<AttemptOutcome> => {
+    try {
+      const response = await breaker.execute(async (): Promise<HttpResponse> => {
+        const controller = new AbortController();
+        const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), effectiveTimeoutMs);
+        try {
+          const headers: Record<string, string> = {
+            "User-Agent": `${SERVER_NAME}/${SERVER_VERSION}`,
+            Accept: "application/json",
+            ...authHeaders,
+          };
+
+          let fetchResponse: Response;
+          try {
+            fetchResponse = await fetch(url, {
+              signal: controller.signal,
+              headers,
+            });
+          } catch (error: unknown) {
+            if (isAbortOrTimeoutError(error)) {
+              throw new TransientHttpError(
+                { ok: false, error: formatTimeoutMessage(effectiveTimeoutMs), source: url },
+                false,
+              );
+            }
+            throw new TransientHttpError({ ok: false, error: "network error", source: url }, true);
+          }
+
+          if (!fetchResponse.ok) {
+            const errorMap: Record<number, string> = {
+              404: "not found",
+              429: "rate limited",
+              500: "service temporarily unavailable",
+              502: "service temporarily unavailable",
+              503: "service temporarily unavailable",
+              504: "service temporarily unavailable",
+            };
+            const httpResponse: HttpResponse = {
+              ok: false,
+              error: errorMap[fetchResponse.status] || `HTTP ${fetchResponse.status}`,
+              source: url,
+            };
+            if (RETRY_STATUS_CODES.has(fetchResponse.status) || fetchResponse.status >= 500) {
+              throw new TransientHttpError(httpResponse, RETRY_STATUS_CODES.has(fetchResponse.status));
+            }
+            return httpResponse;
+          }
+
+          try {
+            const data =
+              fetchResponse.status === 204 && options && "noContent" in options
+                ? options.noContent
+                : await fetchResponse.json();
+            return { ok: true, data, source: url };
+          } catch (error: unknown) {
+            if (isAbortOrTimeoutError(error)) {
+              throw new TransientHttpError(
+                { ok: false, error: formatTimeoutMessage(effectiveTimeoutMs), source: url },
+                false,
+              );
+            }
+            throw new TransientHttpError({ ok: false, error: "invalid JSON response", source: url }, false);
+          }
+        } finally {
+          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
+        }
+      });
+
+      return { response, retryable: false };
+    } catch (error: unknown) {
+      if (error instanceof CircuitBreakerOpenError) {
+        return {
+          response: { ok: false, error: error.message, source: url },
+          retryable: false,
+        };
+      }
+      if (error instanceof TransientHttpError) {
+        return { response: error.response, retryable: error.retryable };
+      }
+      return {
+        response: { ok: false, error: "unknown error", source: url },
+        retryable: false,
+      };
+    }
+  };
+
+  return executeWithRetry(
+    runAttempt,
+    () => breaker.getState() === "OPEN",
+    maxRetries,
+    initialRetryDelayMs,
+    random,
+  );
+}

+++ b/lib/core/mcp-server.ts

+import { Server } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js";
+import {
+  CallToolRequestSchema,
+  ErrorCode,
+  ListResourcesRequestSchema,
+  ListToolsRequestSchema,
+  McpError,
+  ReadResourceRequestSchema,
+} from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js";
+import { SERVER_NAME, SERVER_VERSION } from "../config/index.js";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../config/timeouts.js";
+import { RateLimiter } from "../infrastructure/rate-limiter.js";
+import { createLogger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import { RequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { serializeBoundedToolOutput } from "../shared/agentic-guard.js";
+import { formatErrorResponse, RateLimitError } from "../shared/errors.js";
+import { ApiConfig, Cache } from "../types/index.js";
+import { TOOL_DEFINITIONS, executeTool } from "./tools.js";
+
+const mcpLogger = createLogger("mcp-server");
+const DEFAULT_MCP_TOOL_RATE_LIMIT_MAX = 120;
+
+function parsePositiveIntEnv(name: string): number | undefined {
+  const raw = typeof process !== "undefined" ? process.env?.[name] : undefined;
+  if (!raw) return undefined;
+  const parsed = parseInt(raw, 10);
+  return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : undefined;
+}
+
+export interface MCPServerOptions {
+  apiConfig: ApiConfig;
+  cache?: Cache;
+  rateLimit?: { windowMs: number; maxRequests: number } | false;
+  getRequestContext?: () => RequestContext | undefined;
+}
+
+function formatErrorPayload(error: unknown) {
+  const details = formatErrorResponse(error);
+  return {
+    content: [
+      {
+        type: "text" as const,
+        text: JSON.stringify(
+          {
+            error: details.message,
+            code: details.code,
+            ...(details.data ? { details: details.data } : {}),
+          },
+          null,
+          2,
+        ),
+      },
+    ],
+    isError: true,
+  };
+}
+
+export function createMCPServer(options: MCPServerOptions): Server {
+  const { apiConfig, cache, rateLimit, getRequestContext } = options;
+
+  const disableRateLimit =
+    rateLimit === false ||
+    (typeof process !== "undefined" && process.env?.MCP_DISABLE_RATE_LIMIT === "true");
+  const toolRateLimiter = disableRateLimit
+    ? null
+    : new RateLimiter({
+        windowMs:
+          typeof rateLimit === "object" ? rateLimit.windowMs : TIMEOUTS.RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS,
+        maxRequests:
+          typeof rateLimit === "object"
+            ? rateLimit.maxRequests
+            : (parsePositiveIntEnv("MCP_TOOL_RATE_LIMIT_MAX") ?? DEFAULT_MCP_TOOL_RATE_LIMIT_MAX),
+        enableCleanupTimer: false,
+      });
+
+  const server = new Server(
+    { name: SERVER_NAME, version: SERVER_VERSION },
+    { capabilities: { tools: {}, resources: {} } },
+  );
+
+  server.setRequestHandler(ListToolsRequestSchema, async () => ({
+    tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
+  }));
+
+  server.setRequestHandler(ListResourcesRequestSchema, async () => ({
+    resources: [],
+  }));
+
+  server.setRequestHandler(ReadResourceRequestSchema, async () => {
+    throw new McpError(ErrorCode.InvalidParams, "Unknown resource URI");
+  });
+
+  server.setRequestHandler(CallToolRequestSchema, async (request) => {
+    const { name, arguments: args } = request.params;
+    const requestContext = getRequestContext?.() ?? new RequestContext();
+    requestContext.addMetadata("tool", name);
+
+    if (toolRateLimiter && !toolRateLimiter.checkLimit("mcp-session")) {
+      const retryAfter = Math.max(
+        1,
+        Math.ceil(toolRateLimiter.getResetTime("mcp-session") / 1000),
+      );
+      mcpLogger.warn("mcp.rate_limited", { tool: name, retryAfter }, requestContext.requestId);
+      return formatErrorPayload(new RateLimitError(retryAfter));
+    }
+
+    try {
+      const result = await executeTool(name, args, apiConfig, cache, {
+        requestId: requestContext.requestId,
+        requestContext,
+      });
+
+      if (result.ok) {
+        return {
+          content: [
+            {
+              type: "text",
+              text: serializeBoundedToolOutput(result.data),
+            },
+          ],
+        };
+      }
+
+      return formatErrorPayload(new Error(result.error));
+    } catch (error: unknown) {
+      return formatErrorPayload(error);
+    }
+  });
+
+  return server;
+}
diff --git a/lib/core/registry.ts b/lib/core/registry.ts

@@ -0,0 +1,89 @@
+/**
+ * Tool Registry — Auto-registration pattern for MCP tools.
+ */
+
+import type { ZodType } from "zod";
+import type { RequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { Cache, LookupResult, ApiConfig } from "../types/index.js";
+
+export interface ToolExecutionContext {
+  sessionId?: string;
+  requestId?: string;
+  requestContext?: RequestContext;
+}
+
+export interface ToolHandler {
+  name: string;
+  description: string;
+  inputSchema: Record<string, unknown>;
+  zodSchema: ZodType;
+  execute: (
+    args: unknown,
+    apiConfig: ApiConfig,
+    cache?: Cache,
+    context?: ToolExecutionContext,
+  ) => Promise<LookupResult>;
+}
+
+const registry = new Map<string, ToolHandler>();
+
+export function registerTool(tool: ToolHandler): void {
+  if (registry.has(tool.name)) {
+    console.warn(`[registry] Tool "${tool.name}" already registered, overwriting.`);
+  }
+  registry.set(tool.name, tool);
+}
+
+export function getToolDefinitions(): Array<{
+  name: string;
+  description: string;
+  inputSchema: Record<string, unknown>;
+}> {
+  return Array.from(registry.values()).map(({ name, description, inputSchema }) => ({
+    name,
+    description,
+    inputSchema,
+  }));
+}
+
+export async function executeRegisteredTool(
+  name: string,
+  args: unknown,
+  apiConfig: ApiConfig,
+  cache?: Cache,
+  context?: ToolExecutionContext,
+): Promise<LookupResult> {
+  const tool = registry.get(name);
+  if (!tool) {
+    throw new Error(`Unknown tool: ${name}. Available: ${Array.from(registry.keys()).join(", ")}`);
+  }
+
+  const input = args === undefined ? {} : args;
+  const parsed = tool.zodSchema.safeParse(input);
+  if (!parsed.success) {
+    const details = parsed.error.issues
+      .map((issue) => {
+        const path = issue.path.length > 0 ? `${issue.path.join(".")}: ` : "";
+        return `${path}${issue.message}`;
+      })
+      .join("; ");
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      error: `Invalid parameters for ${name}: ${details}`,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return tool.execute(parsed.data, apiConfig, cache, context);
+}
+
+export function hasTool(name: string): boolean {
+  return registry.has(name);
+}
+
+export function hasToolSchema(name: string): boolean {
+  return Boolean(registry.get(name)?.zodSchema);
+}
+
+export function getToolCount(): number {
+  return registry.size;
+}

+++ b/lib/core/tools.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,187 @@
+/**
+ * Tool orchestration — slim wrapper using the tool registry.
+ */
+
+import { Cache, LookupResult, Metrics, ApiConfig, ToolMetrics } from "../types/index.js";
+import type { ToolExecutionContext } from "./registry.js";
+import { createLogger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import { RequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { maskSensitiveText } from "../shared/log-redaction.js";
+import {
+  getToolDefinitions,
+  executeRegisteredTool,
+  getToolCount,
+} from "../tools/index.js";
+
+const toolsLogger = createLogger("tools");
+
+interface MutableToolMetrics {
+  requests: number;
+  errors: number;
+  cacheHits: number;
+  totalTimeMs: number;
+  maxTimeMs: number;
+}
+
+let metrics: Metrics = {
+  requests: 0,
+  cacheHits: 0,
+  errors: 0,
+  totalTime: 0,
+  startTime: Date.now(),
+};
+
+const toolMetrics = new Map<string, MutableToolMetrics>();
+let lastMetricsLog = 0;
+
+function logPerformanceMetrics(): void {
+  const uptime = (Date.now() - metrics.startTime) / 1000;
+  const avgTime = metrics.requests > 0 ? metrics.totalTime / metrics.requests : 0;
+  console.error(
+    `[metrics] requests=${metrics.requests} cache_hits=${metrics.cacheHits} errors=${metrics.errors} avg_time=${avgTime.toFixed(0)}ms uptime=${uptime.toFixed(0)}s tools=${getToolCount()}`,
+  );
+}
+
+function recordMetrics(
+  elapsed: number,
+  fromCache: boolean,
+  error: boolean,
+  toolName?: string,
+): void {
+  metrics.requests++;
+  metrics.totalTime += elapsed;
+  if (fromCache) metrics.cacheHits++;
+  if (error) metrics.errors++;
+
+  if (toolName) {
+    const existing = toolMetrics.get(toolName) ?? {
+      requests: 0,
+      errors: 0,
+      cacheHits: 0,
+      totalTimeMs: 0,
+      maxTimeMs: 0,
+    };
+    existing.requests++;
+    existing.totalTimeMs += elapsed;
+    if (elapsed > existing.maxTimeMs) existing.maxTimeMs = elapsed;
+    if (fromCache) existing.cacheHits++;
+    if (error) existing.errors++;
+    toolMetrics.set(toolName, existing);
+  }
+
+  if (metrics.requests - lastMetricsLog >= 100) {
+    logPerformanceMetrics();
+    lastMetricsLog = metrics.requests;
+  }
+}
+
+function createObservedCache(cache: Cache, onHit: () => void): Cache {
+  const observed: Cache = {
+    get: async <T = unknown>(key: string): Promise<T | null> => {
+      const value = await cache.get<T>(key);
+      if (value !== null && value !== undefined) {
+        onHit();
+      }
+      return value;
+    },
+    set: <T = unknown>(key: string, data: T): Promise<void> | void => cache.set(key, data),
+  };
+  if (cache.clear) {
+    observed.clear = () => cache.clear!();
+  }
+  return observed;
+}
+
+export const TOOL_DEFINITIONS = getToolDefinitions();
+
+export async function executeTool(
+  name: string,
+  args: unknown,
+  apiConfig: ApiConfig,
+  cache?: Cache,
+  context?: ToolExecutionContext,
+): Promise<LookupResult> {
+  const startTime = Date.now();
+  const requestContext = context?.requestContext ?? new RequestContext(context?.requestId);
+  let cacheHit = false;
+  const observedCache = cache
+    ? createObservedCache(cache, () => {
+        cacheHit = true;
+      })
+    : undefined;
+
+  try {
+    const result = await executeRegisteredTool(name, args, apiConfig, observedCache, {
+      ...context,
+      requestId: requestContext.requestId,
+      requestContext,
+    });
+    const elapsedMs = Date.now() - startTime;
+    recordMetrics(elapsedMs, cacheHit, !result.ok, name);
+    if (result.ok) {
+      toolsLogger.info(
+        "tool.executed",
+        { tool: name, ok: true, cacheHit, elapsedMs },
+        requestContext.requestId,
+      );
+    } else {
+      toolsLogger.warn(
+        "tool.failed",
+        {
+          tool: name,
+          ok: false,
+          cacheHit,
+          elapsedMs,
+          error: maskSensitiveText(result.error || "unknown error"),
+        },
+        requestContext.requestId,
+      );
+    }
+    return result;
+  } catch (err) {
+    const elapsedMs = Date.now() - startTime;
+    recordMetrics(elapsedMs, false, true, name);
+    const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
+    toolsLogger.error(
+      "tool.exception",
+      {
+        tool: name,
+        elapsedMs,
+        error: maskSensitiveText(errMsg),
+      },
+      requestContext.requestId,
+    );
+    throw err;
+  }
+}
+
+export function getMetrics(): Metrics {
+  return { ...metrics };
+}
+
+export function getToolMetrics(): Record<string, ToolMetrics> {
+  const snapshot: Record<string, ToolMetrics> = {};
+  for (const [name, entry] of toolMetrics.entries()) {
+    snapshot[name] = {
+      requests: entry.requests,
+      errors: entry.errors,
+      cacheHits: entry.cacheHits,
+      totalTimeMs: entry.totalTimeMs,
+      avgTimeMs: entry.requests > 0 ? Math.round(entry.totalTimeMs / entry.requests) : 0,
+      maxTimeMs: entry.maxTimeMs,
+    };
+  }
+  return snapshot;
+}
+
+export function resetMetrics(): void {
+  metrics = {
+    requests: 0,
+    cacheHits: 0,
+    errors: 0,
+    totalTime: 0,
+    startTime: Date.now(),
+  };
+  lastMetricsLog = 0;
+  toolMetrics.clear();
+}

diff --git a/lib/infrastructure/http/circuit-breaker.ts b/lib/infrastructure/http/circuit-breaker.ts
new file mode 100644

+++ b/lib/infrastructure/http/circuit-breaker.ts

+/**
+ * Circuit Breaker implementation protecting against cascading failures in external API calls.
+ */
+
+export type CircuitState = "CLOSED" | "OPEN" | "HALF_OPEN";
+
+export interface CircuitBreakerConfig {
+  failureThreshold: number;
+  resetTimeoutMs: number;
+  halfOpenMaxAttempts: number;
+}
+
+export class CircuitBreaker {
+  private failures = 0;
+  private lastFailureTime = 0;
+  private state: CircuitState = "CLOSED";
+  private halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+  private readonly config: CircuitBreakerConfig;
+
+  constructor(config?: Partial<CircuitBreakerConfig>) {
+    this.config = {
+      failureThreshold: config?.failureThreshold ?? 5,
+      resetTimeoutMs: config?.resetTimeoutMs ?? 30000,
+      halfOpenMaxAttempts: config?.halfOpenMaxAttempts ?? 3,
+    };
+  }
+
+  async execute<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
+    if (this.state === "OPEN") {
+      const timeSinceLastFailure = Date.now() - this.lastFailureTime;
+
+      if (timeSinceLastFailure < this.config.resetTimeoutMs) {
+        throw new CircuitBreakerOpenError(
+          `Circuit breaker is OPEN. Retry after ${Math.ceil((this.config.resetTimeoutMs - timeSinceLastFailure) / 1000)}s`,
+        );
+      }
+
+      this.state = "HALF_OPEN";
+      this.halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+    }
+
+    if (this.state === "HALF_OPEN") {
+      if (this.halfOpenAttempts >= this.config.halfOpenMaxAttempts) {
+        throw new CircuitBreakerOpenError("Circuit breaker HALF_OPEN limit reached");
+      }
+      this.halfOpenAttempts++;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      const result = await fn();
+      this.onSuccess();
+      return result;
+    } catch (error) {
+      this.onFailure();
+      throw error;
+    }
+  }
+
+  private onSuccess(): void {
+    this.failures = 0;
+    this.halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+    this.state = "CLOSED";
+  }
+
+  private onFailure(): void {
+    this.failures++;
+    this.lastFailureTime = Date.now();
+
+    if (this.state === "HALF_OPEN") {
+      this.state = "OPEN";
+      this.halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+    } else if (this.failures >= this.config.failureThreshold) {
+      this.state = "OPEN";
+    }
+  }
+
+  getState(): CircuitState {
+    return this.state;
+  }
+
+  getMetrics() {
+    return {
+      state: this.state,
+      failures: this.failures,
+      lastFailureTime: this.lastFailureTime,
+      timeSinceLastFailure: this.lastFailureTime ? Date.now() - this.lastFailureTime : null,
+    };
+  }
+
+  reset(): void {
+    this.failures = 0;
+    this.lastFailureTime = 0;
+    this.state = "CLOSED";
+    this.halfOpenAttempts = 0;
+  }
+}
+
+export class CircuitBreakerOpenError extends Error {
+  constructor(message: string) {
+    super(message);
+    this.name = "CircuitBreakerOpenError";
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * Structured logging system with levels.
+ * Always writes to stderr (console.error) so stdio MCP JSON-RPC framing on stdout is never corrupted.
+ */
+
+export enum LogLevel {
+  DEBUG = 0,
+  INFO = 1,
+  WARN = 2,
+  ERROR = 3,
+}
+
+export interface LogContext {
+  [key: string]: unknown;
+}
+
+export class Logger {
+  private explicitLevel?: LogLevel;
+  private module: string;
+
+  constructor(module: string = "app", level?: LogLevel) {
+    this.module = module;
+    this.explicitLevel = level;
+  }
+
+  private getLogLevelFromEnv(): LogLevel {
+    const env = typeof process !== "undefined" && process.env ? process.env : undefined;
+    const envLevel = env?.LOG_LEVEL?.toLowerCase();
+    switch (envLevel) {
+      case "debug":
+        return LogLevel.DEBUG;
+      case "info":
+        return LogLevel.INFO;
+      case "warn":
+        return LogLevel.WARN;
+      case "error":
+        return LogLevel.ERROR;
+      default:
+        return env?.NODE_ENV === "development" ? LogLevel.DEBUG : LogLevel.INFO;
+    }
+  }
+
+  getLevel(): LogLevel {
+    return this.explicitLevel ?? this.getLogLevelFromEnv();
+  }
+
+  setLevel(level: LogLevel): void {
+    this.explicitLevel = level;
+  }
+
+  private shouldLog(level: LogLevel): boolean {
+    return level >= this.getLevel();
+  }
+
+  private formatMessage(
+    level: string,
+    message: string,
+    context?: LogContext,
+    requestId?: string,
+  ): string {
+    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
+    const logObject: Record<string, unknown> = {
+      timestamp,
+      level,
+      module: this.module,
+      message,
+    };
+
+    if (requestId) {
+      logObject.requestId = requestId;
+    }
+
+    if (context !== undefined) {
+      logObject.context = context;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      return JSON.stringify(logObject);
+    } catch {
+      return JSON.stringify({
+        timestamp,
+        level,
+        module: this.module,
+        message,
+        ...(requestId ? { requestId } : {}),
+        contextUnserializable: true,
+      });
+    }
+  }
+
+  debug(message: string, context?: LogContext, requestId?: string): void {
+    if (this.shouldLog(LogLevel.DEBUG)) {
+      console.error(this.formatMessage("debug", message, context, requestId));
+    }
+  }
+
+  info(message: string, context?: LogContext, requestId?: string): void {
+    if (this.shouldLog(LogLevel.INFO)) {
+      console.error(this.formatMessage("info", message, context, requestId));
+    }
+  }
+
+  warn(message: string, context?: LogContext, requestId?: string): void {
+    if (this.shouldLog(LogLevel.WARN)) {
+      console.error(this.formatMessage("warn", message, context, requestId));
+    }
+  }
+
+  error(message: string, context?: LogContext, requestId?: string): void {
+    if (this.shouldLog(LogLevel.ERROR)) {
+      console.error(this.formatMessage("error", message, context, requestId));
+    }
+  }
+
+  legacyLog(message: string): void {
+    console.error(message);
+  }
+}
+
+export const logger = new Logger("mcp-tse");
+
+export function createLogger(module: string, level?: LogLevel): Logger {
+  return new Logger(module, level);
+}
