+/**
+ * OpenAPI 3.0 schema generation for Cloudflare Workers and REST clients.
+ */
+
+import { Env, getCorsHeaders } from "../adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { SERVER_VERSION } from "../config/index.js";
+import { TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "../core/tools.js";
+
+export function generateOpenAPISchema(requestUrl: string): unknown {
+  const origin = new URL(requestUrl).origin;
+  const toolNames = TOOL_DEFINITIONS.map((tool) => tool.name);
+
+  return {
+    openapi: "3.0.0",
+    info: {
+      title: "MCP TSE API",
+      description:
+        "Brazilian electoral public data API (TSE Resultados EA20 & Dados Abertos CKAN) with MCP JSON-RPC, SSE, and REST tool endpoints",
+      version: SERVER_VERSION,
+      contact: {
+        name: "MCP TSE Maintainers / DPO",
+        url: "https://github.com/cristianoaredes/mcp-tse/blob/main/PRIVACY.md",
+      },
+      "x-privacy-policy":
+        "https://github.com/cristianoaredes/mcp-tse/blob/main/PRIVACY.md",
+    },
+    servers: [{ url: origin }],
+    security: [{ ApiKeyAuth: [] }, { BearerAuth: [] }],
+    components: {
+      securitySchemes: {
+        ApiKeyAuth: {
+          type: "apiKey",
+          in: "header",
+          name: "X-API-Key",
+          description: "API key for authentication via X-API-Key header.",
+        },
+        BearerAuth: {
+          type: "http",
+          scheme: "bearer",
+          description:
+            "API key for authentication via 'Authorization: Bearer <key>' header.",
+        },
+      },
+    },
+    paths: {
+      "/health": {
+        get: {
+          summary: "Worker health check",
+          security: [],
+          responses: {
+            "200": { description: "Service healthy" },
+            "503": { description: "Service degraded (KV unavailable)" },
+          },
+        },
+      },
+      "/mcp": {
+        post: {
+          summary: "MCP JSON-RPC endpoint",
+          description: "Model Context Protocol JSON-RPC endpoint for AI assistants",
+          security: [{ ApiKeyAuth: [] }, { BearerAuth: [] }],
+          requestBody: {
+            required: true,
+            content: {
+              "application/json": {
+                schema: {
+                  type: "object",
+                  properties: {
+                    jsonrpc: { type: "string", enum: ["2.0"] },
+                    id: { type: ["string", "number", "null"] },
+                    method: { type: "string" },
+                    params: { type: "object" },
+                  },
+                  required: ["jsonrpc", "method"],
+                },
+              },
+            },
+          },
+          responses: {
+            "200": { description: "MCP JSON-RPC response" },
+            "400": { description: "Invalid JSON payload" },
+            "401": { description: "Unauthorized - API key required" },
+          },
+        },
+      },
+      "/sse": {
+        get: {
+          summary: "MCP Server-Sent Events endpoint",
+          description: "Model Context Protocol streaming endpoint for real-time communication",
+          security: [{ ApiKeyAuth: [] }, { BearerAuth: [] }],
+          responses: {
+            "200": { description: "SSE stream established" },
+            "400": { description: "Invalid Accept header" },
+            "401": { description: "Unauthorized - API key required" },
+          },
+        },
+      },
+      "/tools/{toolName}": {
+        post: {
+          summary: "Invoke a registered TSE MCP tool via REST",
+          security: [{ ApiKeyAuth: [] }, { BearerAuth: [] }],
+          parameters: [
+            {
+              name: "toolName",
+              in: "path",
+              required: true,
+              schema: {
+                type: "string",
+                enum: toolNames,
+              },
+              description: "Registered TSE MCP tool name",
+            },
+          ],
+          requestBody: {
+            required: true,
+            content: {
+              "application/json": {
+                schema: {
+                  type: "object",
+                  additionalProperties: true,
+                },
+              },
+            },
+          },
+          responses: {
+            "200": { description: "Tool execution result" },
+            "400": { description: "Invalid parameters or JSON payload" },
+            "401": { description: "Unauthorized - API key required" },
+            "404": { description: "Unknown tool or upstream resource not found" },
+            "429": { description: "Rate limit exceeded" },
+          },
+        },
+      },
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+export function handleOpenAPIEndpoint(request: Request, env?: Env): Response {
+  const schema = generateOpenAPISchema(request.url);
+
+  return new Response(JSON.stringify(schema), {
+    status: 200,
+    headers: {
+      "Content-Type": "application/json",
+      ...getCorsHeaders(request, env, { isPublic: true }),
+    },
+  });
+}

+/**
+ * Rate limiting middleware for Cloudflare Workers.
+ */
+
+import { Env, corsHeaders } from "../adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS } from "../core/cache.js";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../config/timeouts.js";
+import { createLogger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import type { RequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { maskSensitiveText } from "../shared/log-redaction.js";
+
+const rateLimitLogger = createLogger("worker-rate-limit");
+
+const RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS = 30;
+const CLIENT_ID_REGEX = /^[A-Za-z0-9.:_-]{1,64}$/;
+
+export interface RateLimitResult {
+  allowed: boolean;
+  error?: Response;
+}
+
+export function extractClientIdentifier(request: Request, env: Env): string {
+  const cfIp = request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP")?.trim();
+  if (cfIp && CLIENT_ID_REGEX.test(cfIp)) {
+    return cfIp;
+  }
+
+  if (env.MCP_TRUST_X_FORWARDED_FOR === "true") {
+    const xff = request.headers.get("X-Forwarded-For");
+    const firstIp = xff?.split(",")[0]?.trim();
+    if (firstIp && CLIENT_ID_REGEX.test(firstIp)) {
+      return firstIp;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return "unknown";
+}
+
+export async function checkRateLimit(
+  request: Request,
+  env: Env,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext,
+): Promise<RateLimitResult> {
+  const url = new URL(request.url);
+  if (url.pathname === "/health") {
+    return { allowed: true };
+  }
+
+  if (env.MCP_DISABLE_RATE_LIMIT === "true") {
+    return { allowed: true };
+  }
+
+  const clientIP = extractClientIdentifier(request, env);
+
+  if (!env.MCP_KV) {
+    return { allowed: true };
+  }
+
+  const windowMs = TIMEOUTS.RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS;
+  const now = Date.now();
+  const windowBucket = Math.floor(now / windowMs);
+  const windowStart = windowBucket * windowMs;
+  const resetTime = windowStart + windowMs;
+  const key = `rate_limit:${clientIP}:${windowBucket}`;
+  const expirationTtl = Math.max(
+    MIN_KV_EXPIRATION_TTL_SECONDS,
+    Math.ceil(windowMs / 1000) * 2,
+  );
+
+  try {
+    const data = await env.MCP_KV.get(key);
+    let requestCount = 0;
+
+    if (data) {
+      try {
+        const parsed = JSON.parse(data) as { count?: unknown } | null;
+        if (
+          parsed &&
+          typeof parsed === "object" &&
+          typeof parsed.count === "number" &&
+          Number.isFinite(parsed.count) &&
+          parsed.count >= 0
+        ) {
+          requestCount = parsed.count;
+        }
+      } catch {
+        requestCount = 0;
+      }
+    }
+
+    requestCount += 1;
+
+    if (requestCount > RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS) {
+      const retryAfter = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((resetTime - now) / 1000));
+
+      return {
+        allowed: false,
+        error: new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({
+            error: "Rate Limit Exceeded",
+            message: `Too many requests. Maximum ${RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS} requests per ${windowMs / 1000} seconds.`,
+            retryAfter,
+          }),
+          {
+            status: 429,
+            headers: {
+              "Content-Type": "application/json",
+              "Retry-After": retryAfter.toString(),
+              "X-RateLimit-Limit": RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS.toString(),
+              "X-RateLimit-Remaining": "0",
+              "X-RateLimit-Reset": Math.ceil(resetTime / 1000).toString(),
+              ...corsHeaders,
+            },
+          },
+        ),
+      };
+    }
+
+    await env.MCP_KV.put(
+      key,
+      JSON.stringify({
+        count: requestCount,
+        windowStart,
+      }),
+      { expirationTtl },
+    );
+
+    return { allowed: true };
+  } catch (error) {
+    rateLimitLogger.error(
+      "rate_limit.check_failed",
+      {
+        error: maskSensitiveText(
+          error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
+        ),
+      },
+      requestContext?.requestId,
+    );
+    return { allowed: true };
+  }
+}
diff --git a/lib/workers/rest.ts b/lib/workers/rest.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d3e1382

@@ -0,0 +1,146 @@
+/**
+ * Generic REST API endpoint for invoking registered TSE MCP tools over HTTP POST /tools/{toolName}.
+ */
+
+import { corsHeaders, Env, handleMCPRequest } from "../adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { hasTool } from "../core/registry.js";
+import { createLogger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import type { RequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { maskSensitiveText } from "../shared/log-redaction.js";
+import { MCPRequest } from "../types/index.js";
+
+const restLogger = createLogger("worker-rest");
+
+function invalidJsonResponse(): { ok: false; response: Response } {
+  return {
+    ok: false,
+    response: new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        error: "Bad Request",
+        message: "Invalid JSON payload",
+      }),
+      {
+        status: 400,
+        headers: {
+          "Content-Type": "application/json",
+          ...corsHeaders,
+        },
+      },
+    ),
+  };
+}
+
+async function parseJsonBody(
+  request: Request,
+): Promise<{ ok: true; data: Record<string, unknown> } | { ok: false; response: Response }> {
+  let parsed: unknown;
+  try {
+    parsed = await request.json();
+  } catch (error: unknown) {
+    if (error instanceof SyntaxError || (error instanceof Error && error.name === "SyntaxError")) {
+      return invalidJsonResponse();
+    }
+    throw error;
+  }
+
+  if (parsed === null || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
+    return invalidJsonResponse();
+  }
+
+  return { ok: true, data: parsed as Record<string, unknown> };
+}
+
+function internalServerErrorResponse(endpoint: string, error: unknown): Response {
+  restLogger.error("rest.handler_error", {
+    endpoint,
+    error: maskSensitiveText(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)),
+  });
+  return new Response(
+    JSON.stringify({
+      error: "Internal server error",
+    }),
+    {
+      status: 500,
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        ...corsHeaders,
+      },
+    },
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Handle POST /tools/{toolName} REST endpoint.
+ */
+export async function handleToolRestEndpoint(
+  toolName: string,
+  request: Request,
+  env: Env,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext,
+): Promise<Response> {
+  if (!hasTool(toolName)) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        error: "Not Found",
+        message: `Unknown tool: ${toolName}`,
+      }),
+      {
+        status: 404,
+        headers: {
+          "Content-Type": "application/json",
+          ...corsHeaders,
+        },
+      },
+    );
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const parsedBody = await parseJsonBody(request);
+    if (!parsedBody.ok) {
+      return parsedBody.response;
+    }
+
+    const mcpRequest: MCPRequest = {
+      jsonrpc: "2.0",
+      id: Date.now(),
+      method: "tools/call",
+      params: {
+        name: toolName,
+        arguments: parsedBody.data,
+      },
+    };
+
+    const mcpResponse = await handleMCPRequest(mcpRequest, env, requestContext);
+
+    if (mcpResponse.error) {
+      const isNotFound =
+        mcpResponse.error.code === -32603 &&
+        typeof mcpResponse.error.data === "string" &&
+        /not found/i.test(mcpResponse.error.data);
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({
+          error: mcpResponse.error.message,
+          code: mcpResponse.error.code,
+          data: mcpResponse.error.data,
+        }),
+        {
+          status: isNotFound ? 404 : 400,
+          headers: {
+            "Content-Type": "application/json",
+            ...corsHeaders,
+          },
+        },
+      );
+    }
+
+    return new Response(JSON.stringify(mcpResponse.result), {
+      status: 200,
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        ...corsHeaders,
+      },
+    });
+  } catch (error: unknown) {
+    return internalServerErrorResponse(`/tools/${toolName}`, error);
+  }
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,261 @@
+/**
+ * Server-Sent Events (SSE) handler for Cloudflare Workers.
+ */
+
+import { Env, handleMCPRequest, corsHeaders } from "../adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { SERVER_NAME, SERVER_VERSION } from "../config/index.js";
+import { MCPRequest } from "../types/index.js";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../config/timeouts.js";
+import { createLogger } from "../infrastructure/logger.js";
+import type { RequestContext } from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { maskSensitiveText } from "../shared/log-redaction.js";
+import "./types.js";
+
+const sseLogger = createLogger("worker-sse");
+
+type SSECloseReason = "timeout" | "abort" | "closed" | "error";
+
+const SSE_STREAM_HIGH_WATER_MARK = 16;
+
+export async function handleSSEEndpoint(
+  request: Request,
+  env: Env,
+  ctx?: ExecutionContext,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext,
+): Promise<Response> {
+  const acceptHeader = request.headers.get("Accept");
+  if (
+    acceptHeader &&
+    !acceptHeader.includes("text/event-stream") &&
+    !acceptHeader.includes("*/*")
+  ) {
+    return new Response("SSE endpoint requires Accept: text/event-stream", {
+      status: 400,
+      headers: corsHeaders,
+    });
+  }
+
+  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+
+  const sseHeaders = {
+    "Content-Type": "text/event-stream",
+    "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
+    Connection: "keep-alive",
+    ...corsHeaders,
+  };
+
+  let closed = false;
+  let pingInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
+  let timeoutHandle: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let abortListener: (() => void) | undefined;
+  let streamController: ReadableStreamDefaultController<Uint8Array> | undefined;
+  let resolveDone!: () => void;
+
+  const donePromise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
+    resolveDone = resolve;
+  });
+  ctx?.waitUntil(donePromise);
+
+  const cleanup = (reason: SSECloseReason): void => {
+    if (closed) {
+      return;
+    }
+    closed = true;
+
+    try {
+      if (pingInterval !== undefined) {
+        clearInterval(pingInterval);
+        pingInterval = undefined;
+      }
+      if (timeoutHandle !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(timeoutHandle);
+        timeoutHandle = undefined;
+      }
+      if (abortListener && request.signal) {
+        request.signal.removeEventListener("abort", abortListener);
+        abortListener = undefined;
+      }
+
+      if (reason !== "closed" && streamController) {
+        try {
+          if (reason === "abort") {
+            streamController.error(new Error("SSE client disconnected"));
+          } else {
+            streamController.close();
+          }
+        } catch {
+          // Ignore errors when controller is already closed, cancelled, or errored
+        }
+      }
+    } finally {
+      resolveDone();
+    }
+  };
+
+  const sendSSEMessage = (
+    data: unknown,
+    event?: string,
+    id?: string,
+    options?: { dropOnBackpressure?: boolean },
+  ): boolean => {
+    if (closed || !streamController) {
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    if (
+      options?.dropOnBackpressure &&
+      streamController.desiredSize !== null &&
+      streamController.desiredSize <= 0
+    ) {
+      return true;
+    }
+
+    let message = "";
+    if (id) message += `id: ${id}\n`;
+    if (event) message += `event: ${event}\n`;
+    message += `data: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`;
+
+    try {
+      streamController.enqueue(encoder.encode(message));
+      return true;
+    } catch (error) {
+      sseLogger.error(
+        "sse.write_error",
+        { error: maskSensitiveText(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)) },
+        requestContext?.requestId,
+      );
+      cleanup("error");
+      return false;
+    }
+  };
+
+  const readable = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>(
+    {
+      start(controller) {
+        streamController = controller;
+
+        if (request.signal) {
+          if (request.signal.aborted) {
+            cleanup("abort");
+            return;
+          }
+          abortListener = () => cleanup("abort");
+          request.signal.addEventListener("abort", abortListener, { once: true });
+        }
+
+        timeoutHandle = setTimeout(() => {
+          sseLogger.info(
+            "sse.connection_timeout",
+            { reason: "timeout" },
+            requestContext?.requestId,
+          );
+          cleanup("timeout");
+        }, TIMEOUTS.SSE_CONNECTION_MS);
+        (timeoutHandle as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
+
+        if (
+          !sendSSEMessage(
+            {
+              type: "connection",
+              status: "connected",
+              server: SERVER_NAME,
+              version: SERVER_VERSION,
+              timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
+            },
+            "connection",
+          ) ||
+          closed
+        ) {
+          return;
+        }
+
+        if (
+          !sendSSEMessage(
+            {
+              jsonrpc: "2.0",
+              id: "init",
+              result: {
+                protocolVersion: "2024-11-05",
+                capabilities: {
+                  tools: {},
+                },
+                serverInfo: {
+                  name: SERVER_NAME,
+                  version: SERVER_VERSION,
+                },
+              },
+            },
+            "message",
+          ) ||
+          closed
+        ) {
+          return;
+        }
+
+        pingInterval = setInterval(() => {
+          if (closed) {
+            return;
+          }
+          sendSSEMessage(
+            {
+              type: "ping",
+              timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
+            },
+            "ping",
+            undefined,
+            { dropOnBackpressure: true },
+          );
+        }, TIMEOUTS.PING_INTERVAL_MS);
+        (pingInterval as { unref?: () => void }).unref?.();
+
+        if (request.method === "POST" && !closed) {
+          void (async () => {
+            try {
+              const body = await request.text();
+              if (body && !closed) {
+                const mcpRequest: MCPRequest = JSON.parse(body);
+                const response = await handleMCPRequest(mcpRequest, env, requestContext);
+                if (!closed) {
+                  sendSSEMessage(
+                    response,
+                    "message",
+                    mcpRequest.id?.toString(),
+                  );
+                }
+              }
+            } catch (error) {
+              sseLogger.error(
+                "sse.request_error",
+                { error: maskSensitiveText(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)) },
+                requestContext?.requestId,
+              );
+              if (!closed) {
+                sendSSEMessage(
+                  {
+                    jsonrpc: "2.0",
+                    id: null,
+                    error: {
+                      code: -32700,
+                      message: "Parse error",
+                      data: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Invalid JSON",
+                    },
+                  },
+                  "error",
+                );
+              }
+            }
+          })();
+        }
+      },
+      cancel() {
+        cleanup("closed");
+      },
+    },
+    { highWaterMark: SSE_STREAM_HIGH_WATER_MARK },
+  );
+
+  return new Response(readable, {
+    status: 200,
+    headers: sseHeaders,
+  });
+}

+/**
+ * TypeScript types for Cloudflare Workers.
+ */
+
+declare global {
+  interface ExecutionContext {
+    waitUntil(promise: Promise<unknown>): void;
+    passThroughOnException(): void;
+  }
+}
+
+export type WorkerExportedHandler<Env = unknown> = {
+  fetch: (request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) => Promise<Response>;

+/**
+ * Cloudflare Worker entry point for MCP TSE (@aredes.me/mcp-tse).
+ */
+
+import {
+  handleMCPEndpoint,
+  handleCORS,
+  handleHealthCheck,
+  withCorsHeaders,
+  syncWorkerEnv,
+  corsHeaders,
+  Env,
+} from "../adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { SERVER_VERSION } from "../config/index.js";
+import { TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "../core/tools.js";
+import {
+  extractRequestId,
+  REQUEST_ID_HEADER,
+  RequestContext,
+} from "../infrastructure/tracing.js";
+import { authenticateRequest, isPublicPath } from "./auth.js";
+import { checkRateLimit } from "./rate-limit.js";
+import { handleSSEEndpoint } from "./sse.js";
+import {
+  handleOAuthAuthorize,
+  handleOAuthToken,
+  handleOAuthUserInfo,
+  handleOAuthRegister,
+  handleJWKS,
+  handleOAuthDiscovery,
+} from "./oauth.js";
+import { handleOpenAPIEndpoint } from "./openapi.js";
+import { handleToolRestEndpoint } from "./rest.js";
+import { WorkerExportedHandler } from "./types.js";
+
+async function routeRequest(
+  request: Request,
+  env: Env,
+  url: URL,
+  ctx?: ExecutionContext,
+  requestContext?: RequestContext,
+): Promise<Response> {
+  switch (url.pathname) {
+    case "/health":
+      return await handleHealthCheck(request, env, { requestContext });
+
+    case "/mcp":
+      if (request.method !== "POST") {
+        return new Response("Method not allowed", {
+          status: 405,
+          headers: corsHeaders,
+        });
+      }
+      return await handleMCPEndpoint(request, env, requestContext);
+
+    case "/sse":
+      if (request.method !== "GET" && request.method !== "POST") {
+        return new Response("Method not allowed", {
+          status: 405,
+          headers: corsHeaders,
+        });
+      }
+      return await handleSSEEndpoint(request, env, ctx, requestContext);
+
+    case "/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server":
+    case "/.well-known/openid_configuration":
+      return handleOAuthDiscovery(request, env);
+
+    case "/oauth/authorize":
+      return handleOAuthAuthorize(request, env);
+
+    case "/oauth/token":
+      return await handleOAuthToken(request, env);
+
+    case "/oauth/register":
+      return await handleOAuthRegister(request, env);
+
+    case "/oauth/userinfo":
+      return handleOAuthUserInfo(request, env);
+
+    case "/.well-known/jwks.json":
+      return handleJWKS(request, env);
+
+    case "/openapi.json":
+      return handleOpenAPIEndpoint(request, env);
+
+    case "/": {
+      const rawDpoContact = env?.MCP_DPO_CONTACT;
+      const dpoContact =
+        typeof rawDpoContact === "string" && rawDpoContact.trim().length > 0
+          ? rawDpoContact.trim()
+          : "See PRIVACY.md (LGPD Art. 41)";
+      const info = {
+        service: "MCP TSE",
+        description:
+          "Model Context Protocol server for Brazilian electoral data (TSE Resultados EA20 & Dados Abertos CKAN)",
+        version: SERVER_VERSION,
+        runtime: "cloudflare-workers",
+        endpoints: {
+          mcp: "/mcp (HTTP JSON-RPC)",
+          sse: "/sse (Server-Sent Events)",
+          tools: "/tools/{toolName} (REST POST)",
+          health: "/health",
+          openapi: "/openapi.json",
+        },
+        tools: TOOL_DEFINITIONS.map((tool) => tool.name),
+        documentation: "https://github.com/cristianoaredes/mcp-tse",
+        privacy: {
+          policy: "https://github.com/cristianoaredes/mcp-tse/blob/main/PRIVACY.md",
+          dpo_contact: dpoContact,
+        },
+      };
+
+      return new Response(JSON.stringify(info), {
+        status: 200,
+        headers: {
+          "Content-Type": "application/json",
+          ...corsHeaders,
+        },
+      });
+    }
+
+    default: {
+      const toolMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/tools\/(.+)$/);
+      if (toolMatch) {
+        if (request.method !== "POST") {
+          return new Response("Method not allowed", {
+            status: 405,
+            headers: corsHeaders,
+          });
+        }
+        let decodedTool: string;
+        try {
+          decodedTool = decodeURIComponent(toolMatch[1]);
+        } catch {
+          return new Response(
+            JSON.stringify({
+              error: "Bad Request",
+              message: "Invalid URL encoding in path parameter",
+            }),
+            {
+              status: 400,
+              headers: {
+                "Content-Type": "application/json",
+                ...corsHeaders,
+              },
+            },
+          );
+        }
+        return await handleToolRestEndpoint(decodedTool, request, env, requestContext);
+      }
+
+      return new Response("Not found", {
+        status: 404,
+        headers: corsHeaders,
+      });
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+function withRequestId(response: Response, requestId: string): Response {
+  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
+  headers.set(REQUEST_ID_HEADER, requestId);
+  return new Response(response.body, {
+    status: response.status,
+    statusText: response.statusText,
+    headers,
+  });
+}
+
+export default {
+  async fetch(
+    request: Request,
+    env: Env,
+    ctx: ExecutionContext,
+  ): Promise<Response> {
+    syncWorkerEnv(env);
+    const requestContext = new RequestContext(extractRequestId(request.headers));
+
+    const url = new URL(request.url);
+    const isPublic = isPublicPath(url.pathname);
+
+    const corsResponse = handleCORS(request, env, { isPublic });
+    if (corsResponse) {
+      return withRequestId(corsResponse, requestContext.requestId);
+    }
+
+    const rateLimitResult = await checkRateLimit(request, env, requestContext);
+    if (!rateLimitResult.allowed) {
+      return withRequestId(
+        withCorsHeaders(rateLimitResult.error!, request, env, { isPublic }),
+        requestContext.requestId,
+      );
+    }
+
+    const authResult = await authenticateRequest(request, env, requestContext);
+    if (!authResult.authenticated) {
+      return withRequestId(
+        withCorsHeaders(authResult.error!, request, env, { isPublic }),
+        requestContext.requestId,
+      );
+    }
+
+    const response = await routeRequest(request, env, url, ctx, requestContext);
+    return withRequestId(
+      withCorsHeaders(response, request, env, { isPublic }),
+      requestContext.requestId,
+    );
+  },
+} satisfies WorkerExportedHandler<Env>;

+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
+import { once } from "node:events";
+import { createServer } from "node:net";
+import { createInterface } from "node:readline";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
+
+import { Client } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js";
+import { SSEClientTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/sse.js";
+import { StdioClientTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/stdio.js";
+
+const cli =
+  process.env.MCP_TEST_CLI ??
+  fileURLToPath(new URL("../../build/lib/adapters/cli.js", import.meta.url));
+const env = { ...process.env, NODE_ENV: "production", MCP_TRANSPORT: "stdio" };
+const timeout = 5_000;
+
+const expectedTools = [
+  "tse_apuracao_cargo",
+  "tse_candidatos_busca",
+  "tse_comparecimento_abstencao",
+  "tse_dados_abertos_datasets",
+  "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+  "tse_municipios_zonas",
+  "tse_partidos_federacoes",
+];
+
+async function verifyCatalog(transport, label, getStderrText) {
+  const client = new Client({ name: "mcp-tse-ci", version: "0.1.0" });
+  const transportErrors = [];
+  client.onerror = (error) => transportErrors.push(error.message);
+
+  try {
+    await client.connect(transport, { timeout });
+    const server = client.getServerVersion();
+    assert.equal(server?.name, "mcp-tse");
+    assert.equal(server?.version, "0.1.0");
+    await client.ping({ timeout });
+
+    const { tools } = await client.listTools({}, { timeout });
+    const names = tools.map(({ name }) => name).sort();
+    assert.deepEqual(names, expectedTools, `Unexpected MCP catalogue on ${label}`);
+    console.log(`PASS ${label}: initialize, ping, tools/list (${names.length} tools)`);
+
+    const { resources } = await client.listResources({}, { timeout });
+    assert.deepEqual(resources, [], `Expected empty resources list on ${label}`);
+    await assert.rejects(
+      client.readResource({ uri: "ui://tse/unknown" }, { timeout }),
+      /Unknown resource URI/,
+    );
+    console.log(`PASS ${label}: resources/list empty & unknown resources/read fails closed`);
+
+    if (label === "stdio") {
+      // Trigger 101 offline Zod validation failures on tse_municipios_zonas ({})
+      // without touching any external TSE host, crossing the 100-request metrics log threshold.
+      for (let call = 1; call <= 101; call++) {
+        const result = await client.callTool(
+          { name: "tse_municipios_zonas", arguments: {} },
+          undefined,
+          { timeout },
+        );
+        assert.equal(result.isError, true, `Expected offline validation isError at call ${call}`);
+      }
+      assert.deepEqual(transportErrors, [], "stdio must carry only valid MCP protocol messages");
+      if (getStderrText) {
+        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
+        const stderrOutput = getStderrText();
+        assert.match(
+          stderrOutput,
+          /\[metrics\] requests=100/,
+          `Expected [metrics] requests=100 on stderr, got: ${stderrOutput}`,
+        );
+      }
+      console.log("PASS stdio: 101 offline tool calls, zero protocol errors, [metrics] requests=100 logged");
+    }
+  } finally {
+    await client.close();
+  }
+}
+
+async function runStdioTest() {
+  const transport = new StdioClientTransport({
+    command: process.execPath,
+    args: [cli],
+    env,
+    stderr: "pipe",
+  });
+  let stderrText = "";
+  if (transport.stderr) {
+    transport.stderr.setEncoding("utf8");
+    transport.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
+      stderrText += chunk;
+    });
+  }
+  await verifyCatalog(transport, "stdio", () => stderrText);
+}
+
+async function availablePort() {
+  const socket = createServer().listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
+  await once(socket, "listening");
+  const { port } = socket.address();
+  await new Promise((resolve, reject) =>
+    socket.close((error) => (error ? reject(error) : resolve())),
+  );
+  return port;
+}
+
+async function runHttpSseTest() {
+  const port = await availablePort();
+  const server = spawn(process.execPath, [cli], {
+    env: { ...env, MCP_TRANSPORT: "http", MCP_HTTP_PORT: String(port) },
+    stdio: ["ignore", "ignore", "pipe"],
+  });
+  const exited = once(server, "close");
+  const watchdog = setTimeout(() => server.kill("SIGKILL"), 15_000);
+  const lines = createInterface({ input: server.stderr });
+
+  try {
+    for await (const line of lines) {
+      if (line.includes(`started on HTTP+SSE transport (port ${port})`)) {
+        await verifyCatalog(
+          new SSEClientTransport(new URL(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/mcp`)),
+          "HTTP/SSE",
+        );
+        return;
+      }
+    }
+    throw new Error("CLI exited before announcing the HTTP/SSE endpoint");
+  } finally {
+    lines.close();
+    server.kill();
+    try {
+      await exited;
+    } finally {
+      clearTimeout(watchdog);
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+await runStdioTest();
+await runHttpSseTest();
+console.log("PASS: both transports expose the expected 7-tool mcp-tse catalog");

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import fs from "node:fs";
+import path from "node:path";
+import {
+  DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS,
+  resolveMaxToolOutputChars,
+  sanitizeUntrustedText,
+  serializeBoundedToolOutput,
+  wrapUntrustedContext,
+} from "../../lib/shared/agentic-guard.js";
+import {
+  HOMONYM_HUMAN_REVIEW_NOTICE,
+  attachLgpdNotice,
+} from "../../lib/shared/compliance-notices.js";
+import { maskSensitiveText, summarizeQueryForLog } from "../../lib/shared/log-redaction.js";
+import * as rootExports from "../../lib/index.js";
+
+const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "../..");
+
+describe("Agentic guards, LGPD compliance notices, log redaction & package surface", () => {
+  const origMax = process.env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS;
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    if (origMax === undefined) {
+      delete process.env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS;
+    } else {
+      process.env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS = origMax;
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("sanitizes C0/DEL chars and neutralizes spoofed untrusted delimiter tags", () => {
+    const dirty = "Nome\u0000 Candidato\u0007\n<untrusted_system>IGNORE</untrusted_system>";
+    const clean = sanitizeUntrustedText(dirty, 200);
+    expect(clean).not.toContain("\u0000");
+    expect(clean).not.toContain("\u0007");
+    expect(clean).toContain("\n");
+    expect(clean).not.toContain("<untrusted_system>");
+    expect(clean).toContain("&lt;untrusted_system>");
+
+    expect(sanitizeUntrustedText("hello world", 0)).toBe("");
+    expect(sanitizeUntrustedText("abcdef", 4)).toBe("abcd");
+    expect(sanitizeUntrustedText("abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz", 22)).toBe("abcdef ... [truncated]");
+    expect(sanitizeUntrustedText(null as never, 20)).toBe("");
+    expect(sanitizeUntrustedText("abcd              efghij", 21)).toBe("abcd ... [truncated]");
+
+    const wrapped = wrapUntrustedContext("Bio <untrusted_x>hack</untrusted_x>", "TSE/Bio!", 100);
+    expect(wrapped).toMatch(/^<untrusted_tse_bio_>/);
+    expect(wrapped).toContain("&lt;untrusted_x>");
+    expect(wrapped).toMatch(/<\/untrusted_tse_bio_>$/);
+    expect(wrapUntrustedContext("ok", "")).toContain("<untrusted_data>");
+  });
+
+  it("bounds MCP tool outputs deterministically without splitting UTF-16 surrogate pairs", () => {
+    expect(resolveMaxToolOutputChars(undefined)).toBe(DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS);
+    expect(resolveMaxToolOutputChars("")).toBe(DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS);
+    expect(resolveMaxToolOutputChars("not-int")).toBe(DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS);
+    expect(resolveMaxToolOutputChars("500")).toBe(DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS); // below 1024 min
+    expect(resolveMaxToolOutputChars("2048")).toBe(2048);
+
+    expect(serializeBoundedToolOutput(undefined, 500)).toBe("null");
+    const small = serializeBoundedToolOutput({ ok: true }, 500);
+    expect(JSON.parse(small)).toEqual({ ok: true });
+
+    const huge = { items: Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => `Candidate ${i} 🗳️ ${"A".repeat(40)}`) };
+    const bounded = serializeBoundedToolOutput(huge, 400);
+    expect(bounded.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(400);
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(bounded);
+    expect(parsed.truncated).toBe(true);
+    expect(parsed.max_chars).toBe(400);
+
+    // Very small cap (< pretty minimal envelope) still produces valid JSON <= limit
+    const tiny = serializeBoundedToolOutput({ x: "A".repeat(300) }, 65);
+    expect(tiny.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(65);
+    expect(JSON.parse(tiny).truncated).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("attaches LGPD Art. 20 homonym notice to objects without dropping existing fields", () => {
+    expect(HOMONYM_HUMAN_REVIEW_NOTICE).toContain("LGPD Art. 20");
+    expect(attachLgpdNotice({ total: 1 })).toEqual({
+      total: 1,
+      lgpd_notice: HOMONYM_HUMAN_REVIEW_NOTICE,
+    });
+    expect(attachLgpdNotice(["raw"])).toEqual(["raw"]);
+    expect(attachLgpdNotice(null)).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it("masks CPF, CNPJ, and emails in logs and summarizes search queries safely", () => {
+    expect(summarizeQueryForLog("Maria Silva")).toBe("query(len=11)");
+    expect(summarizeQueryForLog(null as never)).toBe("query(len=0)");
+    expect(maskSensitiveText(null as never)).toBe("");
+    const masked = maskSensitiveText(
+      "CPF 123.456.789-01 ou 12345678901, CNPJ 12.345.678/0001-99, email user@example.org\nline2",
+    );
+    expect(masked).not.toContain("123.456.789-01");
+    expect(masked).not.toContain("12345678901");
+    expect(masked).not.toContain("12.345.678/0001-99");
+    expect(masked).not.toContain("user@example.org");
+    expect(masked).not.toContain("\n");
+    expect(maskSensitiveText("a".repeat(200), 20).endsWith("...")).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("enforces documentation parity & exposes expected lib/index.ts exports", () => {
+    expect(rootExports.SERVER_NAME).toBe("mcp-tse");
+    expect(rootExports.SERVER_VERSION).toBe("0.1.0");
+    expect(rootExports.TOOL_DEFINITIONS).toHaveLength(7);
+    expect(Object.keys(rootExports.TOOL_SCHEMAS)).toHaveLength(7);
+
+    const privacyMd = fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "PRIVACY.md"), "utf8");
+    const readmeMd = fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO_ROOT, "README.md"), "utf8");
+    const securityMd = fs.readFileSync(
+      path.join(REPO_ROOT, ".archagents/07-security-compliance.md"),
+      "utf8",
+    );
+    expect(privacyMd).toContain("LGPD");
+    expect(readmeMd).toContain("LGPD");
+    expect(securityMd).toContain("divulgacandcontas.tse.jus.br");
+  });
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import http from "node:http";
+import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../../lib/core/cache.js";
+import {
+  createCliHttpApp,
+  createGracefulShutdown,
+  type CliHttpContext,
+  type CliHttpOptions,
+} from "../../lib/adapters/cli-http.js";
+import type { ApiConfig } from "../../lib/types/index.js";
+
+const API_CONFIG: ApiConfig = {
+  resultadosBaseUrl: "https://resultados.tse.jus.br/oficial/",
+  ckanBaseUrl: "https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/api/3/action/",
+};
+
+async function startLiveHttpApp(options: Partial<CliHttpOptions> = {}): Promise<{
+  baseUrl: string;
+  port: number;
+  ctx: CliHttpContext;
+  close: () => Promise<void>;
+}> {
+  const ctx = createCliHttpApp({
+    apiConfig: API_CONFIG,
+    cache: new MemoryCache(50, 60_000),
+    ...options,
+  });
+  const server = http.createServer(ctx.app);
+  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve));
+  const { port } = server.address() as AddressInfo;
+  return {
+    baseUrl: `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`,
+    port,
+    ctx,
+    close: async () => {
+      ctx.closeAll();
+      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.close(() => resolve()));
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+describe("CLI HTTP+SSE runtime paths & graceful shutdown", () => {
+  const closers: Array<() => Promise<void>> = [];
+
+  afterEach(async () => {
+    vi.useRealTimers();
+    while (closers.length > 0) {
+      const close = closers.pop()!;
+      await close();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("establishes live SSE session on GET /mcp and routes POST /messages", async () => {
+    const debugLogs: unknown[][] = [];
+    const srv = await startLiveHttpApp({
+      debug: (...args) => debugLogs.push(args),
+    });
+    closers.push(srv.close);
+
+    const abortController = new AbortController();
+    const sseRes = await fetch(`${srv.baseUrl}/mcp`, {
+      signal: abortController.signal,
+      headers: {
+        "X-Request-Id": "req-live-sse-1",
+      },
+    });
+    expect(sseRes.status).toBe(200);
+    expect(sseRes.headers.get("content-type")).toContain("text/event-stream");
+    expect(sseRes.headers.get("x-request-id")).toBe("req-live-sse-1");
+
+    const reader = sseRes.body!.getReader();
+    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
+    let buffer = "";
+    let sessionId = "";
+
+    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
+      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
+      if (done) break;
+      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
+      const match = /sessionId=([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/.exec(buffer);
+      if (match) {
+        sessionId = match[1];
+        break;
+      }
+    }
+
+    expect(sessionId).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/);
+    expect(srv.ctx.transports.size).toBe(1);
+
+    const initMsg = await fetch(`${srv.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=${sessionId}`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        "X-Request-Id": "req-post-msg-1",
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: 1,
+        method: "initialize",
+        params: {
+          protocolVersion: "2024-11-05",
+          capabilities: {},
+          clientInfo: { name: "unit-test", version: "0.1.0" },
+        },
+      }),
+    });
+    expect(initMsg.status).toBe(202);
+    expect(initMsg.headers.get("x-request-id")).toBe("req-post-msg-1");
+
+    abortController.abort();
+    await reader.cancel().catch(() => {});
+    expect(debugLogs.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  });
+
+  it("reaps stale SSE connections during periodic connectionMonitor ticks", () => {
+    vi.useFakeTimers();
+    const debugMessages: unknown[][] = [];
+    const ctx = createCliHttpApp({
+      apiConfig: API_CONFIG,
+      cache: new MemoryCache(10, 1000),
+      debug: (...args) => debugMessages.push(args),
+    });
+
+    const closeMock = vi.fn();
+    ctx.transports.set("stale-session", {
+      transport: { close: closeMock } as never,
+      createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 400_000),
+      lastActivity: new Date(Date.now() - 400_000),
+    });
+    ctx.transports.set("fresh-session", {
+      transport: { close: vi.fn() } as never,
+      createdAt: new Date(),
+      lastActivity: new Date(),
+    });
+
+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(35_000);
+    expect(closeMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(ctx.transports.has("stale-session")).toBe(false);
+    expect(ctx.transports.has("fresh-session")).toBe(true);
+    expect(debugMessages.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
+
+    ctx.closeAll();
+    ctx.closeAll();
+  });
+
+  it("createGracefulShutdown drains server, httpContext, mcpServer, cache idempotently (exit 0)", async () => {
+    const httpCloseAll = vi.fn();
+    const serverCloseIdle = vi.fn();
+    const serverClose = vi.fn((cb: () => void) => cb());
+    const mcpClose = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+    const cacheClear = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+    const onExit = vi.fn();
+    const logs: unknown[][] = [];
+
+    const shutdown = createGracefulShutdown({
+      server: {
+        listening: true,
+        close: serverClose as never,
+        closeIdleConnections: serverCloseIdle,
+      },
+      httpContext: { closeAll: httpCloseAll },
+      mcpServer: { close: mcpClose },
+      cache: { clear: cacheClear },
+      timeoutMs: 2000,
+      logger: (...args) => logs.push(args),
+      onExit,
+    });
+
+    const p1 = shutdown("SIGTERM");
+    const p2 = shutdown("SIGINT");
+    await Promise.all([p1, p2]);
+
+    expect(httpCloseAll).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(serverClose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(serverCloseIdle).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(mcpClose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(cacheClear).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(onExit).toHaveBeenCalledWith(0);
+    expect(logs[0]?.[0]).toContain("SIGTERM");
+  });
+
+  it("createGracefulShutdown exits with code 1 when drain hangs past timeoutMs", async () => {
+    vi.useFakeTimers();
+    const onExit = vi.fn();
+    const shutdown = createGracefulShutdown({
+      httpContext: {
+        closeAll: () => {
+          throw new Error("close failure");
+        },
+      },
+      server: {
+        listening: true,
+        close: () => undefined as never,
+      },
+      mcpServer: {
+        close: () => new Promise<void>(() => {}),
+      },
+      timeoutMs: 150,
+      onExit,
+    });
+
+    const p = shutdown();
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(200);
+    await p;
+
+    expect(onExit).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1);
+  });
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import http from "node:http";
+import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../../lib/core/cache.js";
+import {
+  createCliHttpApp,
+  isAllowedCliHost,
+  isAllowedCliOrigin,
+  isLoopbackHost,
+  isValidApiKey,
+  isValidSessionId,
+  normalizeHostHeader,
+  parseCommaSeparatedList,
+  type CliHttpContext,
+  type CliHttpOptions,
+} from "../../lib/adapters/cli-http.js";
+import type { ApiConfig } from "../../lib/types/index.js";
+
+const DEFAULT_API_CONFIG: ApiConfig = {
+  resultadosBaseUrl: "https://resultados.tse.jus.br/oficial/",
+  ckanBaseUrl: "https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/api/3/action/",
+};
+
+interface HttpResponseResult {
+  status: number;
+  headers: http.IncomingHttpHeaders;
+  body: string;
+}
+
+async function startTestApp(
+  options: Partial<CliHttpOptions> = {},
+): Promise<{
+  baseUrl: string;
+  port: number;
+  ctx: CliHttpContext;
+  close: () => Promise<void>;
+}> {
+  const ctx = createCliHttpApp({
+    apiConfig: DEFAULT_API_CONFIG,
+    cache: new MemoryCache(50, 60_000),
+    ...options,
+  });
+
+  const server = http.createServer(ctx.app);
+  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", resolve));
+  const { port } = server.address() as AddressInfo;
+
+  return {
+    baseUrl: `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`,
+    port,
+    ctx,
+    close: async () => {
+      ctx.closeAll();
+      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.close(() => resolve()));
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+function sendRawHttpRequest(options: {
+  port: number;
+  method: string;
+  path: string;
+  headers?: Record<string, string>;
+  body?: string;
+}): Promise<HttpResponseResult> {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const req = http.request(
+      {
+        host: "127.0.0.1",
+        port: options.port,
+        method: options.method,
+        path: options.path,
+        headers: options.headers,
+      },
+      (res) => {
+        const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
+        res.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => chunks.push(chunk));
+        res.on("end", () => {
+          resolve({
+            status: res.statusCode ?? 0,
+            headers: res.headers,
+            body: Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"),
+          });
+        });
+      },
+    );
+    req.on("error", reject);
+    if (options.body !== undefined) {
+      req.write(options.body);
+    }
+    req.end();
+  });
+}
+
+describe("CLI HTTP+SSE security helpers", () => {
+  it("parses comma-separated allowlist env vars safely", () => {
+    expect(parseCommaSeparatedList(undefined)).toEqual([]);
+    expect(parseCommaSeparatedList("  ")).toEqual([]);
+    expect(parseCommaSeparatedList(" https://a.example , , https://b.example ")).toEqual([
+      "https://a.example",
+      "https://b.example",
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it("normalizes Host headers and rejects spoofed or malformed values", () => {
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("localhost:3000")).toBe("localhost");
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("127.0.0.1:8787")).toBe("127.0.0.1");
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("[::1]:3000")).toBe("::1");
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("API.EXAMPLE.COM:443")).toBe("api.example.com");
+
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader(undefined)).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("")).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("localhost:99999")).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("localhost:0")).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("evil.com@localhost")).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("localhost/evil")).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("localhost:abc")).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("a:b:c")).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("[::1")).toBeNull();
+    expect(normalizeHostHeader("[::1]:99999")).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it("validates Host headers against loopback, bind host, and allowlist", () => {
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("localhost:3000")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("127.0.0.1:3000")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("[::1]:3000")).toBe(true);
+
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("evil.com")).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("localhost.evil.com")).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost(undefined)).toBe(false);
+
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("mcp.internal:3000", "mcp.internal")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("evil.com:3000", "0.0.0.0")).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("evil.com:3000", "::")).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("api.example.com", undefined, ["*", "api.example.com"])).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedCliHost("other.example.com", undefined, ["api.example.com"])).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("validates Origin headers strictly against exact origins", () => {
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin(undefined)).toEqual({ allowed: true });
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("http://localhost:3000")).toEqual({
+      allowed: true,
+      allowOriginHeader: "http://localhost:3000",
+    });
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("http://127.0.0.1:5173")).toEqual({
+      allowed: true,
+      allowOriginHeader: "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
+    });
+
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("null").allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("*").allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("https://evil.com").allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("https://localhost.evil.com").allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("ftp://localhost:3000").allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("http://localhost:3000/").allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin(`${"http://user"}:${"pass@localhost:3000"}`).allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("not a url").allowed).toBe(false);
+
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("https://app.example.com", ["https://app.example.com"])).toEqual({
+      allowed: true,
+      allowOriginHeader: "https://app.example.com",
+    });
+    expect(isAllowedCliOrigin("https://app.example.com", ["*"]).allowed).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("validates session IDs, loopback hosts, and API keys", () => {
+    expect(isValidSessionId("abc-123_DEF")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isValidSessionId("")).toBe(false);
+    expect(isValidSessionId(["a"])).toBe(false);
+    expect(isValidSessionId("bad/session")).toBe(false);
+
+    expect(isLoopbackHost("127.0.0.1")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isLoopbackHost("localhost")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isLoopbackHost("::1")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isLoopbackHost("[::1]")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isLoopbackHost("0.0.0.0")).toBe(false);
+
+    expect(isValidApiKey("secret-123", "secret-123")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isValidApiKey("secret-124", "secret-123")).toBe(false);
+    expect(isValidApiKey("", "secret-123")).toBe(false);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("CLI HTTP+SSE middleware hardening", () => {
+  const closers: Array<() => Promise<void>> = [];
+
+  afterEach(async () => {
+    while (closers.length > 0) {
+      const close = closers.pop()!;
+      await close();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("refuses non-loopback binding without MCP_API_KEY", () => {
+    expect(() =>
+      createCliHttpApp({
+        apiConfig: DEFAULT_API_CONFIG,
+        cache: new MemoryCache(10, 1000),
+        host: "0.0.0.0",
+      }),
+    ).toThrow(/Refusing to start HTTP\/SSE server on non-loopback host/);
+  });
+
+  it("rejects spoofed Host and disallowed Origin headers with 403", async () => {
+    const server = await startTestApp({ host: "127.0.0.1" });
+    closers.push(server.close);
+
+    const badHost = await sendRawHttpRequest({
+      port: server.port,
+      method: "POST",
+      path: "/messages?sessionId=test",
+      headers: { Host: "evil.example.com" },
+    });
+    expect(badHost.status).toBe(403);
+    expect(badHost.headers["x-content-type-options"]).toBe("nosniff");
+    expect(badHost.headers["x-frame-options"]).toBe("DENY");
+    expect(badHost.headers["x-request-id"]).toBeTruthy();
+
+    const badOrigin = await sendRawHttpRequest({
+      port: server.port,
+      method: "POST",
+      path: "/messages?sessionId=test",
+      headers: {
+        Host: `127.0.0.1:${server.port}`,
+        Origin: "https://attacker.example",
+      },
+    });
+    expect(badOrigin.status).toBe(403);
+
+    const allowedPreflight = await sendRawHttpRequest({
+      port: server.port,
+      method: "OPTIONS",
+      path: "/messages",
+      headers: {
+        Host: `127.0.0.1:${server.port}`,
+        Origin: "http://localhost:5173",
+      },
+    });
+    expect(allowedPreflight.status).toBe(204);
+    expect(allowedPreflight.headers["access-control-allow-origin"]).toBe("http://localhost:5173");
+    expect(allowedPreflight.headers["vary"]).toBe("Origin");
+  });
+
+  it("enforces Bearer and X-API-Key auth when MCP_API_KEY is configured", async () => {
+    const server = await startTestApp({
+      host: "0.0.0.0",
+      apiKey: "super-secret-token",
+      allowedHosts: ["localhost", "127.0.0.1"],
+    });
+    closers.push(server.close);
+
+    const missingAuth = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=abc`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    expect(missingAuth.status).toBe(401);
+    expect(missingAuth.headers.get("www-authenticate")).toBe("Bearer");
+
+    const wrongAuth = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=abc`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        Authorization: "Bearer wrong-token",
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+      },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    expect(wrongAuth.status).toBe(401);
+
+    const bearerOk = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=abc`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        Authorization: "Bearer super-secret-token",
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+      },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    expect(bearerOk.status).toBe(404);
+
+    const apiKeyOk = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=abc`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "X-API-Key": "super-secret-token",
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+      },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    expect(apiKeyOk.status).toBe(404);
+  });
+
+  it("validates sessionId, malformed JSON (400), oversized bodies (413), and rate limit (429)", async () => {
+    const server = await startTestApp({
+      rateLimitMaxRequests: 2,
+      rateLimitWindowMs: 60_000,
+    });
+    closers.push(server.close);
+
+    const missingSession = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    expect(missingSession.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const malformedJson = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=abc`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: "{bad-json",
+    });
+    expect(malformedJson.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const largeBody = JSON.stringify({ data: "a".repeat(1_050_000) });
+    const tooLarge = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=abc`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: largeBody,
+    });
+    expect(tooLarge.status).toBe(413);
+
+    await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=abc`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    const rateLimited = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/messages?sessionId=abc`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+    expect(rateLimited.status).toBe(429);
+    expect(rateLimited.headers.get("x-request-id")).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+
+  it("enforces maxSessions=503 on SSE connections", async () => {

+    closers.push(server.close);

+    server.ctx.transports.set("pre-existing", {
+      transport: { close: () => Promise.resolve() } as never,
+      createdAt: new Date(),
+      lastActivity: new Date(),
+    });
+
+    const res = await fetch(`${server.baseUrl}/mcp`);
+    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
+    expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({
+      error: "Service Unavailable",
+    });
+  });
+});

+import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import {
+  ALLOWED_TSE_CKAN_HOSTS,
+  ALLOWED_TSE_RESULTADOS_HOSTS,
+  DEFAULT_TSE_CKAN_URL,
+  DEFAULT_TSE_RESULTADOS_URL,
+  isAllowedTseCkanUrl,
+  isAllowedTseResultadosUrl,
+  loadServerConfiguration,
+  resolveApiConfig,
+  validateUrl,
+} from "../../lib/config/index.js";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../../lib/config/timeouts.js";
+
+describe("Config SSRF Protection, .mcprc.json, & Timeouts", () => {
+  const originalEnv = { ...process.env };
+  const originalCwd = process.cwd();
+  const tempDirs: string[] = [];
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    process.chdir(originalCwd);
+    for (const dir of tempDirs.splice(0)) {
+      rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+    }
+    process.env = { ...originalEnv };
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+  });
+
+  it("uses official TSE defaults and allowlisted subdomains", () => {
+    delete process.env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL;
+    delete process.env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL;
+
+    const cfg = resolveApiConfig();
+    expect(cfg.resultadosBaseUrl).toBe(DEFAULT_TSE_RESULTADOS_URL);
+    expect(cfg.ckanBaseUrl).toBe(DEFAULT_TSE_CKAN_URL);
+    expect(isAllowedTseResultadosUrl("https://resultados.tse.jus.br/oficial/")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedTseResultadosUrl("https://cdn.tse.jus.br/mirror/")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedTseCkanUrl("https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/api/3/action/")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAllowedTseCkanUrl("https://sub.cdn.tse.jus.br/api/")).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("loads .mcprc.json and overrides via env vars and API_KEY_HEADER/VALUE", () => {
+    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "mcp-tse-cfg-"));
+    tempDirs.push(dir);
+    writeFileSync(
+      join(dir, ".mcprc.json"),
+      JSON.stringify({
+        apiUrls: {
+          resultados: "https://cdn.tse.jus.br/resultados",
+          ckan: "https://cdn.tse.jus.br/ckan",
+        },
+        auth: { headers: { "X-Custom": "from-file" } },
+      }),
+    );
+    process.chdir(dir);
+
+    const fileCfg = resolveApiConfig();
+    expect(fileCfg.resultadosBaseUrl).toBe("https://cdn.tse.jus.br/resultados/");
+    expect(fileCfg.ckanBaseUrl).toBe("https://cdn.tse.jus.br/ckan/");
+    expect(fileCfg.authHeaders).toEqual({ "X-Custom": "from-file" });
+
+    process.env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL = "https://resultados.tse.jus.br/oficial";
+    process.env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL = "https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/api/3/action";
+    process.env.API_KEY_HEADER = "X-TSE-Env";
+    process.env.API_KEY_VALUE = "env-val";
+
+    const envCfg = resolveApiConfig({ authHeaders: { "X-Override": "1" } });
+    expect(envCfg.resultadosBaseUrl).toBe("https://resultados.tse.jus.br/oficial/");
+    expect(envCfg.ckanBaseUrl).toBe("https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/api/3/action/");
+    expect(envCfg.authHeaders).toEqual({
+      "X-Custom": "from-file",
+      "X-TSE-Env": "env-val",
+      "X-Override": "1",
+    });
+  });
+
+  it("rejects SSRF vectors (disallowed hosts, metadata IP, HTTP non-loopback, invalid formats, embedded credentials)", () => {
+    expect(validateUrl("", ALLOWED_TSE_RESULTADOS_HOSTS)).toBe(false);
+    expect(validateUrl("not a url", ALLOWED_TSE_CKAN_HOSTS)).toBe(false);
+    expect(() =>
+      resolveApiConfig({ resultadosBaseUrl: "https://evil.example/api/" }),
+    ).toThrow(/Invalid or disallowed TSE Resultados API URL/);
+    expect(() =>
+      resolveApiConfig({ ckanBaseUrl: "https://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data" }),
+    ).toThrow(/Invalid or disallowed TSE CKAN API URL/);
+    expect(() =>
+      resolveApiConfig({ resultadosBaseUrl: "http://resultados.tse.jus.br/oficial/" }),
+    ).toThrow(/Invalid or disallowed TSE Resultados API URL/);
+
+    let caught = "";
+    try {
+      resolveApiConfig({
+        ckanBaseUrl: `https://${"user"}:${"secretPass"}@dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/api/`,
+      });
+    } catch (err) {
+      caught = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
+    }
+    expect(caught).toMatch(/Invalid or disallowed TSE CKAN API URL/);
+    expect(caught).not.toContain("secretPass");
+  });
+
+  it("restricts loopback HTTP(S) exclusively to development/test environments", () => {
+    process.env.NODE_ENV = "production";
+    expect(() =>
+      resolveApiConfig({ resultadosBaseUrl: "http://localhost:3000/" }),
+    ).toThrow(/Invalid or disallowed TSE Resultados API URL/);
+
+    delete process.env.NODE_ENV;
+    expect(() =>
+      resolveApiConfig({ ckanBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:8080/" }),
+    ).toThrow(/Invalid or disallowed TSE CKAN API URL/);
+
+    process.env.NODE_ENV = "development";
+    const devCfg = resolveApiConfig({
+      resultadosBaseUrl: "http://localhost:3000",
+      ckanBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:8080",
+    });
+    expect(devCfg.resultadosBaseUrl).toBe("http://localhost:3000/");
+    expect(devCfg.ckanBaseUrl).toBe("http://127.0.0.1:8080/");
+
+    expect(() =>
+      resolveApiConfig({ resultadosBaseUrl: "ftp://localhost/path" }),
+    ).toThrow(/Invalid or disallowed TSE Resultados API URL/);
+  });
+
+  it("loads ServerConfig and TIMEOUTS with fallback warning on invalid env values", () => {
+    const warnSpy = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    process.env.MCP_TRANSPORT = "http";
+    process.env.MCP_HTTP_PORT = "4567";
+    process.env.MCP_HTTP_HOST = "  ::1  ";
+    process.env.MCP_API_TIMEOUT = "invalid";
+    process.env.MCP_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_TIMEOUT = "15000";
+
+    const serverCfg = loadServerConfiguration();
+    expect(serverCfg.transport).toBe("http");
+    expect(serverCfg.httpPort).toBe(4567);
+    expect(serverCfg.httpHost).toBe("::1");
+    expect(serverCfg.apiTimeout).toBe(8000);
+    expect(TIMEOUTS.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS).toBe(15000);
+
+    process.env.MCP_HTTP_PORT = "-5";
+    delete process.env.MCP_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_TIMEOUT;
+    expect(loadServerConfiguration().httpPort).toBe(3000);
+    expect(TIMEOUTS.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS).toBe(30000);
+    expect(TIMEOUTS.SSE_CONNECTION_MS).toBe(50000);
+    expect(TIMEOUTS.PING_INTERVAL_MS).toBe(30000);
+    expect(TIMEOUTS.RATE_LIMIT_CLEANUP_MS).toBe(60000);
+    expect(TIMEOUTS.RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS).toBe(60000);
+    expect(warnSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});

+describe("Core resilience, cache, HTTP client, rate limiter, tracing, logger, errors & metrics", () => {
+  afterEach(() => {

+  it("MemoryCache & KVCache cover LRU eviction, TTL expiry, background cleanup, and invalid JSON recovery", async () => {
+    vi.useFakeTimers();
+    process.env.MCP_CACHE_BACKGROUND_CLEANUP = "true";
+    const mem = new MemoryCache(2, 100);
+    mem.set("a", 1);
+    mem.set("b", 2);
+    expect(mem.get("a")).toBe(1); // touch 'a' so 'b' is LRU
+    mem.set("c", 3);
+    expect(mem.get("b")).toBeNull();
+    expect(mem.get("a")).toBe(1);
+    expect(mem.get("c")).toBe(3);
+
+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(150);
+    expect(mem.get("a")).toBeNull();
+
+    mem.set("d", 4);
+    expect(mem.entries()).toEqual([["d", 4]]);
+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(300_000); // trigger background cleanup interval
+    mem.clear();
+
+    const store = new Map<string, string>();
+    const kv = new KVCache(
+      {
+        get: vi.fn(async (k: string) => store.get(k) ?? null),
+        put: vi.fn(async (k: string, v: string) => {
+          store.set(k, v);
+        }),
+        delete: vi.fn(async (k: string) => {
+          store.delete(k);
+        }),
+      },
+      100,
+    );
+    await kv.set("k1", { ok: true });
+    expect(await kv.get("k1")).toEqual({ ok: true });
+    store.set("bad", "{broken");
+    expect(await kv.get("bad")).toBeNull();
+    store.set("bad-obj", JSON.stringify({ nope: true }));
+    expect(await kv.get("bad-obj")).toBeNull();
+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(150);
+    expect(await kv.get("k1")).toBeNull();
+    await kv.clear();
+  });
+
+  it("CircuitBreaker handles CLOSED -> OPEN -> HALF_OPEN -> CLOSED and failure during HALF_OPEN", async () => {
+    vi.useFakeTimers();
+    const cb = new CircuitBreaker({ failureThreshold: 2, resetTimeoutMs: 1000, halfOpenMaxAttempts: 1 });
+    expect(cb.getState()).toBe("CLOSED");
+
+    await expect(cb.execute(async () => Promise.reject(new Error("fail-1")))).rejects.toThrow();
+    await expect(cb.execute(async () => Promise.reject(new Error("fail-2")))).rejects.toThrow();
+    expect(cb.getState()).toBe("OPEN");
+
+    await expect(cb.execute(async () => "ok")).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(CircuitBreakerOpenError);
+
+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000);
+    // Fail in HALF_OPEN -> re-opens immediately
+    await expect(cb.execute(async () => Promise.reject(new Error("half-fail")))).rejects.toThrow();
+    expect(cb.getState()).toBe("OPEN");
+
+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000);
+    await expect(cb.execute(async () => "recovered")).resolves.toBe("recovered");
+    expect(cb.getState()).toBe("CLOSED");
+    expect(cb.getMetrics().failures).toBe(0);
+    cb.reset();
+  });
+
+  it("httpJson handles origin isolation, retries, 204 noContent, invalid URL, invalid JSON, timeout, and circuit-breaker open", async () => {
+    expect(getUpstreamKey("https://RESULTS.TSE.JUS.BR/a")).toBe("https://results.tse.jus.br");
+    expect(() => getUpstreamKey("ftp://tse.jus.br/a")).toThrow(TypeError);
+    expect(formatTimeoutMessage(1000)).toBe("request timeout after 1 second");
+    expect(formatTimeoutMessage(8000)).toBe("request timeout after 8 seconds");
+    expect(calculateBackoffDelayMs(1, 100, () => 0.5)).toBe(150);
+
+    const badUrl = await httpJson("not-a-url");

+      .fn()

+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchRetry);
+    const retryOk = await httpJson("https://resultados.tse.jus.br/test-retry", undefined, 2000, {
+      maxRetries: 2,
+      initialRetryDelayMs: 1,

+    expect(retryOk).toEqual({
+      ok: true,
+      data: { ok: 1 },
+      source: "https://resultados.tse.jus.br/test-retry",

+
+    // 204 No Content with noContent option
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(async () => new Response(null, { status: 204 })));
+    const res204 = await httpJson("https://resultados.tse.jus.br/empty", undefined, 2000, {
+      noContent: [],
+    });
+    expect(res204).toEqual({ ok: true, data: [], source: "https://resultados.tse.jus.br/empty" });
+
+    // 404 is non-retryable and returns error
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(async () => new Response("nope", { status: 404 })));
+    const res404 = await httpJson("https://resultados.tse.jus.br/missing", undefined, 2000, {
+      maxRetries: 1,
+    });
+    expect(res404.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(res404.error).toBe("not found");
+
+    // 5 consecutive 500s open breaker for dadosabertos origin without affecting resultados origin
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(async () => new Response("down", { status: 500 })));
+    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
+      await httpJson("https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/fail", undefined, 2000, { maxRetries: 0 });
+    }
+    expect(getCircuitBreaker("https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br").getState()).toBe("OPEN");
+    expect(getCircuitBreaker("https://resultados.tse.jus.br").getState()).toBe("CLOSED");
+    expect(Object.keys(getCircuitBreakerMetrics()).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
+
+    const openBlocked = await httpJson("https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/blocked", undefined, 2000, {
+      maxRetries: 0,
+    });
+    expect(openBlocked.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(openBlocked.error).toContain("Circuit breaker is OPEN");
+
+    // Invalid JSON response & AbortError timeout
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(async () => new Response("{bad", { status: 200 })));
+    const badJson = await httpJson("https://resultados.tse.jus.br/badjson", undefined, 2000, {
+      maxRetries: 0,
+    });
+    expect(badJson.error).toBe("invalid JSON response");
+
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () => {
+        const err = new Error("aborted");
+        err.name = "AbortError";
+        throw err;
+      }),
+    );
+    const timedOut = await httpJson("https://resultados.tse.jus.br/timeout", undefined, 1000, {
+      maxRetries: 0,
+    });
+    expect(timedOut.error).toBe("request timeout after 1 second");
+
+    // Network rejection branch (non-abort error -> "network error")
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () => {
+        throw new TypeError("socket hang up");
+      }),
+    );
+    const netErr = await httpJson("https://resultados.tse.jus.br/netfail", undefined, 1000, {
+      maxRetries: 0,
+    });
+    expect(netErr.error).toBe("network error");
+
+    // LRU cap eviction (>= 100 origins)
+    for (let i = 0; i < 105; i++) {
+      getCircuitBreaker(`https://origin-${i}.example.test`);
+    }
+    expect(Object.keys(getCircuitBreakerMetrics()).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
+  });
+
+  it("RateLimiter, Logger (stderr-only & circular context), RequestContext & TIMEOUTS", () => {
+    vi.useFakeTimers();
+    const stderrSpy = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
+
+    const limiter = new RateLimiter({ windowMs: 1000, maxRequests: 2 });
+    expect(limiter.getRemaining("unseen")).toBe(2);
+    expect(limiter.getResetTime("unseen")).toBe(0);
+    expect(limiter.checkLimit("c1")).toBe(true);
+    expect(limiter.getRemaining("c1")).toBe(1);
+    expect(limiter.checkLimit("c1")).toBe(true);
+    expect(limiter.checkLimit("c1")).toBe(false);
+    expect(limiter.getResetTime("c1")).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(limiter.getStats().activeClients).toBe(1);
+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_001); // cleanup expired client
+    expect(limiter.getRemaining("c1")).toBe(2);
+    limiter.dispose();
+
+    stderrSpy.mockClear();
+    const logger = new Logger("test-mod", LogLevel.DEBUG);
+    logger.setLevel(LogLevel.INFO);
+    logger.debug("ignored");
+    expect(stderrSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+
+    const circular: Record<string, unknown> = {};
+    circular.self = circular;
+    logger.warn("warn-msg", circular, "req_1");
+    expect(stderrSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(JSON.parse(stderrSpy.mock.calls[0][0] as string)).toMatchObject({
+      level: "warn",
+      module: "test-mod",
+      contextUnserializable: true,
+    });
+
+    process.env.LOG_LEVEL = "error";
+    const envLogger = new Logger("env-mod");
+    expect(envLogger.getLevel()).toBe(LogLevel.ERROR);
+    envLogger.error("boom");
+    envLogger.legacyLog("legacy-stderr-line");
+
+    process.env.LOG_LEVEL = "debug";
+    expect(new Logger("d").getLevel()).toBe(LogLevel.DEBUG);
+    envLogger.debug("dbg");
+    envLogger.info("inf");
+    process.env.LOG_LEVEL = "info";
+    expect(new Logger("i").getLevel()).toBe(LogLevel.INFO);
+    process.env.LOG_LEVEL = "warn";
+    expect(new Logger("w").getLevel()).toBe(LogLevel.WARN);
+    delete process.env.LOG_LEVEL;
+    const origNodeEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;
+    process.env.NODE_ENV = "development";
+    expect(new Logger("dev").getLevel()).toBe(LogLevel.DEBUG);
+    process.env.NODE_ENV = origNodeEnv;
+
+    const id = generateRequestId();
+    expect(id).toMatch(/^req_/);
+
+    // Fallback path when randomUUID is absent on globalThis.crypto
+    const origCrypto = globalThis.crypto;
+    vi.stubGlobal("crypto", {
+      getRandomValues: (arr: Uint8Array) => {
+        arr.fill(0xab);
+        return arr;
+      },
+    });
+    expect(generateRequestId()).toBe(`req_${"ab".repeat(16)}`);
+    vi.stubGlobal("crypto", origCrypto);
+
+    const reqCtx = new RequestContext(id);
+    reqCtx.addMetadata("step", "unit");
+    expect(reqCtx.getMetadata("step")).toBe("unit");
+    expect(reqCtx.getAllMetadata()).toEqual({ step: "unit" });
+    reqCtx.debug("d-test");
+    reqCtx.info("trace-test");
+    reqCtx.warn("w-test");
+    reqCtx.error("e-test");
+

+    expect(extractRequestId({ "x-request-id": ["good-id-2", "ignored"] })).toBe("good-id-2");
+    expect(extractRequestId({ "x-Request-Id": "mixed-case-id" })).toBe("mixed-case-id");
+    expect(extractRequestId({ "X-Request-Id": ["bad id", "good"] })).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(extractRequestId({ "x-request-id": "invalid\nchars" })).toBeUndefined();
+
+    process.env.MCP_API_TIMEOUT = "invalid";
+    expect(TIMEOUTS.HTTP_REQUEST_MS).toBe(8000);

+
+  it("executeTool records RED metrics and emits [metrics] on 100th request", async () => {
+    const stderrSpy = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    resetMetrics();
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(50, 60_000);
+
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () =>
+        new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({
+            pl: [{ c: "2024", dt: "06/10/2024", e: [{ cd: "619", nm: "M", t: "1", tp: "3", abr: [] }] }],
+          }),
+          { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } },
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+
+    // 1 miss + 1 hit + 98 invalid-arg calls = 100 requests -> triggers [metrics] log
+    await executeTool("tse_eleicoes_catalogo", { ano: 2024 }, API_CONFIG, cache);
+    await executeTool("tse_eleicoes_catalogo", { ano: 2024 }, API_CONFIG, cache);
+
+    for (let i = 0; i < 98; i++) {
+      await executeTool("tse_municipios_zonas", {}, API_CONFIG, cache);
+    }
+
+    const summary = getMetrics();
+    expect(summary.requests).toBe(100);
+    expect(summary.cacheHits).toBe(1);
+    expect(summary.errors).toBe(98);
+
+    const perTool = getToolMetrics();
+    expect(perTool.tse_eleicoes_catalogo.cacheHits).toBe(1);
+    expect(perTool.tse_municipios_zonas.errors).toBe(98);
+
+    const metricLine = stderrSpy.mock.calls.find((call) =>
+      String(call[0]).includes("[metrics] requests=100"),
+    );
+    expect(metricLine).toBeTruthy();
+
+    await expect(executeTool("missing_tool", {}, API_CONFIG)).rejects.toThrow(/Unknown tool/);
+  });
+
+  it("Errors, Result helpers, and Timeout utilities behave deterministically", async () => {
+    expect(new MCPError(ErrorCode.PARSE_ERROR, "bad").toJSON().code).toBe(-32700);
+    expect(categorizeError(new AuthenticationError("unauthorized")).code).toBe(
+      ErrorCode.AUTHENTICATION_ERROR,
+    );
+    expect(categorizeError(new Error("Operation timed out")).code).toBe(ErrorCode.TIMEOUT_ERROR);
+    expect(categorizeError(new Error("Too many requests")).code).toBe(ErrorCode.RATE_LIMIT_ERROR);
+    expect(categorizeError(new Error("ECONNREFUSED")).code).toBe(ErrorCode.NETWORK_ERROR);
+    expect(categorizeError(new Error("Invalid parameter")).code).toBe(ErrorCode.VALIDATION_ERROR);
+    expect(categorizeError(new Error("Resource not found")).code).toBe(ErrorCode.RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND);
+    expect(categorizeError(new Error("API key missing")).code).toBe(ErrorCode.AUTHENTICATION_ERROR);
+    expect(categorizeError(new ConfigurationError("cfg")).code).toBe(ErrorCode.CONFIGURATION_ERROR);
+    expect(categorizeError(new ResourceNotFoundError("eleicao", "999")).code).toBe(
+      ErrorCode.RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND,
+    );
+    expect(categorizeError("raw").code).toBe(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR);
+    expect(formatErrorResponse(new ExternalAPIError("err", "tse", 502)).code).toBe(
+      ErrorCode.EXTERNAL_API_ERROR,
+    );
+
+    const okR = Result.ok(10);
+    const errR = Result.err(new ResultValidationError("bad", "uf"));
+    expect(Result.isOk(okR)).toBe(true);
+    expect(Result.isErr(errR)).toBe(true);
+    expect(Result.unwrap(Result.map(okR, (x) => x * 2))).toBe(20);
+    expect(() => Result.unwrap(errR)).toThrow(/Called unwrap on an error/);
+    expect(Result.unwrapOr(okR, 42)).toBe(10);
+    expect(Result.unwrapOr(errR, 42)).toBe(42);
+    expect(Result.match(okR, { ok: (v) => String(v), err: () => "err" })).toBe("10");
+    expect(
+      Result.match(errR, { ok: () => "ok", err: (e) => e.message }),
+    ).toBe("bad");
+    expect(Result.flatMap(okR, (x) => Result.ok(x + 1))).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 11 });
+    expect(Result.flatMap(errR, () => okR).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(Result.map(errR, () => 1).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(Result.mapErr(okR, () => "x")).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 10 });
+    expect(Result.mapErr(errR, (e) => e.message)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "bad" });
+    expect(Result.all([Result.ok(1), Result.ok(2)])).toEqual({ ok: true, value: [1, 2] });
+    expect(Result.all([Result.ok(1), errR]).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(await Result.fromPromise(Promise.resolve("hi"))).toEqual({ ok: true, value: "hi" });
+    expect((await Result.fromPromise(Promise.reject(new NotFoundError("x")))).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(new ResultRateLimitError("rl", 10).retryAfter).toBe(10);
+    expect(new ResultNetworkError("net", 502).statusCode).toBe(502);
+    expect(new McpTimeoutError("op", 100).code).toBe(ErrorCode.TIMEOUT_ERROR);
+    expect(new McpRateLimitError(5).code).toBe(ErrorCode.RATE_LIMIT_ERROR);
+    expect(new McpValidationError("v").code).toBe(ErrorCode.VALIDATION_ERROR);
+    expect(new NetworkError("n").code).toBe(ErrorCode.NETWORK_ERROR);
+
+    expect(isAbortOrTimeoutError(Object.assign(new Error(), { name: "AbortError" }))).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAbortOrTimeoutError(Object.assign(new Error(), { name: "TimeoutError" }))).toBe(true);
+    expect(isAbortOrTimeoutError(new Error("normal"))).toBe(false);
+    expect(isAbortOrTimeoutError(null)).toBe(false);
+
+    const parent = new AbortController();
+    const linked = createTimeoutController(5000, parent.signal);
+    parent.abort();
+    expect(linked.wasAbortedByParent()).toBe(true);
+    expect(linked.didTimeout()).toBe(false);
+    linked.cleanup();
+
+    const preAborted = new AbortController();
+    preAborted.abort();
+    const preLinked = createTimeoutController(5000, preAborted.signal);
+    expect(preLinked.wasAbortedByParent()).toBe(true);
+    preLinked.cleanup();
+
+    vi.useFakeTimers();
+    const timedController = createTimeoutController(25);
+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(30);
+    expect(timedController.didTimeout()).toBe(true);
+    timedController.cleanup();
+    vi.useRealTimers();
+
+    await expect(
+      raceWithTimeoutAndSignal(Promise.resolve("fast"), 100, undefined, "fast"),
+    ).resolves.toBe("fast");
+
+    const racePreAbort = new AbortController();
+    racePreAbort.abort();
+    await expect(
+      raceWithTimeoutAndSignal(new Promise(() => {}), 100, racePreAbort.signal, "pre"),
+    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ResultTimeoutError);
+
+    const raceLiveAbort = new AbortController();
+    const racing = raceWithTimeoutAndSignal(
+      new Promise(() => {}),
+      5000,
+      raceLiveAbort.signal,
+      "live-abort",
+    );
+    raceLiveAbort.abort();
+    await expect(racing).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ResultTimeoutError);
+
+    await expect(
+      raceWithTimeoutAndSignal(new Promise(() => {}), 20, undefined, "slow"),
+    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ResultTimeoutError);
+  });
+});
