+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import { Client } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js";
+import { InMemoryTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/inMemory.js";
+import { createMCPServer } from "../../lib/core/mcp-server.js";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../../lib/core/cache.js";
+import { RequestContext } from "../../lib/infrastructure/tracing.js";
+
+const API_CONFIG = {
+  resultadosBaseUrl: "https://resultados.tse.jus.br/oficial/",
+  ckanBaseUrl: "https://dadosabertos.tse.jus.br/api/3/action/",
+};
+
+const EXPECTED_TOOLS = [
+  "tse_apuracao_cargo",
+  "tse_candidatos_busca",
+  "tse_comparecimento_abstencao",
+  "tse_dados_abertos_datasets",
+  "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+  "tse_municipios_zonas",
+  "tse_partidos_federacoes",
+];
+
+describe("MCP Server handlers (ListTools, ListResources, ReadResource, CallTool)", () => {
+  const origMaxOutput = process.env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS;
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+    if (origMaxOutput === undefined) {
+      delete process.env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS;
+    } else {
+      process.env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS = origMaxOutput;
+    }
+  });
+
+  async function connectClientServer(options: {
+    rateLimit?: { windowMs: number; maxRequests: number } | false;
+    getRequestContext?: () => RequestContext | undefined;
+  } = {}) {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(50, 60_000);
+    const server = createMCPServer({
+      apiConfig: API_CONFIG,
+      cache,
+      ...options,
+    });
+    const [clientTransport, serverTransport] = InMemoryTransport.createLinkedPair();
+    const client = new Client({ name: "unit-client", version: "0.1.0" });
+
+    await Promise.all([
+      server.connect(serverTransport),
+      client.connect(clientTransport),
+    ]);
+
+    return {
+      client,
+      server,
+      close: async () => {
+        await client.close();
+        await server.close();
+        cache.clear();
+      },
+    };
+  }
+
+  it("ListTools returns exact 7 tools, ListResources returns [], ReadResource throws Unknown resource URI", async () => {
+    const pair = await connectClientServer();
+    try {
+      const { tools } = await pair.client.listTools();
+      expect(tools.map((t) => t.name).sort()).toEqual(EXPECTED_TOOLS);
+
+      const { resources } = await pair.client.listResources();
+      expect(resources).toEqual([]);
+
+      await expect(
+        pair.client.readResource({ uri: "ui://tse/nonexistent" }),
+      ).rejects.toThrow(/Unknown resource URI/);
+    } finally {
+      await pair.close();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("CallTool executes tool successfully, bounds oversized output, and converts validation/rate-limit/exceptions to isError payloads", async () => {
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () =>
+        new Response(
+          JSON.stringify({
+            pl: [
+              {
+                c: "2024",
+                dt: "06/10/2024",
+                e: [
+                  {
+                    cd: "619",
+                    cdt2: "",
+                    nm: "Eleicoes Municipais 2024",
+                    t: "1",
+                    tp: "3",
+                    abr: [],
+                  },
+                ],
+              },
+            ],
+          }),
+          { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } },
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+
+    const ctx = new RequestContext("req-mcp-handler-1");
+    const pair = await connectClientServer({
+      rateLimit: { windowMs: 60_000, maxRequests: 3 },
+      getRequestContext: () => ctx,
+    });
+
+    try {
+      // 1) Successful tool call
+      const okCall = await pair.client.callTool({
+        name: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+        arguments: { ano: 2024 },
+      });
+      expect(okCall.isError).toBeFalsy();
+      expect(ctx.getMetadata("tool")).toBe("tse_eleicoes_catalogo");
+
+      // 2) Validation error returns isError: true
+      const invalidCall = await pair.client.callTool({
+        name: "tse_municipios_zonas",
+        arguments: {},
+      });
+      expect(invalidCall.isError).toBe(true);
+      expect((invalidCall.content as Array<{ text: string }>)[0].text).toContain("Invalid parameters");
+
+      // 3) Unknown tool throws inside executeTool -> caught and formatted as isError: true
+      const unknownCall = await pair.client.callTool({
+        name: "nonexistent_tool",
+        arguments: {},
+      });
+      expect(unknownCall.isError).toBe(true);
+
+      // 4) 4th call exceeds maxRequests: 3 -> RateLimitError formatted as isError: true
+      const rateLimitedCall = await pair.client.callTool({
+        name: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+        arguments: { ano: 2024 },
+      });
+      expect(rateLimitedCall.isError).toBe(true);
+      expect((rateLimitedCall.content as Array<{ text: string }>)[0].text).toContain("Rate limit exceeded");
+    } finally {
+      await pair.close();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("CallTool respects MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS output bounding and MCP_TOOL_RATE_LIMIT_MAX", async () => {
+    process.env.MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS = "1024";
+    process.env.MCP_TOOL_RATE_LIMIT_MAX = "5";
+    const bigEleicoes = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({
+      cd: String(600 + i),
+      cdt2: "",
+      nm: `Eleicao Estadual/Municipal TesteNumero_${i}_${"X".repeat(40)}`,
+      t: "1",
+      tp: "3",
+      abr: [],
+    }));
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () =>
+        new Response(JSON.stringify({ pl: [{ c: "2024", dt: "06/10/2024", e: bigEleicoes }] }), {
+          status: 200,
+          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        }),
+      ),
+    );
+
+    const pair = await connectClientServer({ rateLimit: false });
+    const envLimitedServer = createMCPServer({ apiConfig: API_CONFIG });
+    expect(envLimitedServer).toBeTruthy();
+    delete process.env.MCP_TOOL_RATE_LIMIT_MAX;
+    await envLimitedServer.close();
+    try {
+      const res = await pair.client.callTool({
+        name: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+        arguments: { ano: 2024 },
+      });
+      const text = (res.content as Array<{ text: string }>)[0].text;
+      expect(text.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1024);
+      expect(JSON.parse(text)).toMatchObject({ truncated: true, max_chars: 1024 });
+    } finally {
+      await pair.close();
+    }
+  });
+});

+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { resolve } from "node:path";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import "../../lib/tools/index.js";
+import { resolveApiConfig } from "../../lib/config/index.js";
+import {
+  executeRegisteredTool,
+  getToolCount,
+  hasTool,
+  hasToolSchema,
+  registerTool,
+} from "../../lib/core/registry.js";
+import { TOOL_SCHEMAS } from "../../lib/core/tool-schemas.js";
+import { TOOL_DEFINITIONS } from "../../lib/core/tools.js";
+
+describe("Runtime Zod Validation (.strict()) & SC-9 Allowlist Across All 7 TSE Tools", () => {
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+  });
+
+  it("registers all 7 tools with Zod schemas and zero api_key parameters", () => {
+    expect(getToolCount()).toBe(7);
+    expect(TOOL_DEFINITIONS).toHaveLength(7);
+
+    for (const def of TOOL_DEFINITIONS) {
+      expect(hasTool(def.name)).toBe(true);
+      expect(hasToolSchema(def.name)).toBe(true);
+      expect(TOOL_SCHEMAS[def.name]).toBeDefined();
+      const props = (def.inputSchema as { properties?: Record<string, unknown> }).properties;
+      expect(props).not.toHaveProperty("api_key");
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("rejects invalid types, .strict() extra fields, and scope violations without calling fetch", async () => {
+    const fetchSpy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch");
+    const apiConfig = resolveApiConfig();
+
+    const invalidCases: Array<{ tool: string; args: unknown; label: string }> = [
+      { tool: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo", args: { tipo: "invalido" }, label: "invalid tipo" },
+      { tool: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo", args: { ano: 2000 }, label: "ano out of range" },
+      { tool: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo", args: { limit: 100 }, label: "limit > 50" },
+      { tool: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo", args: { api_key: "secret" }, label: "strict unknown api_key" },
+
+      { tool: "tse_municipios_zonas", args: {}, label: "missing all filters" },
+      { tool: "tse_municipios_zonas", args: { uf: "br" }, label: "uf cannot be br" },
+      { tool: "tse_municipios_zonas", args: { uf: "xx" }, label: "invalid uf" },
+      { tool: "tse_municipios_zonas", args: { codigo_ibge: "123" }, label: "short codigo_ibge" },
+      { tool: "tse_municipios_zonas", args: { codigo_tse: "123" }, label: "short codigo_tse" },
+      { tool: "tse_municipios_zonas", args: { nome: "A" }, label: "short nome" },
+
+      { tool: "tse_apuracao_cargo", args: { cargo: "prefeito", uf: "sp" }, label: "prefeito missing municipio" },
+      { tool: "tse_apuracao_cargo", args: { cargo: "vereador", uf: "br", codigo_tse_municipio: "71072" }, label: "vereador uf=br" },
+      { tool: "tse_apuracao_cargo", args: { cargo: "governador", uf: "br" }, label: "governador uf=br" },
+      { tool: "tse_apuracao_cargo", args: { cargo: "senador" }, label: "senador missing uf" },
+      { tool: "tse_apuracao_cargo", args: { cargo: "presidente", codigo_tse_municipio: "71072" }, label: "municipio without state uf" },
+      { tool: "tse_apuracao_cargo", args: { cargo: "presidente", uf: "xx" }, label: "invalid uf" },
+      { tool: "tse_apuracao_cargo", args: { cargo: "presidente", page: 0 }, label: "page < 1" },
+
+      { tool: "tse_candidatos_busca", args: { cargo: "prefeito" }, label: "missing uf and municipio" },
+      { tool: "tse_candidatos_busca", args: { cargo: "deputado_federal" }, label: "missing state uf" },
+      { tool: "tse_candidatos_busca", args: { cargo: "presidente", numero: "1" }, label: "short numero" },
+      { tool: "tse_candidatos_busca", args: { cargo: "presidente", nome: "X" }, label: "short nome" },
+
+      { tool: "tse_partidos_federacoes", args: { cargo: "deputado_estadual", uf: "br" }, label: "state cargo uf=br" },
+      { tool: "tse_partidos_federacoes", args: { cargo: "presidente", limit: 51 }, label: "limit > 50" },
+
+      { tool: "tse_comparecimento_abstencao", args: { codigo_tse_municipio: "71072" }, label: "municipio with default br" },
+      { tool: "tse_comparecimento_abstencao", args: { uf: "zz", codigo_tse_municipio: "abcde" }, label: "invalid codigo_tse_municipio" },
+      { tool: "tse_comparecimento_abstencao", args: { uf: "qq" }, label: "invalid uf" },
+
+      { tool: "tse_dados_abertos_datasets", args: { package_id: "BAD SLUG!" }, label: "invalid package_id" },
+      { tool: "tse_dados_abertos_datasets", args: { ano: 2010 }, label: "ano < 2014" },
+      { tool: "tse_dados_abertos_datasets", args: { rows: 26 }, label: "rows > 25" },
+      { tool: "tse_dados_abertos_datasets", args: { api_key: "x" }, label: "strict unknown api_key" },
+    ];
+
+    const testedTools = new Set(invalidCases.map((c) => c.tool));
+    expect(testedTools.size).toBe(7);
+
+    for (const { tool, args, label } of invalidCases) {
+      const res = await executeRegisteredTool(tool, args, apiConfig);
+      expect(res.ok, `Expected ${tool} (${label}) to fail`).toBe(false);
+      expect(res.error).toMatch(/^Invalid parameters for /);
+    }
+
+    await expect(executeRegisteredTool("unknown_tool", {}, apiConfig)).rejects.toThrow(
+      /Unknown tool: unknown_tool/,
+    );
+    expect(fetchSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it("warns on duplicate tool registration while keeping registry count intact", () => {
+    const warnSpy = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => {});
+    const existing = TOOL_DEFINITIONS[0];
+    registerTool({
+      ...existing,
+      zodSchema: TOOL_SCHEMAS[existing.name],
+      execute: async () => ({ ok: true, data: {} }),
+    });
+    expect(warnSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining("already registered"));
+    expect(getToolCount()).toBe(7);
+  });
+
+  it("keeps .archagents/_capabilities.json and .archagents/mcp-tool-allowlist.json aligned with all 7 tools", () => {
+    const cap = JSON.parse(
+      readFileSync(resolve(process.cwd(), ".archagents/_capabilities.json"), "utf8"),
+    ) as { enabled: boolean; agentic?: { mcp?: boolean; server_name?: string; tool_count?: number } };
+
+    expect(cap.enabled).toBe(false);
+    expect(cap.agentic?.mcp).toBe(true);
+    expect(cap.agentic?.server_name).toBe("mcp-tse");
+    expect(cap.agentic?.tool_count).toBe(7);
+
+    const allowlist = JSON.parse(
+      readFileSync(resolve(process.cwd(), ".archagents/mcp-tool-allowlist.json"), "utf8"),
+    ) as {
+      server: string;
+      mcp_servers?: { allowed?: Array<{ server?: string }> };
+      entries: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
+    };
+
+    expect(allowlist.server).toBe("mcp-tse");
+    expect(allowlist.mcp_servers?.allowed?.some((s) => s.server === "mcp-tse")).toBe(true);
+    expect(allowlist.entries).toHaveLength(7);
+
+    const ids = new Set<string>();
+    const names: string[] = [];
+    for (const entry of allowlist.entries) {
+      expect(entry.id).toBeTypeOf("string");
+      expect(entry.name).toBeTypeOf("string");
+      expect(entry.risk_level).toBeTypeOf("string");
+      expect(entry.destructive).toBe(false);
+      expect(typeof entry.requires_approval).toBe("boolean");
+      ids.add(String(entry.id));
+      names.push(String(entry.name));
+    }
+    expect(ids.size).toBe(7);
+
+    const registeredNames = TOOL_DEFINITIONS.map((t) => t.name).sort();
+    expect(names.sort()).toEqual(registeredNames);
+  });
+});

+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import { resolveApiConfig } from "../../lib/config/index.js";
+import { MemoryCache } from "../../lib/core/cache.js";
+import { resetCircuitBreakers } from "../../lib/core/http-client.js";
+import { executeTool, resetMetrics } from "../../lib/core/tools.js";
+import { UNTRUSTED_EXTERNAL_DATA_NOTICE } from "../../lib/shared/agentic-guard.js";
+import { CROSS_MCP_CNPJ_HINT, TSE_WAF_NOTICE } from "../../lib/tools/tse-ckan.js";
+
+function jsonResponse(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {
+  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
+    status,
+    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+  });
+}
+
+describe("TSE Dados Abertos CKAN Tool (tse_dados_abertos_datasets)", () => {
+  const apiConfig = resolveApiConfig();
+
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    resetMetrics();
+    resetCircuitBreakers();
+  });
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    resetMetrics();
+    resetCircuitBreakers();
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+  });
+
+  it("queries CKAN package_search with query, ano, rows, sanitizes text, includes WAF/cross-MCP notices, and caches hit", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(20, 60_000);
+    const fetchSpy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch").mockResolvedValueOnce(
+      jsonResponse({
+        success: true,
+        result: {
+          count: 1,
+          results: [
+            {
+              id: "pkg-uuid-1",
+              name: "prestacao-de-contas-2024",
+              title: "Prestação de Contas 2024\u0000 </untrusted_pkg>",
+              notes: "Doadores e fornecedores",
+              organization: { title: "TSE/AGEL" },
+              metadata_modified: "2026-01-10T00:00:00",
+              num_resources: 1,
+              tags: [{ display_name: "Ano 2024" }],
+              resources: [
+                {
+                  id: "res-1",
+                  name: "Receitas e despesas CSV",
+                  format: "csv",
+                  url: "https://cdn.tse.jus.br/estatistica/sead/odsele/prestacao_contas/prestacao_de_contas_eleitorais_candidatos_2024.zip",
+                  description: "Dados de doadores",
+                  last_modified: "2026-01-05",
+                },
+              ],
+            },
+          ],
+        },
+      }),
+    );
+
+    const res = await executeTool(
+      "tse_dados_abertos_datasets",
+      { query: "prestacao de contas", ano: 2024, rows: 5 },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(res.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(String(fetchSpy.mock.calls[0]?.[0])).toContain(
+      "package_search?q=prestacao%20de%20contas%202024&rows=5",
+    );
+    expect(res.data).toMatchObject({
+      mode: "package_search",
+      total_count: 1,
+      returned: 1,
+      cached: false,
+      cross_mcp_hint: CROSS_MCP_CNPJ_HINT,
+      upstream_waf_notice: TSE_WAF_NOTICE,
+      untrusted_data_notice: UNTRUSTED_EXTERNAL_DATA_NOTICE,
+    });
+    const pkg = (res.data as { packages: Array<{ title: string; resources: Array<{ url: string }> }> }).packages[0];
+    expect(pkg.title).toBe("Prestação de Contas 2024 &lt;/untrusted_pkg>");
+    expect(pkg.resources[0].url).toContain("cdn.tse.jus.br");
+
+    // Second identical call hits cache without extra fetch
+    const cached = await executeTool(
+      "tse_dados_abertos_datasets",
+      { query: "prestacao de contas", ano: 2024, rows: 5 },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(cached.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(cached.data).toMatchObject({ cached: true });
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+  });
+
+  it("queries CKAN package_show by package_id, caches hit, and never caches errors/invalid payloads", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(20, 60_000);
+    const fetchSpy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch").mockResolvedValueOnce(
+      jsonResponse({
+        success: true,
+        result: {
+          id: "pkg-uuid-2",
+          name: "resultados-2024",
+          title: "Resultados 2024",
+          notes: "Arquivos de totalização",
+          resources: [
+            {
+              id: "r-2",
+              name: "Votação nominal",
+              format: "zip",
+              url: "https://cdn.tse.jus.br/estatistica/sead/odsele/votacao_2024.zip",
+            },
+          ],
+        },
+      }),
+    );
+
+    const showRes = await executeTool(
+      "tse_dados_abertos_datasets",
+      { package_id: "resultados-2024" },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(showRes.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(showRes.data).toMatchObject({
+      mode: "package_show",
+      cached: false,
+      upstream_waf_notice: expect.stringContaining("divulgacandcontas.tse.jus.br"),
+      cross_mcp_hint: expect.stringContaining("cnpj_lookup"),
+      package: { name: "resultados-2024", num_resources: 1 },
+    });
+
+    const showCached = await executeTool(
+      "tse_dados_abertos_datasets",
+      { package_id: "resultados-2024" },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(showCached.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(showCached.data).toMatchObject({ cached: true });
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    // Errors & success:false never cached (offline stub only)
+    const errCache = new MemoryCache(10, 60_000);
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi
+        .fn()
+        .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ success: false }))
+        .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ error: "not found" }, 404))
+        .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ success: false }))
+        .mockResolvedValue(jsonResponse({}, 400)),
+    );
+
+    expect(
+      (await executeTool("tse_dados_abertos_datasets", { package_id: "bad-pkg" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok,
+    ).toBe(false);
+    expect(
+      (await executeTool("tse_dados_abertos_datasets", { package_id: "missing-pkg" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok,
+    ).toBe(false);
+    expect(
+      (await executeTool("tse_dados_abertos_datasets", { query: "abc" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok,
+    ).toBe(false);
+    expect(
+      (await executeTool("tse_dados_abertos_datasets", { query: "def" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok,
+    ).toBe(false);
+    expect(errCache.entries()).toHaveLength(0);
+
+    // Sparse CKAN metadata (null org, string tags, no num_resources, missing notes/desc)
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () =>
+        jsonResponse({
+          success: true,
+          result: {
+            results: [
+              {
+                id: "sparse-1",
+                name: "sparse-ds",
+                title: "Sparse",
+                tags: ["tag-a", { name: "tag-b" }],
+                resources: [{ id: "r0", name: "CSV", format: "csv", url: "https://cdn.tse.jus.br/a.csv" }],
+              },
+            ],
+          },
+        }),
+      ),
+    );
+    const sparseRes = await executeTool("tse_dados_abertos_datasets", { ano: 2022 }, apiConfig);
+    expect(sparseRes.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(sparseRes.data).toMatchObject({
+      query: null,
+      ano: 2022,
+      total_count: 1,
+      packages: [{ organization: null, num_resources: 1, tags: ["tag-a", "tag-b"] }],
+    });
+  });
+});

+describe("TSE Resultados EA20 Tools (1..6) & Worker Hygiene", () => {
+  const apiConfig = resolveApiConfig();
+
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    resetMetrics();
+    resetCircuitBreakers();

+  it("keeps zero node:* imports inside lib/shared/ and lib/tools/", () => {
+    for (const relPath of [
+      "lib/shared/tse-schemas.ts",
+      "lib/shared/agentic-guard.ts",
+      "lib/shared/compliance-notices.ts",
+      "lib/shared/errors.ts",
+      "lib/shared/log-redaction.ts",
+      "lib/shared/types/result.ts",
+      "lib/shared/utils/constants.ts",
+      "lib/shared/utils/timeout.ts",
+      "lib/tools/tse-resultados.ts",
+      "lib/tools/tse-ckan.ts",
+      "lib/tools/index.ts",
+    ]) {
+      const content = readFileSync(resolve(ROOT, relPath), "utf8");
+      expect(content, `${relPath} must be Web-standard`).not.toMatch(/from\s+["']node:/);
+    }
+  });

+  it("tse_eleicoes_catalogo filters ano, tipo, uf, limit, caches successes, and never caches errors", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(20, 60_000);
+    const fetchSpy = vi
+      .spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
+      .mockResolvedValue(jsonResponse(SAMPLE_ELE_C));
+
+    const allRes = await executeTool("tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {}, apiConfig, cache);
+    expect(allRes.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(allRes.data).toMatchObject({
+      total_matches: 5,
+      returned: 5,
+      cached: false,
+      untrusted_data_notice: UNTRUSTED_EXTERNAL_DATA_NOTICE,
+    });
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    // Filter suplementar + cached hit
+    const supRes = await executeTool(
+      "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+      { tipo: "suplementar", ano: 2025, uf: "ms", limit: 5 },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(supRes.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(supRes.data).toMatchObject({
+      total_matches: 1,
+      returned: 1,
+      cached: true,
+    });
+    expect((supRes.data as { eleicoes: Array<{ codigo_eleicao: string }> }).eleicoes[0].codigo_eleicao).toBe("6120");
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    // Filter ordinaria + ano 2026
+    const ordRes = await executeTool(
+      "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+      { tipo: "ordinaria", ano: 2026, uf: "br", limit: 1 },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(ordRes.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(ordRes.data).toMatchObject({ total_matches: 2, returned: 1 });
+
+    // Error & malformed never cached
+    const errCache = new MemoryCache(10, 60_000);
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ error: "not found" }, 404))
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ unexpected: true }));
+
+    const fail404 = await executeTool("tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {}, apiConfig, errCache);
+    expect(fail404.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(fail404.error).toContain("not found");
+
+    const failShape = await executeTool("tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {}, apiConfig, errCache);
+    expect(failShape.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(failShape.error).toContain("unexpected ele-c.json");
+    expect(errCache.entries()).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it("tse_municipios_zonas translates IBGE <-> TSE codes, accents, capitals, zones, and caches cleanly", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(20, 60_000);
+    const fetchSpy = vi
+      .spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
+      .mockResolvedValue(jsonResponse(SAMPLE_MUN_CM));
+
+    const byName = await executeTool(
+      "tse_municipios_zonas",
+      { nome: "sao paulo" },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(byName.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(byName.data).toMatchObject({
+      total_matches: 1,
+      returned: 1,
+      cached: false,
+      municipios: [
+        {
+          codigo_tse: "71072",
+          codigo_ibge: "3550308",

+          uf: "SP",

+          zonas_eleitorais: ["0001", "0002"],

+      ],
+    });

+      "tse_municipios_zonas",

+      apiConfig,

+    );

+      cached: true,

+      "tse_municipios_zonas",

+    );
+    expect(byTseAndUf.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(byTseAndUf.data).toMatchObject({
+      total_matches: 1,
+      municipios: [{ nome: "CAMPINAS" }],
+    });
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    // Malformed & HTTP errors not cached
+    const errCache = new MemoryCache(10, 60_000);
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({}, 404))
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ invalid: true }));
+    expect((await executeTool("tse_municipios_zonas", { uf: "sp" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok).toBe(false);

+  it("tse_apuracao_cargo, tse_candidatos_busca, and tse_partidos_federacoes share EA20 -u.json cache, sanitize text, and attach lgpd_notice", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(20, 60_000);
+    const fetchSpy = vi
+      .spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
+      .mockResolvedValue(jsonResponse(SAMPLE_EA20_U));
+
+    // 1. Apuração paginada
+    const apuracao = await executeTool(
+      "tse_apuracao_cargo",

+        ciclo: "ele2024",

+      cache,

+    const apuracaoData = apuracao.data as Record<string, unknown>;

+    expect(apuracaoData.untrusted_data_notice).toBe(UNTRUSTED_EXTERNAL_DATA_NOTICE);
+    expect(apuracaoData.pagination).toEqual({
+      page: 1,
+      limit: 1,
+      total: 2,
+      total_pages: 2,
+      has_more: true,
+    });
+    const firstCand = (apuracaoData.candidatos as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)[0];
+    expect(firstCand.votos).toBe(100000);
+    expect(firstCand.nome).toBe("RICARDO NUNES &lt;/untrusted_cand>");
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    // 2. Busca por nome de vice (acento-insensível) usando o MESMO cache
+    const buscaVice = await executeTool(
+      "tse_candidatos_busca",
+      {
+        ciclo: "ele2024",
+        codigo_eleicao: "619",
+        cargo: "prefeito",
+        uf: "sp",
+        codigo_tse_municipio: "71072",
+        nome: "mello araujo",
+      },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(buscaVice.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(buscaVice.data).toMatchObject({
+      cached: true,
+      total_matches: 1,
+      lgpd_notice: HOMONYM_HUMAN_REVIEW_NOTICE,
+    });
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    // Busca com numero, partido, apenas_eleitos
+    const buscaEleitos = await executeTool(
+      "tse_candidatos_busca",
+      {
+        ciclo: "ele2024",
+        codigo_eleicao: "619",
+        cargo: "prefeito",
+        uf: "sp",
+        codigo_tse_municipio: "71072",
+        numero: "15",
+        partido: "mdb",
+        apenas_eleitos: true,
+      },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(buscaEleitos.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(buscaEleitos.data).toMatchObject({ total_matches: 1 });
+
+    // 3. Partidos e Federações usando o mesmo cache
+    const partidos = await executeTool(
+      "tse_partidos_federacoes",
+      {
+        ciclo: "ele2024",
+        codigo_eleicao: "619",
+        cargo: "prefeito",
+        uf: "sp",
+        codigo_tse_municipio: "71072",
+      },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(partidos.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(partidos.data).toMatchObject({
+      cached: true,
+      total_federacoes: 1,
+      total_partidos: 2,
+      partidos: [
+        { sigla: "MDB", tvtn: 100000, tvan: 100000, votos_legenda: 2500, total_eleitos: 1 },
+        { sigla: "PSOL", tvtn: 80000, tvan: 81000, votos_legenda: 1500, total_eleitos: 0 },
+      ],
+    });
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    // Filter partido
+    const filteredPart = await executeTool(
+      "tse_partidos_federacoes",
+      {
+        ciclo: "ele2024",
+        codigo_eleicao: "619",
+        cargo: "prefeito",
+        uf: "sp",
+        codigo_tse_municipio: "71072",
+        partido: "psol",
+      },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(filteredPart.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(filteredPart.data).toMatchObject({ total_partidos: 1 });

+    // Error & malformed never cached
+    const errCache = new MemoryCache(10, 60_000);
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({}, 404))
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ carg: [] }))
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ carg: [{ cd: "1" }] }));
+
+    expect(
+      (await executeTool("tse_apuracao_cargo", { cargo: "presidente" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok,
+    ).toBe(false);
+    expect(
+      (await executeTool("tse_candidatos_busca", { cargo: "presidente" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok,
+    ).toBe(false);
+    expect(
+      (await executeTool("tse_partidos_federacoes", { cargo: "presidente" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok,
+    ).toBe(false);
+    expect(errCache.entries()).toHaveLength(0);
+  });

+  it("tse_comparecimento_abstencao handles BR summary, municipal filter, missing municipality, truncation >100, and errors", async () => {
+    const cache = new MemoryCache(20, 60_000);
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch").mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse(SAMPLE_EA20_AB));
+

+      truncated: true,

+        eleitores_aptos: 120000,

+      },

+
+    const spOne = await executeTool(

+      cached: true,
+      total_abrangencias: 1,
+      abr: [{ cdabr: "70005" }],
+    });
+
+    const spMissing = await executeTool(
+      "tse_comparecimento_abstencao",
+      { uf: "sp", codigo_tse_municipio: "99999" },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(spMissing.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(spMissing.error).toMatch(/not found/i);
+
+    // Malformed & HTTP 404 never cached
+    const errCache = new MemoryCache(10, 60_000);
+    vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({}, 404))
+      .mockResolvedValueOnce(jsonResponse({ bad: true }));
+    expect((await executeTool("tse_comparecimento_abstencao", { uf: "rj" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect((await executeTool("tse_comparecimento_abstencao", { uf: "rj" }, apiConfig, errCache)).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(errCache.entries()).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it("direct tool executors return structured validation errors on invalid params", async () => {
+    const {
+      executeEleicoesCatalogo,
+      executeMunicipiosZonas,
+      executeApuracaoCargo,
+      executeCandidatosBusca,
+      executePartidosFederacoes,
+      executeComparecimentoAbstencao,
+    } = await import("../../lib/tools/tse-resultados.js");
+    const { executeDadosAbertosDatasets } = await import("../../lib/tools/tse-ckan.js");
+    const apiConfig = resolveApiConfig();
+
+    expect((await executeEleicoesCatalogo({ ano: 1990 } as never, apiConfig)).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect((await executeMunicipiosZonas({} as never, apiConfig)).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect((await executeApuracaoCargo({ cargo: "governador" } as never, apiConfig)).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect((await executeCandidatosBusca({ nome: "Silva" } as never, apiConfig)).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect((await executePartidosFederacoes({ cargo: "prefeito" } as never, apiConfig)).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect((await executeComparecimentoAbstencao({ uf: "xx" } as never, apiConfig)).ok).toBe(false);
+    expect((await executeDadosAbertosDatasets({ rows: 999 } as never, apiConfig)).ok).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("handles tied candidate/party votes, sparse EA20 fields, and ele-c fallback year extraction", async () => {

+          cdabr: "sp",

+              cd: "3",

+                      nm: "Partido Socialista",
+                      tvtn: "100",
+                      tvan: "90",
+                      tval: "10",
+                      cand: [
+                        { n: "40", sqcand: "1", nm: "Zeca", nmu: "Zeca", st: "Não eleito", dvt: "Válido", vap: "90", e: "n" },
+                      ],
+                    },
+                    {
+                      n: "18",
+                      sg: "REDE",
+                      nm: "Rede Sustentabilidade",
+                      tvtn: "100",
+                      tvan: "95",
+                      tval: "10",
+                      cand: [
+                        { n: "18", sqcand: "2", nm: "Ana", nmu: "Ana", st: "Eleito", dvt: "Válido", vap: "90", e: "s" },
+                      ],

+                },

+            },
+          ],

+

+    expect(cat.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(cat.data).toMatchObject({ total_matches: 1 });
+
+    const cat2 = await executeTool("tse_eleicoes_catalogo", { ano: 2018, tipo: "ordinaria" }, apiConfig);
+    expect(cat2.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(cat2.data).toMatchObject({ total_matches: 1 });
+
+    const catSup = await executeTool("tse_eleicoes_catalogo", { tipo: "suplementar" }, apiConfig);

+    expect(catSup.data).toMatchObject({ total_matches: 0 });

+    const apur = await executeTool(

+
+    const parts = await executeTool(
+      "tse_partidos_federacoes",
+      { cargo: "governador", uf: "sp" },
+      apiConfig,
+      cache,
+    );
+    expect(parts.ok).toBe(true);
+    const partyList = (parts.data as { partidos: Array<{ sigla: string }> }).partidos;
+    expect(partyList.map((p) => p.sigla)).toEqual(["REDE", "MDB", "PSB"]);
+  });

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import worker from "../../lib/workers/worker.js";
+import {
+  authenticateRequest,
+  constantTimeEqual,
+  isPublicPath,
+} from "../../lib/workers/auth.js";
+import {
+  handleJWKS,
+  handleOAuthAuthorize,
+  handleOAuthDiscovery,
+  handleOAuthRegister,
+  handleOAuthToken,
+  handleOAuthUserInfo,
+} from "../../lib/workers/oauth.js";
+import {
+  getCorsHeaders,
+  handleCORS,
+  parseAllowedOrigins,
+  withCorsHeaders,
+} from "../../lib/adapters/cloudflare.js";
+
+const MOCK_CTX = {
+  waitUntil: vi.fn(),
+  passThroughOnException: vi.fn(),
+} as unknown as ExecutionContext;
+
+describe("Worker auth, OAuth fail-closed, and CORS policy", () => {
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+  });
+
+  it("constantTimeEqual handles equal, different-content, and different-length inputs", () => {
+    expect(constantTimeEqual("abc-123", "abc-123")).toBe(true);
+    expect(constantTimeEqual("abc-123", "abc-124")).toBe(false);
+    expect(constantTimeEqual("abc", "abcd")).toBe(false);
+    expect(constantTimeEqual("", "")).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("isPublicPath skips auth only for documented public endpoints", () => {
+    expect(isPublicPath("/health")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isPublicPath("/")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isPublicPath("/openapi.json")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isPublicPath("/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server")).toBe(true);
+    expect(isPublicPath("/mcp")).toBe(false);
+    expect(isPublicPath("/tools/tse_eleicoes_catalogo")).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("authenticateRequest enforces Bearer or X-API-Key when MCP_API_KEY is configured", async () => {
+    const publicRes = await authenticateRequest(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/health"),
+      { MCP_API_KEY: "secret-key" },
+    );
+    expect(publicRes.authenticated).toBe(true);
+
+    const missing = await authenticateRequest(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", { method: "POST" }),
+      { MCP_API_KEY: "secret-key" },
+    );
+    expect(missing.authenticated).toBe(false);
+    expect(missing.error?.status).toBe(401);
+    expect(missing.error?.headers.get("WWW-Authenticate")).toBe("Bearer");
+
+    const invalid = await authenticateRequest(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { Authorization: "Bearer wrong" },
+      }),
+      { MCP_API_KEY: "secret-key" },
+    );
+    expect(invalid.authenticated).toBe(false);
+
+    const validBearer = await authenticateRequest(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { Authorization: "Bearer secret-key" },
+      }),
+      { MCP_API_KEY: "secret-key" },
+    );
+    expect(validBearer.authenticated).toBe(true);
+
+    const validApiKey = await authenticateRequest(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "X-API-Key": "secret-key" },
+      }),
+      { MCP_API_KEY: "secret-key" },
+    );
+    expect(validApiKey.authenticated).toBe(true);
+
+    const openMode = await authenticateRequest(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", { method: "POST" }),
+      {},
+    );
+    expect(openMode.authenticated).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("OAuth endpoints are fail-closed 501 with Cache-Control: no-store and no Location header", async () => {
+    const req = new Request("https://worker.test/oauth/authorize?redirect_uri=https://evil.test");
+    const env = { MCP_API_KEY: "k" };
+
+    const responses = await Promise.all([
+      handleOAuthAuthorize(),
+      handleOAuthToken(req, env),
+      handleOAuthUserInfo(req, env),
+      handleJWKS(req, env),
+      handleOAuthRegister(req, env),
+      handleOAuthDiscovery(req, env),
+      worker.fetch(
+        new Request("https://worker.test/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server"),
+        env,
+        MOCK_CTX,
+      ),
+      worker.fetch(
+        new Request("https://worker.test/oauth/token", {
+          method: "POST",
+          headers: { Authorization: "Bearer k" },
+        }),
+        env,
+        MOCK_CTX,
+      ),
+      worker.fetch(
+        new Request("https://worker.test/oauth/register", {
+          method: "POST",
+          headers: { Authorization: "Bearer k" },
+        }),
+        env,
+        MOCK_CTX,
+      ),
+      worker.fetch(
+        new Request("https://worker.test/oauth/userinfo", {
+          headers: { Authorization: "Bearer k" },
+        }),
+        env,
+        MOCK_CTX,
+      ),
+      worker.fetch(new Request("https://worker.test/.well-known/jwks.json"), env, MOCK_CTX),
+    ]);
+
+    for (const res of responses) {
+      expect(res.status).toBe(501);
+      expect(res.headers.get("Location")).toBeNull();
+      expect(res.headers.get("Cache-Control")).toBe("no-store");
+      expect(await res.json()).toMatchObject({ error: "oauth_not_supported" });
+    }
+
+    const unauthOauth = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/oauth/token", { method: "POST" }),
+      env,
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(unauthOauth.status).toBe(401);
+  });
+
+  it("CORS policy restricts origins when MCP_API_KEY or MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS are set", async () => {
+    expect(
+      parseAllowedOrigins("*, https://app.aredes.me/path, not-a-url, ftp://bad, http://localhost:3000"),
+    ).toEqual(["https://app.aredes.me", "http://localhost:3000"]);
+
+    const strictHeaders = getCorsHeaders(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", { headers: { Origin: "https://app.aredes.me" } }),
+      { MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: "https://app.aredes.me" },
+    );
+    expect(strictHeaders["Access-Control-Allow-Origin"]).toBe("https://app.aredes.me");
+    expect(strictHeaders.Vary).toBe("Origin");
+
+    const rejectedPreflight = handleCORS(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+        method: "OPTIONS",
+        headers: { Origin: "https://evil.example" },
+      }),
+      { MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: "https://app.aredes.me" },
+    );
+    expect(rejectedPreflight?.status).toBe(403);
+
+    const apiKeyHeaders = getCorsHeaders(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", { headers: { Origin: "https://evil.example" } }),
+      { MCP_API_KEY: "secret" },
+    );
+    expect(apiKeyHeaders["Access-Control-Allow-Origin"]).toBeUndefined();
+
+    const wrapped = withCorsHeaders(
+      new Response("ok", { status: 200 }),
+      new Request("https://worker.test/health", { headers: { Origin: "https://public.test" } }),
+      { MCP_API_KEY: "secret" },
+      { isPublic: true },
+    );
+    expect(wrapped.headers.get("Access-Control-Allow-Origin")).toBe("*");
+  });
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import worker from "../../lib/workers/worker.js";
+import {
+  handleHealthCheck,
+  handleMCPEndpoint,
+  handleMCPRequest,
+  syncWorkerEnv,
+  type Env,
+} from "../../lib/adapters/cloudflare.js";
+import { handleToolRestEndpoint } from "../../lib/workers/rest.js";
+import { generateOpenAPISchema, handleOpenAPIEndpoint } from "../../lib/workers/openapi.js";
+
+const MOCK_CTX = {
+  waitUntil: vi.fn(),
+  passThroughOnException: vi.fn(),
+} as unknown as ExecutionContext;
+
+function createMockKv(initial: Record<string, string> = {}) {
+  const store = new Map<string, string>(Object.entries(initial));
+  return {
+    get: vi.fn(async (key: string) => store.get(key) ?? null),
+    put: vi.fn(async (key: string, value: string) => {
+      store.set(key, value);
+    }),
+    delete: vi.fn(async (key: string) => {
+      store.delete(key);
+    }),
+    store,
+  };
+}
+
+const SAMPLE_CATALOG = {
+  pl: [
+    {
+      c: "2024",
+      dt: "06/10/2024",
+      e: [
+        {
+          cd: "619",
+          cdt2: "",
+          nm: "Eleicoes Municipais 2024",
+          t: "1",
+          tp: "3",
+          abr: [{ cd: "sp", ds: "SAO PAULO", mu: [] }],
+        },
+      ],
+    },
+  ],
+};
+
+describe("Worker REST, Cloudflare MCP endpoint, Health & OpenAPI", () => {
+  const origResultados = process.env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL;
+  const origCkan = process.env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL;
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+    if (origResultados === undefined) {
+      delete process.env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL;
+    } else {
+      process.env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL = origResultados;
+    }
+    if (origCkan === undefined) {
+      delete process.env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL;
+    } else {
+      process.env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL = origCkan;
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("syncWorkerEnv propagates TSE override keys when non-empty", () => {
+    syncWorkerEnv({
+      TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL: "https://cdn.tse.jus.br/oficial/",
+      TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL: "https://cdn.tse.jus.br/api/3/action/",
+    });
+    expect(process.env.TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL).toBe("https://cdn.tse.jus.br/oficial/");
+    expect(process.env.TSE_CKAN_BASE_URL).toBe("https://cdn.tse.jus.br/api/3/action/");
+  });
+
+  it("handleHealthCheck reports healthy/unconfigured, healthy/ok with KV, and 503 degraded on KV error/timeout", async () => {
+    const unconfigured = await handleHealthCheck(undefined, {});
+    expect(unconfigured.status).toBe(200);
+    expect(await unconfigured.json()).toMatchObject({
+      status: "healthy",
+      service: "mcp-tse",
+      version: "0.1.0",
+      runtime: "cloudflare-workers",
+      checks: { kv: { status: "unconfigured" } },
+    });
+
+    const healthyKv = createMockKv();
+    const okRes = await handleHealthCheck(new Request("https://worker.test/health"), {
+      MCP_CACHE: healthyKv as unknown as KVNamespace,
+    });
+    expect(okRes.status).toBe(200);
+    expect(await okRes.json()).toMatchObject({
+      status: "healthy",
+      checks: { kv: { status: "ok" } },
+    });
+
+    const failingKv = {
+      get: vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error("KV connection refused secret=abc")),
+      put: vi.fn(),
+      delete: vi.fn(),
+    };
+    const degradedRes = await handleHealthCheck(new Request("https://worker.test/health"), {
+      MCP_KV: failingKv as unknown as KVNamespace,
+    });
+    expect(degradedRes.status).toBe(503);
+    expect(await degradedRes.json()).toMatchObject({
+      status: "degraded",
+      checks: { kv: { status: "degraded", error: "kv_unavailable" } },
+    });
+
+    const slowKv = {
+      get: vi.fn(() => new Promise<string | null>(() => {})),
+      put: vi.fn(),
+      delete: vi.fn(),
+    };
+    const timeoutRes = await handleHealthCheck(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/health"),
+      { MCP_CACHE: slowKv as unknown as KVNamespace },
+      { kvTimeoutMs: 15 },
+    );
+    expect(timeoutRes.status).toBe(503);
+  });
+
+  it("handleMCPRequest handles tools/list, tools/call (with KV cache + bounded output), invalid args, and unknown methods", async () => {
+    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async () => {
+      return new Response(JSON.stringify(SAMPLE_CATALOG), {
+        status: 200,
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      });
+    });
+    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchMock);
+    const kv = createMockKv();
+    const env: Env = {
+      MCP_CACHE: kv as unknown as KVNamespace,
+    };
+
+    const listRes = await handleMCPRequest(
+      { jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 1, method: "tools/list" },
+      env,
+    );
+    expect((listRes.result as { tools: unknown[] }).tools).toHaveLength(7);
+
+    const callRes = await handleMCPRequest(
+      {
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: 2,
+        method: "tools/call",
+        params: {
+          name: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+          arguments: { ano: 2024 },
+        },
+      },
+      env,
+    );
+    expect(callRes.error).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(kv.put).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+
+    const invalidArgsRes = await handleMCPRequest(
+      {
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: 3,
+        method: "tools/call",
+        params: {
+          name: "tse_municipios_zonas",
+          arguments: {},
+        },
+      },
+      env,
+    );
+    expect(invalidArgsRes.error?.code).toBe(-32603);
+
+    const bigCatalog = {
+      pl: [
+        {
+          c: "2024",
+          dt: "06/10/2024",
+          e: Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, idx) => ({
+            cd: String(600 + idx),
+            cdt2: "",
+            nm: `Eleicao Teste ${idx} ${"X".repeat(60)}`,

+            tp: "3",
+            abr: [{ cd: "sp", ds: "SAO PAULO", mu: [] }],
+          })),
+        },
+      ],
+    };
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () =>
+        new Response(JSON.stringify(bigCatalog), {
+          status: 200,
+          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        }),
+      ),
+    );
+    const truncatedRes = await handleMCPRequest(
+      {
+        jsonrpc: "2.0",
+        id: 4,
+        method: "tools/call",
+        params: {
+          name: "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+          arguments: { ano: 2024, limit: 10 },
+        },
+      },
+      { MCP_MAX_TOOL_OUTPUT_CHARS: "1024" },
+    );
+    const text = (truncatedRes.result as { content: Array<{ text: string }> }).content[0].text;
+    expect(text).toContain('"truncated": true');

+    expect(unknownMethod.error?.code).toBe(-32601);
+  });
+
+  it("handleMCPEndpoint handles valid POST, malformed JSON (-32700), and unexpected errors (-32603)", async () => {
+    const okRes = await handleMCPEndpoint(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 10, method: "tools/list" }),
+      }),
+      {},
+    );
+    expect(okRes.status).toBe(200);

+    const badJson = await handleMCPEndpoint(

+      }),
+      {},
+    );
+    expect(badJson.status).toBe(400);
+    expect((await badJson.json()).error.code).toBe(-32700);

+    const invalidUrlEnv = await handleMCPEndpoint(

+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 11, method: "tools/list" }),
+      }),
+      { TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL: "https://evil.example/" },
+    );
+    expect(invalidUrlEnv.status).toBe(500);
+  });
+
+  it("REST POST /tools/{toolName} covers valid calls, 404 unknown tool, 400 invalid JSON/array/null, 405, %ZZ 400, and sanitized 500", async () => {
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () =>
+        new Response(JSON.stringify(SAMPLE_CATALOG), {
+          status: 200,
+          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        }),
+      ),
+    );
+
+    const okRest = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ ano: 2024 }),
+      }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(okRest.status).toBe(200);
+    const body = (await okRest.json()) as { content: Array<{ text: string }> };
+    const parsedPayload = JSON.parse(body.content[0].text) as { eleicoes: unknown[] };
+    expect(parsedPayload.eleicoes).toHaveLength(1);
+
+    const unknownTool = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/does_not_exist", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: "{}",
+      }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(unknownTool.status).toBe(404);
+
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      "fetch",
+      vi.fn(async () => new Response("not found", { status: 404 })),
+    );
+    const upstream404 = await handleToolRestEndpoint(
+      "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: JSON.stringify({}),
+      }),
+      {},
+    );
+    expect(upstream404.status).toBe(404);
+
+    const validation400 = await handleToolRestEndpoint(
+      "tse_municipios_zonas",
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/tse_municipios_zonas", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: JSON.stringify({}),
+      }),
+      {},
+    );
+    expect(validation400.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const malformedJson = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: "{oops",
+      }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(malformedJson.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const arrayBody = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: "[]",
+      }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(arrayBody.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const getDisallowed = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/tse_eleicoes_catalogo", { method: "GET" }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(getDisallowed.status).toBe(405);
+
+    const badEncoding = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/%ZZ", { method: "POST" }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(badEncoding.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const error500 = await handleToolRestEndpoint(
+      "tse_eleicoes_catalogo",
+      new Request("https://worker.test/tools/tse_eleicoes_catalogo", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+        body: "{}",
+      }),
+      { TSE_RESULTADOS_BASE_URL: "https://attacker.invalid/" },
+    );
+    expect(error500.status).toBe(500);
+    expect(await error500.json()).toEqual({ error: "Internal server error" });
+  });
+
+  it("OpenAPI schema lists all 7 tools in /tools/{toolName} enum and root / returns metadata", async () => {
+    const schema = generateOpenAPISchema("https://mcp-tse.aredes.me") as {
+      openapi: string;
+      paths: Record<string, { post?: { parameters?: Array<{ schema?: { enum?: string[] } }> } }>;
+    };
+    expect(schema.openapi).toBe("3.0.0");
+    const toolEnum = schema.paths["/tools/{toolName}"]?.post?.parameters?.[0]?.schema?.enum ?? [];
+    expect(toolEnum).toHaveLength(7);
+    expect(toolEnum).toContain("tse_apuracao_cargo");
+    expect(toolEnum).toContain("tse_dados_abertos_datasets");
+
+    const oaRes = handleOpenAPIEndpoint(new Request("https://worker.test/openapi.json"));
+    expect(oaRes.status).toBe(200);
+
+    const rootRes = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/"),
+      { MCP_DPO_CONTACT: "dpo@aredes.me" },
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(rootRes.status).toBe(200);
+    const rootJson = await rootRes.json();
+    expect(rootJson.service).toBe("MCP TSE");
+    expect(rootJson.tools).toHaveLength(7);
+    expect(rootJson.privacy.dpo_contact).toBe("dpo@aredes.me");
+  });
+});
diff --git a/test/unit/worker-security-headers.test.ts b/test/unit/worker-security-headers.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..dee6491
--- /dev/null
+++ b/test/unit/worker-security-headers.test.ts

+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import worker from "../../lib/workers/worker.js";
+import { REQUEST_ID_HEADER } from "../../lib/infrastructure/tracing.js";
+
+const MOCK_CTX = {
+  waitUntil: vi.fn(),
+  passThroughOnException: vi.fn(),
+} as unknown as ExecutionContext;
+
+function assertDefensiveHeaders(res: Response) {
+  expect(res.headers.get("X-Content-Type-Options")).toBe("nosniff");
+  expect(res.headers.get("X-Frame-Options")).toBe("DENY");
+  expect(res.headers.get("Referrer-Policy")).toBe("no-referrer");
+  expect(res.headers.get("Cache-Control")).toBe("no-store");
+  expect(res.headers.get(REQUEST_ID_HEADER)).toBeTruthy();
+}
+
+describe("Worker defensive security headers & HTTP method enforcement", () => {
+  it("applies defensive headers and request-id across public, error, and 405 routes", async () => {
+    const routes = [
+      new Request("https://worker.test/health"),
+      new Request("https://worker.test/"),
+      new Request("https://worker.test/openapi.json"),
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", { method: "GET" }),
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", { method: "DELETE" }),
+      new Request("https://worker.test/unknown-route"),
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+        method: "OPTIONS",
+        headers: { Origin: "http://localhost:3000" },
+      }),
+    ];
+
+    for (const req of routes) {
+      const res = await worker.fetch(req, {}, MOCK_CTX);
+      assertDefensiveHeaders(res);
+    }
+
+    const mcpGet = await worker.fetch(new Request("https://worker.test/mcp"), {}, MOCK_CTX);
+    expect(mcpGet.status).toBe(405);
+
+    const sseDelete = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", { method: "DELETE" }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(sseDelete.status).toBe(405);
+
+    const sseGet = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", {
+        method: "GET",
+        headers: { Accept: "text/event-stream" },
+      }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(sseGet.status).toBe(200);
+    await sseGet.body?.cancel();
+
+    const notFound = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/nonexistent"),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(notFound.status).toBe(404);
+  });
+
+  it("propagates inbound X-Request-Id or rejects unsafe IDs", async () => {
+    const validRes = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/health", {
+        headers: { "X-Request-Id": "trace-worker_01" },
+      }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(validRes.headers.get(REQUEST_ID_HEADER)).toBe("trace-worker_01");
+
+    const unsafeRes = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/health", {
+        headers: { "X-Request-Id": "bad id with spaces\r\n" },
+      }),
+      {},
+      MOCK_CTX,
+    );
+    expect(unsafeRes.headers.get(REQUEST_ID_HEADER)).not.toContain("bad id");
+  });
+});
diff --git a/test/unit/worker-sse-kv-shutdown.test.ts b/test/unit/worker-sse-kv-shutdown.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ccf5546
--- /dev/null
+++ b/test/unit/worker-sse-kv-shutdown.test.ts

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import worker from "../../lib/workers/worker.js";
+import { checkRateLimit, extractClientIdentifier } from "../../lib/workers/rate-limit.js";
+import { handleSSEEndpoint } from "../../lib/workers/sse.js";
+import { TIMEOUTS } from "../../lib/config/timeouts.js";
+
+function createRateLimitKv(initial: Record<string, string> = {}, failMode = false) {
+  const store = new Map<string, string>(Object.entries(initial));
+  return {
+    get: vi.fn(async (key: string) => {
+      if (failMode) throw new Error("KV unreachable secret=xyz");
+      return store.get(key) ?? null;
+    }),
+    put: vi.fn(async (key: string, value: string) => {
+      if (failMode) throw new Error("KV write failed");
+      store.set(key, value);
+    }),
+    delete: vi.fn(async () => {}),
+    store,
+  };
+}
+
+describe("Worker KV rate limiting & SSE lifecycle", () => {
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.useRealTimers();
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+  });
+
+  it("extracts client identifier honoring CF-Connecting-IP and MCP_TRUST_X_FORWARDED_FOR", () => {
+    expect(
+      extractClientIdentifier(
+        new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+          headers: { "CF-Connecting-IP": "198.51.100.10", "X-Forwarded-For": "203.0.113.1" },
+        }),
+        { MCP_TRUST_X_FORWARDED_FOR: "true" },
+      ),
+    ).toBe("198.51.100.10");
+
+    expect(
+      extractClientIdentifier(
+        new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+          headers: { "X-Forwarded-For": "203.0.113.5, 10.0.0.1" },
+        }),
+        {},
+      ),
+    ).toBe("unknown");
+
+    expect(
+      extractClientIdentifier(
+        new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+          headers: { "X-Forwarded-For": "203.0.113.5, 10.0.0.1" },
+        }),
+        { MCP_TRUST_X_FORWARDED_FOR: "true" },
+      ),
+    ).toBe("203.0.113.5");
+  });
+
+  it("checkRateLimit enforces KV quota (429), tolerates corrupt JSON, and fails open on KV error", async () => {
+    expect(
+      (await checkRateLimit(new Request("https://worker.test/health"), {})).allowed,
+    ).toBe(true);
+    expect(
+      (
+        await checkRateLimit(new Request("https://worker.test/mcp"), {
+          MCP_DISABLE_RATE_LIMIT: "true",
+        })
+      ).allowed,
+    ).toBe(true);
+    expect(
+      (await checkRateLimit(new Request("https://worker.test/mcp"), {})).allowed,
+    ).toBe(true);
+
+    const kv = createRateLimitKv();
+    const env = { MCP_KV: kv as unknown as KVNamespace };
+    const req = () =>
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "CF-Connecting-IP": "203.0.113.9" },
+      });
+
+    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
+      const res = await checkRateLimit(req(), env);
+      expect(res.allowed).toBe(true);
+    }
+
+    const blocked = await checkRateLimit(req(), env);
+    expect(blocked.allowed).toBe(false);
+    expect(blocked.error?.status).toBe(429);
+    expect(blocked.error?.headers.get("Retry-After")).toBeTruthy();
+
+    // Corrupt KV entry resets cleanly to count = 1
+    const windowBucket = Math.floor(Date.now() / TIMEOUTS.RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS);
+    kv.store.set(`rate_limit:203.0.113.9:${windowBucket}`, "{not-json");
+    const recovered = await checkRateLimit(req(), env);
+    expect(recovered.allowed).toBe(true);
+
+    // KV errors fail open instead of dropping traffic
+    const failingEnv = {
+      MCP_KV: createRateLimitKv({}, true) as unknown as KVNamespace,
+    };
+    const failOpen = await checkRateLimit(req(), failingEnv);
+    expect(failOpen.allowed).toBe(true);
+
+    // Integration via worker.fetch when rate limited
+    const maxedKv = createRateLimitKv({
+      [`rate_limit:203.0.113.77:${windowBucket}`]: JSON.stringify({ count: 30 }),
+    });
+    const workerRes = await worker.fetch(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/mcp", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { "CF-Connecting-IP": "203.0.113.77" },
+      }),
+      { MCP_KV: maxedKv as unknown as KVNamespace },
+      { waitUntil: vi.fn(), passThroughOnException: vi.fn() } as unknown as ExecutionContext,
+    );
+    expect(workerRes.status).toBe(429);
+  });
+
+  it("handleSSEEndpoint validates Accept, streams connection + init, handles POST RPC & parse error, and cleans up on cancel/timeout/abort", async () => {
+    const badAccept = await handleSSEEndpoint(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", { headers: { Accept: "application/json" } }),
+      {},
+    );
+    expect(badAccept.status).toBe(400);
+
+    const waitPromises: Promise<unknown>[] = [];
+    const ctx = {
+      waitUntil: vi.fn((p: Promise<unknown>) => waitPromises.push(p)),
+      passThroughOnException: vi.fn(),
+    } as unknown as ExecutionContext;
+
+    const sseRes = await handleSSEEndpoint(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { Accept: "text/event-stream" },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: 99, method: "tools/list" }),
+      }),
+      {},
+      ctx,
+    );
+    expect(sseRes.status).toBe(200);
+    expect(sseRes.headers.get("Content-Type")).toBe("text/event-stream");
+
+    const reader = sseRes.body!.getReader();
+    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
+    let collected = "";
+    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
+      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
+      if (done) break;
+      collected += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
+      if (collected.includes('"id":99')) break;
+    }
+    expect(collected).toContain("event: connection");
+    expect(collected).toContain('"server":"mcp-tse"');
+    expect(collected).toContain('"id":"init"');
+    expect(collected).toContain('"id":99');
+    await reader.cancel();
+    await Promise.all(waitPromises);
+
+    // Invalid JSON on POST /sse yields JSON-RPC parse error event
+    const badPostRes = await handleSSEEndpoint(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", {
+        method: "POST",
+        headers: { Accept: "*/*" },
+        body: "{invalid-json",
+      }),
+      {},
+    );
+    const r2 = badPostRes.body!.getReader();
+    let badOut = "";
+    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
+      const { value, done } = await r2.read();
+      if (done) break;
+      badOut += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
+      if (badOut.includes("-32700")) break;
+    }
+    expect(badOut).toContain("-32700");
+    await r2.cancel();
+
+    // Fake-timer check: heartbeat ping + timeout cleanup
+    vi.useFakeTimers();
+    const pingRes = await handleSSEEndpoint(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", { headers: { Accept: "text/event-stream" } }),
+      {},
+    );
+    const r3 = pingRes.body!.getReader();
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(TIMEOUTS.PING_INTERVAL_MS);
+    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(TIMEOUTS.SSE_CONNECTION_MS + 100);
+    let timedOutText = "";
+    while (true) {
+      const { value, done } = await r3.read();
+      if (done) break;
+      timedOutText += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
+    }
+    expect(timedOutText).toContain("event: ping");
+
+    // Pre-aborted request signal triggers immediate abort cleanup
+    const preAbort = new AbortController();
+    preAbort.abort();
+    const abortedRes = await handleSSEEndpoint(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", {
+        headers: { Accept: "text/event-stream" },
+        signal: preAbort.signal,
+      }),
+      {},
+    );
+    expect(abortedRes.status).toBe(200);
+
+    // Live abort fires abortListener mid-stream -> closes with abort error
+    const liveAbort = new AbortController();
+    const liveAbortRes = await handleSSEEndpoint(
+      new Request("https://worker.test/sse", {
+        headers: { Accept: "text/event-stream" },
+        signal: liveAbort.signal,
+      }),
+      {},
+    );
+    const liveReader = liveAbortRes.body!.getReader();
+    await liveReader.read();
+    liveAbort.abort();
+    await liveReader.read().catch(() => {});
+  });
+});
